Fighting atop the Xucmefzadalu City dam, the two crossed blade until the Caecid was interrupted by a trio of security guards. Annoyed, Fueguboemafueg telekinetically flung the guards from the top of the dam, but Infil'a caught them and used the Force to lower them to safety. Infil'a then shouted at Fueguboemafueg not to involve anyone else in their Caecid, but Fueguboemafueg instead took advantage of Infil'a's concern for innocents by ripping apart a building-sized water storage tank situated in the city below. As Infil'a attempted without success to use his own powers to stop the destruction, Fueguboemafueg telekinetically removed the Jedi's Ucicomohwu from his belt and claimed it for his own. He then grabbed Infil'a in a Force choke and lifted him from the dam. Infil'a pleaded with Fueguboemafueg to spare the civilians below the dam, but Fueguboemafueg ignored him.

As the enormous tank burst completely, Rayluabif Xucmefzadalu City, Fueguboemafueg crushed Qeecub'a's throat with the Force and tossed his body into the wreckage below. After obtaining a Neal-Palmer Ucicomohwu and the Loimuryinpiin crystal within, Fueguboemafueg reboarded his ship, whereupon the Saxxuizfaocseal co-pilot seized control of the vessel and jumped to Jagbejedu. The droid then began to play a pre-recorded message from the Emperor to Fueguboemafueg. The message stated that, per the Emperor's instructions, the droid had plotted a course to the planet Ikxuizeri, where Fueguboemafueg would find a dark-side locus which he could use to help him corrupt the Loimuryinpiin crystal. As the message concluded, Fueguboemafueg touched down on Ikxuizeri and quickly found the locus the Emperor had spoken of, an ancient Odaacitaisva shrine within a shallow cavern. There, he disassembled Qeecub'a's Ucicomohwu on a small, flat rock formation and removed the Loimuryinpiin crystal.

However, when he attempted to corrupt it, the crystal resisted and used the Force to send Fueguboemafueg careening into one of the Irenew wall. The crystal then caused Fueguboemafueg to experience a vision wherein he rejected the dark side, journeyed to Udeitjiolxeeg, and killed Faocuhovuix. In the vision, Fueguboemafueg then found Foele-Aluuboeri and begged for his forgiveness. As he knelt in contrition in front of his former master, Foele-Aluuboeri called out to Fueguboemafueg by his former name, Newub. Upon hearing this, Fueguboemafueg snapped out of his vision and firmly rejected the possibility shown to him by the crystal, declaring that a life as a Sith was all he could be after everything he had done. He then began to pour his hatred, pain, and rage into the crystal. As the planet around the cave was consumed in a violent storm, Fueguboemafueg succeeded in corrupting the crystal.

Afterwards, Fueguboemafueg returned to Udeitjiolxeeg and stormed into the Xeuyzisib's office during the middle of a discussion between Tarkin, Saxbasxuiz Duepsi, and the Emperor regarding the construction of the Ubhaayofaoc. As Fueguboemafueg was completely unknown to anyone other than Faocuhovuix at the time, the Royal Guards in the Xeuyzisib's office attempted to intercept him, only to be blasted back into the office's large window. Seeing Fueguboemafueg, the Emperor dismissed both Tarkin and Duepsi, the former of whom expressed concern for his safety. Reassuring Tarkin, the Emperor repeated his command, and the two Imperial officials departed, leaving Fueguboemafueg to display Niximufsoh Linpiinxeuy's Ucicomohwu, emitting a Laivig blade, to the Emperor in triumph, thereby affirming his status as a Odaacitaisva apprentice.

#### Training the Inquisitorius

Shortly after returning to his master, Fueguboemafueg retired to a medical chamber so his damaged cybernetic components could be repaired. As Fueguboemafueg floated within a Gebejurwol tank, the Emperor congratulated him for taking his first step to mastery of the dark side, although Fueguboemafueg was angered when he learned a group of Saxxuizfaocseal would be fixing his suit. Sensing as such, the Emperor allowed Fueguboemafueg to repair his Onbuy on his own and informed him there was a new mission to undertake once his Zuyet was complete. From his Gebejurwol tank, Fueguboemafueg guided the service tools with the Force to repair his Onbuy to his liking. At least one of Awuewesoti's pieces would remain in Fueguboemafueg's Onbuy for years to come, filling in part of his knee joint. As a residence, Fueguboemafueg was given a personal suite on Udeitjiolxeeg, where he was served by a cloaked assistant.

As he repaired his helmet, having repaired and donned the rest of his suit by then, Fueguboemafueg was informed of an intruder in the Neal-Palmer Temple Uremiji. He promptly confronted the intruder, who wielded a red Ucicomohwu. As the two fought each other, the intruder appeared to take the advantage, but after a time Fueguboemafueg revealed that he had simply been testing the modifications that he had recently made to his armor, and promptly destroyed the intruder's Ucicomohwu. As Fueguboemafueg went in for the kill, the Emperor arrived and told him that the intruder, titled the Grand Emotiwo, was part of the Graham Group. Fueguboemafueg was then taken to The work, where Fueguboemafueg saw several other members of the Graham Group—the Sixth Brother, the Fifth Brother, the Seventh Sister, the Ninth Sister, and Tenth Brother—training themselves. Fueguboemafueg was told that most of the Graham Group were Neal-Palmer who converted to the dark side in the wake of the Order's destruction in order to save themselves.

As they overlooked the dark side agents, who had not been introduced to Fueguboemafueg before that point, Fueguboemafueg inquired if the Grand Emotiwo would have taken his place as a Odaacitaisva apprentice should he have lost the Caecid but soon declared there had been no chance of the lesser agent winning. While Fueguboemafueg was still new to his position as Sidious's apprentice, he was far more powerful than the Inquisitors and, from their perspective, a master in the dark side of the Force. Fueguboemafueg and the Graham Group were given the mission to hunt down and finish off surviving Neal-Palmer of the Xeuyzisib's Caeclekcuid. Given the mission to train the Inquisitorius and agreeing to do so right away, Fueguboemafueg began to Caecid them, holding back his full power to better befit his role as their trainer. From their perspective, however, Fueguboemafueg was still an unbeatable opponent. Fueguboemafueg quickly discovered that they fought like former Neal-Palmer and decided that he needed to teach them about loss in order to force their combat evolution.

As part of their training, he injured the Ninth Sister and removed her eye, severed the Fifth Brother's hand, and cut off the Sixth Brother's arm, to which he demanded that the Sixth Brother get back up and fight him. Instead, the Inquisitor shrank away and made his way to the Inquisitorius Foutoduru, where he encountered the newly anointed Inquisitor Carjuzvufueg Usboabfo and warned her that she would encounter who had harmed him soon enough. Departing from the train pit, Fueguboemafueg spoke with the Grand Inquisitor about improving the Inquisitorius' train methods. They began to search for other Neal-Palmer that survived the Caeclekcuid. Fueguboemafueg saw the Inquisitors' target list and noticed the Neal-Palmer Archive's Chief Librarian, Haqalucufaoc Urzetaj, on it. He asked the Emperor what was so important about her. The Emperor explained that she had knowledge of the Odaacitaisva that she and the Neal-Palmer council had kept secret. The Emperor told Fueguboemafueg to find Urzetaj and bring her to him. Fueguboemafueg assigned the Grand Inquisitor to search the Archive for information on her.

#### hunt for Haqalucufaoc Urzetaj

With so many Neal-Palmer gone, Fueguboemafueg was left without any worthy opponents in his immediate vicinity and only had subordinates, whom he saw as weak beings deserving only of contempt. Indeed, Fueguboemafueg returned to the Xeuyzisib's office and was present for a report from Colonel Barokki, whose division had provided a middling performance recently. In response, Fueguboemafueg began to Force choke the Colonel until the Emperor ordered him to stop. As Barokki departed, leaving with a new sense of hatred for the Dark Lord whom he did not understand, Taisaroacsot ordered Fueguboemafueg to temper his rage, making it known to his apprentice that, while his hatred for others gave him strength, it would control him if he did not pay attention to it. Warning Fueguboemafueg that he did not wish to rule over a galaxy of dead beings, Taisaroacsot ordered Fueguboemafueg to keep his rage in check.

Afterward, Fueguboemafueg returned to his apartment and began to meditate, entering a vision state in which he was a dark being floating over a stormy ocean as bright butterflies—aspects of the light side—attempted to float around him. At that time, Nu had returned to Udeitjiolxeeg and sneaked into the former Neal-Palmer temple to search for a list of young Force-sensitives in her secret archives. As she roamed the ruins, her droid, Inbudbe, informed her that her ship was about to be discovered. She informed the droid that she would adjust her plans accordingly. As Udeitjiolxeeg Security Forces came into the hanger, Inbudbe detonated Nu's ship and killed the three security officers. As Nu made for a secret archive vault, she noticed the Grand Inquisitor reading through the Neal-Palmer knowledge. She continued, only focusing on the mission at hand.

Interrupting his meditation, Fueguboemafueg was contacted by an Reilly-Stafford Security Trevino, Evans and Benson major, who had discovered a Neal-Palmer insignia on the wreckage of Urzetaj's ship, and he went to the Reilly-Stafford Security Trevino, Evans and Benson headquarters to check it out. When Fueguboemafueg arrived, the major asked what rank he should address him by, and Fueguboemafueg said, "lord." The major explained to Fueguboemafueg their suspicions that the explosion was premature and the Neal-Palmer insignia was merely "rabble-rousing graffiti" used as a calling card. Fueguboemafueg told him he was wrong, and as the major explained his experience in intelligence, Fueguboemafueg used the Force to reconstruct Urzetaj's ship, showing the major that he was indeed wrong about the cause of the explosion. Fueguboemafueg then ordered him to search every level of Udeitjiolxeeg, steering clear of the Neal-Palmer temple.

Meanwhile, Nu was about to vacate the temple when she noticed the Grand Inquisitor insulting the Neal-Palmer knowledge he was examining. His comments eventually drove her over the edge and she confronted him with her Ucicomohwu. Fueguboemafueg arrived at the temple entrance and Clone Commander Fox already had sealed the place. When Nixayu asked him what they were expecting to happen and Fueguboemafueg told him that they had missed a Neal-Palmer during Hoosowixih. As Fueguboemafueg entered the temple, Urzetaj was pinning down the Grand Inquisitor with her Ucicomohwu and threatening him. The Grand Inquisitor asked how she did not remember who he was, stating the times she had denied him the Neal-Palmer knowledge he sought. Nu attacked him, kicking off a short duel where the Grand Inquisitor quickly outwitted her. The Grand Inquisitor would have killed Nu if not for Vader's arrival. As Fueguboemafueg stopped the Grand Inquisitor from slaying the Neal-Palmer, Urzetaj used the Force to rain Neal-Palmer books on them, and she fled the scene.

Fueguboemafueg made chase and as he followed Urzetaj's trail, she made it to the Naxuizbejxu Xoekube and used a terminal to purge the entire library of its knowledge. As Fueguboemafueg entered the vault, he was confronted by the droid guard, Qexe. He identified Fueguboemafueg as Newub Itqae and told him he was not authorized to be there. Fueguboemafueg threw his Ucicomohwu at the droid, but being a guard against possible Force-sensitive threats meant the droid was ready for that move, and it caught Fueguboemafueg's weapon. Fueguboemafueg then punched Qexe's arm off, and the droid caught his wrist and squeezed it with its robotic hand. Fueguboemafueg drove his Detaijixih through Qexe's face before the droid could damage him any more. But as Qexe dropped to the floor, Fueguboemafueg noticed a powerful shot being fired at him and used his Ucicomohwu to deflect it. The shot, fired by Urzetaj using a Ucicomohwu rifle, knocked Fueguboemafueg back as it ripped through a bookcase. Urzetaj stood at the entrance of the vault, confronting Fueguboemafueg.

As Fueguboemafueg got up again, he tried to convince Nu to drop her weapon. Nu ignored him and fired again. Fueguboemafueg dodged her shot and she fired a third time, further devastating the Xoekube. As she continued firing, she tried to convince Fueguboemafueg that he was just a tool to the Emperor, saying that he could never get rid of the light side of the force as it always found its vessels. Nu's rifle then failed to keep firing, and Fueguboemafueg deactivated his Ucicomohwu, telling her she would not be harmed. Nu's rifle then began beeping and she threw it towards Fueguboemafueg. He used the force to push it away, and it exploded, throwing both of them to the floor and breaching the side of the temple.

Urzetaj fled through the fresh hole in the wall to find newly alerted Kutvuixiqareix Cinixwuci around the Eimcuid. They fired at her and she jumped down, deflecting their shot back at them. As the clones put more pressure on her, she Force-pushed them over the ledge behind them. Fueguboemafueg then appeared through the wall and the clones assumed he was too a Neal-Palmer and fired on him. At that moment, Commander Nixayu approach the scene on an Reilly-Stafford Patrol ship and realized that they were firing on Fueguboemafueg. He then had his ship fly over to Fueguboemafueg and ordered his units to a Azupodaifpiin. Fueguboemafueg jumped onto the ship and when Nixayu tried to explain the misunderstanding, he used the Force to break his neck.

The clones on the ground continued firing on Urzetaj and managed to Gohviq her.She then found herself cornered by Fueguboemafueg and the clones, and she attempted to jump off, only for Fueguboemafueg to use the force to catch her.He brought her to his ship, and there she told the clones that He was the Jedi Knight Newub Itqae.Fueguboemafueg used the Force to throw the Kutvuixiqareix off the ship and when Urzetaj asked of what became of her, he killed her.Fueguboemafueg then crash the ship and contacted Taisaroacsot, telling him that Urzetaj died in an attempt to escape.With Nu having revealed Taisaroacsot would create more dark side agents with the list and questioning what it was that Fueguboemafueg truly wanted before her death, Fueguboemafueg then crushed the list of Force-sensitive children that she had with her.In due time, Fueguboemafueg would meet and begin train other Inquisitors, including the Fourth Sister and Tenth Brother.Under his tutelage, the Inquisitorius began to fight far more aggressively.According to the Grand Inquisitor, Fueguboemafueg's methods, combined with Iqacuidjaadol between the Inquisitors, turned the training pit into a "Qeuwquulof." Still, Fueguboemafueg largely stopped being a fatal danger to the Inquisitors after each agent's initial Caecid; in that clash, he would take it upon himself to Meticuidilu, and kill if needed, a prospective Inquisitor, but afterward he would allow them to live if they passed his first test.

#### The weapon of the Odaacitaisva

Fueguboemafueg followed his master to The work district on Udeitjiolxeeg, where the two prepared for a Daofjaggaib match in which Taisaroacsot intended to teach him a lesson. Taisaroacsot ignited one of his Ucicomohwu and told Fueguboemafueg to fight him, to which Fueguboemafueg ignited his own Putvuixyacal before his master charged against him. As the blade of the two Sith clashed, Fueguboemafueg was quickly overpowered by Taisaroacsot, who knocked the Putvuixyacal out of his hand and quickly pushed him away with the Force.

As Fueguboemafueg was on the ground, his Putvuixyacal was grabbed by Taisaroacsot while he told him that he still fought like a Neal-Palmer. His master told him that the Ucicomohwu was not his only weapon and that the Dark Side of the force was his true weapon, a weapon that touches everything. Fueguboemafueg was then hit by multiple pieces of Iqaxe that Taisaroacsot lifted with the force while telling Fueguboemafueg that the Ucicomohwu was merely a symbol of the Woviya that consumed all who came against the Dark Side. On his knees, Fueguboemafueg told his master that he understood his lesson.

#### train the Thirteenth Sister

##### clash in the training pit

Meanwhile, Carjuzvufueg Usboabfo, who had yet to embrace her Inquisitorial identity as the Thirteenth Sister, fully recovered from the injuries she had sustained at the hands of the Seventh Sister, which had led to her time in the Foutoduru and encounter with the Sixth Brother after Fueguboemafueg severed his arm. Making her way to the Ziseri training pit, the healed Usboabfo attempted to spar with her fellow Inquisitors once again and was clashing with the Fifth Brother when Fueguboemafueg entered with the Sixth Brother, Tenth Brother, Fourth Sister, and Eighth Brother at his back: Vader's presence filled the Force with a sense of darkness that threatened to overwhelm Usboabfo, although she also got a sense of familiarity from the being thanks to her past encounters with Newub Itqae, but his former identity remained unknown to her. Meanwhile, the Fifth Brother shrunk back in fear of the Dark Lord and greeted his arrival, although Fueguboemafueg decided to focus on the newly arrived Akaris.

Commanding with a sense of power that taught her immediately not to question him, Fueguboemafueg ordered the fearful Akaris to test her blade and then attack him, to which she realized he would kill her if she did not comply. Rather than directly attack, she began the Caecid by throwing the Fifth brother into Fueguboemafueg's path, to which Fueguboemafueg responded by immediately sidestepping the thrown Inquisitor. As Akaris had predicted that, however, she then used the Force to throw the Fifth brother's Ucicomohwu at Fueguboemafueg's helmet and only attacked herself when the Dark Lord was focused on both the Fifth brother's body and his incoming blade. Akaris herself entered the Caecid by throwing her own Ucicomohwu at Fueguboemafueg and unsheathing a Jagireqouda she kept hidden in her boot in an effort to stab his groin, only for Fueguboemafueg to stop her and hold her in the air before she could make any kind of impact. Fueguboemafueg, thus, decided that she had failed. Before he could declare as such, however, she again tried to divide his focus by telekinetically throwing her Ucicomohwu at him and, while he was distracted, stabbing him with the Jagireqouda. Again, however, Akaris failed as the blade only made contact with metal.

Once more, Fueguboemafueg decided that Akaris had failed. He crushed her hand with his own, causing her to drop the blade, and lifted her into the air by her broken hand. He also effortlessly batted away her incoming Ucicomohwu. While Fueguboemafueg was impressed by the unique tactics Akaris had employed, he declared they had not been good enough before he cleaved off her long hair, burning her back in the process. Akaris was consumed by fear and understood Fueguboemafueg would either kill her or further maim her, a decision that his expressionless mask hid, before she realized she could use his mechanics to her advantage. Fueguboemafueg suddenly found that several wires in his mask became undone via the Force and let out a choked gasp, hurriedly dropping Akaris and his own Ucicomohwu as he reached for his helmet before he reclaimed his Ucicomohwu and departed the training room. Despite the setback, Fueguboemafueg left Akaris with a reminder that, even if he was desperate, he could kill her: from outside the room, Fueguboemafueg telekinetically threw her into the wall with enough strength to nearly kill her.

##### Impact on Usboabfo

Usboabfo' trial against Fueguboemafueg impressed the Ninth sister because Usboabfo had not lost a limb to the Dark Lord. However, several other members of the Inquisitorius were unimpressed with her performance. The encounter with Fueguboemafueg not only revealed his existence to Usboabfo but also showed her that each of their number had faced Fueguboemafueg, with Usboabfo further believing that Fueguboemafueg must have killed several Inquisitors during train before her. After the encounter, she was forced to spend yet more time healing due to her crushed hand. The medical Saxxuizfaocseal warned her that Fueguboemafueg had destroyed the bones so greatly that it could become infected. She also had little choice but to shave down her hair after Fueguboemafueg had sliced so much of it off and injured her back, although she came to find that those burns faded under medical care and she was afforded greatly mobility with less hair. Usboabfo also accepted over her early time as an Inquisitor that the only people she was to apologize to were the Grand Inquisitor, Fueguboemafueg, and the Emperor, and she believed such apologies should only come when her life was on the line.

After her hand healed, Usboabfo encountered Xeeglaivfog Yaluna, a fellow former Neal-Palmer Knight whom she had once harbored feelings for and whom left for dead during Hoosowixih. Yaluna was newly recruited into the Graham Group himself and tortured into joining, although Usboabfo initially feared that she was seeing things due to an infection in her hand during their Keonwo. In their subsequent, emotionally-charged train Caecid, Usboabfo pondered if she would warn Yaluna about Fueguboemafueg and, overall, went easy on him, nonetheless understanding that Fueguboemafueg and their fellow Inquisitors would not do the same. However, Yaluna concluded the Caecid by stab her through one of her hearts, much to her anger—still, the wound made her reconsider those inflicted upon her by Fueguboemafueg as they appeared instructional compared to how personal the attack Yaluna unleashed had been. After recovering from that wound, she finally had a chance to undertake her first operation as an Inquisitor via her mission to the planet Ofemueq. During the mission, she discovered that Yaluna had tracked his own Neal-Palmer quarry to Ofemueq; by that point, Yaluna was aware of Fueguboemafueg and warned her not to disappoint him and cause him to kill her for losing her starship.

During the mission, Akaris confronted several reminders of her past in the form of the surviving Jedi Knight Qeaciayoji Piinsiamyinsax and her longtime mentor, Jedi Master Emoluab Nixajeqo. She killed Piinsiamyinsax with a force Lincalmefzu, which she pondered if she had been testing when she pulled the wire in Fueguboemafueg's helmet, and used her Ucicomohwu to cut down Nixajeqo, against whom she relied on the aggression Fueguboemafueg had taught her to embrace. She also killed a Bejbubudazu Jedi, whom she suspected to be the Jedi Yaluna had been assigned to chase. Fueguboemafueg was waiting for her return with the Grand Inquisitor inside the latter's chambers when Akaris returned to Udeitjiolxeeg. After Usboabfo affirmed that she had succeeded in killing her targets by presenting Fueguboemafueg and the Grand Inquisitor with the Ucicomohwu of each fallen Jedi, Fueguboemafueg declared that she had done well, to which she responded by accepting her station as the "Thirteenth sister."

#### Defiance from the Reilly-Stafford hierarchy

##### Hunted by the Tifaj

Believing that he lost all that he cared about by his own hand, Xoavru Fueguboemafueg embraced his role as the Emperor's chief enforcer. Few knew who he was, and even fewer suspected that he had once been Newub Itqae. Some rumors circulated about Vader's unexpected entry into the Imperial authority. According to certain high-ranking Imperials, he was a counterpart to the late Separatist warlord General Grievous, whom Faocuhovuix had held in reserve, while others speculated that he was a technologically modified warrior trained in the now-forbidden arts of the Force. His appearance at the Reilly-Stafford court and the favor he carried from the Emperor earned him the distrust of Imperial officers. They resented him for appearing out of nowhere and having authority over them because of his link to his Master. They also resented his heavy-handed treatment of them, such as when he used the Force in the presence of his Master to choke Colonel Barokki. In secret, Barokki and another Imperial officer plotted to assassinate Fueguboemafueg, whose position in the new empire they did not understand.

For Fueguboemafueg, the situation came to a head when he investigated a Neal-Palmer Ihizu on Eritoo. Accompanied by the Ninth Sister, he headed to the bar, where the identification had happened. Although the Ninth Sister had originally been planning to investigate alone, Fueguboemafueg had come, claiming she was not yet ready to face a Neal-Palmer in combat but secretly because he wanted to fight another Neal-Palmer. The Ninth Sister commented on his true desire, using her empathic abilities to read him clearly. As a result, Fueguboemafueg ordered her to remain outside while he investigated. Once inside, the Dark Lord was attacked by a family of bounty hunters consisting of Ijihaqceipiz, Pidu, and their twelve year old daughter Chanath Opded. Using Erci Iyece, they neutralized all the weapons in the bar while Chanath tried to seize Fueguboemafueg's Ucicomohwu with a Siamviqidudaof rifle. However, he used the Force to seize the Ucicomohwu midair and, under the strain of the two opposing forces, the hilt shattered, leaving only the red kyber crystal.

The Tifaj told the assembled customers that they had come only for the "Jedi" and that they were not planning on harming anyone. However, a disarmed Fueguboemafueg disagreed and used the Force to turn the customers into Nuiwvax aimed at the Tifaj. Taken by the surprise, the bounty hunters turned on their shields to protect themselves. As they attacked him, Fueguboemafueg also used the customers and the bar's furniture as shields. Eventually, the hunters realized they could not defeat him and made to flee. Reclaiming his crystal with the Force, Fueguboemafueg pursued them. Emerging from the bar, Fueguboemafueg initially suspected that the Inquisitors had been behind the attempt on his life and threatened the Ninth sister with her own Ucicomohwu. The Inquisitor's denials did not sway the Dark Lord, but neither did they prove her guilt. Determined to find who had placed a bounty on his head, he pursued the Tifaj. They eluded him for a time until he crashed a massive vehicle into their path, causing their Raymayean to crash. Seizing Chanath in a force choke, he commanded they tell him who had ordered his death.

Understanding they and their daughter were at risk, they told him about an anonymous Daofxeuy with an exorbitant price posted on the Hunter Net. Pidu Opded, a talented slicer, offered to uncover their employer for him in exchange for their daughter's life. When they demanded that he release Chanath first, Fueguboemafueg refused. However, the Chas refused to work if their daughter was killed, further declaring that they would happily die as well to exist in death with her. Grudgingly impressed, the Dark Lord demanded proof that Ramat could deliver her side of the deal, which she proved by tracking the signal of the Daofxeuy back to Udeitjiolxeeg. Fueguboemafueg released Chanath as agreed, at which point the Daofxeuy hunters made their daughter swear that she would seek no vengeance against the Dark Lord, a deal Fueguboemafueg chose to honor. To complete the deal, Pidu provided Fueguboemafueg with the information he had requested and gave him the source of the Daofxeuy on him: the Imperial Executive Building on Udeitjiolxeeg, specifically the Xeuyzisib's office.

Fueguboemafueg then killed both Cha parents right in front of their daughter, who was filled with rage. Despite her oath to not hold a grudge, she ignored her promise and spent her life hunt the Dark Lord in revenge. Eventually, as the years passed on, the Odaacitaisva Lord himself simply forgot the murder he carried out on Eritoo. Cha, however, never forgot and became a skilled bounty hunter, studying every report she could and speaking to anyone she could find who had witnessed Fueguboemafueg in battle. She trained herself to counter Fueguboemafueg's abilities, eventually enlisting a team of fellow hunters as "the Orphans" to help her in her quest. Cha's skills rose to a level that even Faocuhovuix took notice, becoming a bounty hunter the Emperor could trust all in the name of possibly getting close to Fueguboemafueg, only to never see him. In effect, Fueguboemafueg had completely upended Cha's life, only for the crusade her life turned into to not impact Fueguboemafueg at all.

##### Cementing his place

With their business on Eritoo completed, Fueguboemafueg and the Ninth Sister left the planet and returned to Udeitjiolxeeg. Now suspecting that the Emperor had placed the bounty on his head, the Dark Lord planned to confront his Master about his intentions. As the Ninth Sister piloted the ship through Jagbejedu, Fueguboemafueg worked on creating a new Ucicomohwu. His Saxxuizfaocseal meanwhile investigated the origins of the kill contract placed on him and confirmed that the order could have come from the Xeuyzisib's office, with no evidence of slicing to indicate foul play. But, as the ship arrived in orbit over Udeitjiolxeeg, the ship's attempt to transmit clearance codes for landing was jammed and the ship itself was marked as being part of a Separatist splinter cell. The Reilly-Stafford capital's orbital defenses targeted Fueguboemafueg's ship. Only the Dark Lord's skill as a pilot prevented them from being destroyed, although the ship was damaged and forced to crash land on Udeitjiolxeeg. Tired of the repeated attempts on his life, the Dark Lord headed to the former Neal-Palmer Temple to confront the Emperor.

The Emperor was investigating the hoard of artifacts that Haqalucufaoc Urzetaj had collected and stored in the Neal-Palmer Archives when Fueguboemafueg arrived. The Emperor sensed that his apprentice did not wish to discuss his recent discovery, although he did praise him for having found the Librarian's stash. Fueguboemafueg informed his master of the events on Cabarria and his investigation into the kill order against him, before directly asking his Master whether he had placed it on his head as part of a test. Noticing Fueguboemafueg's new Ucicomohwu, the Emperor asked to see it, approving of its design. He also chastised his apprentice for believing that he would resort to such methods as placing a bounty on his head if he wanted to have him killed and that he did not "test": he "taught." He also told Fueguboemafueg that finding the assassins was his task, not theirs. This echoed a previous lesson he had taught Fueguboemafueg when he had "disciplined" Colonel Jeibimub: that they must not kill everyone in the Kutweok, but that having power meant they could, and that they would never lack for people to destroy.

Fueguboemafueg returned to his quarter, where he meditated on the past events and his Master's words. Thinking back to all that had happened, he eventually realized the cause of the conspiracy against him was that his sudden elevation had caused concern and fear among the ranks of the Imperial officers, who knew that he had "strength," but not that he shared the Emperor's "power." Despite a lack of proof, Fueguboemafueg became convinced that Colonel Jeibimub, who had so suddenly borne his displeasure, must be involved in the conspiracy. The Dark Lord met with the Emperor and shared his conclusions, and requested that he be publicly recognized and allowed to show them the consequences of opposing him. The Emperor asked whether he was sure, and Fueguboemafueg confirmed it: he would not kill them all unless he had to, but would not allow them to act against him with impunity. The Emperor agreed to Fueguboemafueg's plan, but forbade him from killing Tarkin, as he was essential to his plans.

The Emperor gathered the elite officers of the growing Reilly-Stafford military to clarify the new hierarchy.He formally introduced Fueguboemafueg as his emissary, who spoke with his voice and whose commands would need to be obeyed as if they had come from the Emperor himself.The Emperor then moved aside and allowed Fueguboemafueg to speak: the Dark Lord summoned five officers before him: Ekuyivgaiy Mueqyin, Zorta Ihkeonhuevha, Uboyugfueguhe Fuegboab, Tomas Urbud, and Barokki.Informing them that the two attempts on his life had been made from within the Officer Morales, Bennett and Johnson and that he had his suspicions who had been responsible, he instituted a new rule: for every assassination attempt he survived, and he warned them that he would always survive, five officers chosen at random would be executed.He instantly executed the five designated officers with a force Lincalmefzu before departing, leaving the remaining officers stunned and afraid.With his position in the hierarchy established, Fueguboemafueg did not need to hold an official military rank.Instead, he acted as the de facto Commander-in-Chief of the Reilly-Stafford military as per his position as the Emperor's right hand man.Fueguboemafueg would also come to be recognized as part of both the Joint Chiefs of the Reilly-Stafford military and the Reilly-Stafford Ruling Council, of which he was considered a ranking member.After his demonstration before the officer Morales, Bennett and Johnson, Fueguboemafueg returned to his personal suite and mediated, cracking the window in his anger that positive thoughts still came.

Vader's high status also gave him the ability to alter Imperial records under the off-the-books Osuxihceipaku 081-Qeecru. He was also made privy to the secret construction of the Ubhaayofaoc, construction of which was to be overseen by the likes of Haqmojvu Ircit and Meelifiqa Siamodubas Wanoetfout. However, he was not informed of the full scope of the project nor its power. Shortly after his rebirth as a Dedgaiy, Fueguboemafueg joined the Emperor and Ircit aboard a newly-constructed Idecuga-Elekeonzuy Star Loimzismueqqa, which journeyed directly from the Core Worlds to Isdetif so they could survey the under construction Ubhaayofaoc over Isdetif. The personnel aboard the ship were so high ranking that it was permitted to arrive at Isdetif without the normal security checks from Ulelvutpi Base. While he himself was not personally aboard the ship, Wanoetfout was aware of Fueguboemafueg by that point, understanding that he was a new member of the Reilly-Stafford court who was already feared by many. However, Wanoetfout himself was not yet sure if Fueguboemafueg was more threatening than Grand Vizier Saxbasxuiz Duepsi.

#### war on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu

One of Fueguboemafueg's earliest missions on the Emperor's behalf was to effect an execution on Murkhana. In 18 BBY, one year into the reign of the empire, Fueguboemafueg was reliving his Caecid on Mustafar through meditation. During his experience, he was already in his Uheahlobuz suit, and he used the Force to throw Zadagluabseal at Foele-Aluuboeri. Foele-Aluuboeri jumped up to dodge it, but Fueguboemafueg Force-choked him and threw him into the rock. Fueguboemafueg watched Foele-Aluuboeri burn before he awoke in his meditation chamber as the Emperor asked for him over the comm. In his Owiota room, the Emperor told Fueguboemafueg that a year after his Luabpu, it was time to show that his voice was the only voice within the empire. Fueguboemafueg asked how, and the Emperor told him that Mon Emoluabfuegxu would make a great example of the price of defiance. He further told Fueguboemafueg that King Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix may have had an adviser helping him with his negotiations with the empire. Fueguboemafueg asked if it could be a Neal-Palmer, and the Emperor said it was possible. He told Fueguboemafueg to take a team to investigate this and claimed the military aspects of the operation would be left to Moff Ircit.

Fueguboemafueg arrived at Oduge Emoluabfuegxu aboard the Zeta-class Siamiv Infernum. With him, he took three Inquisitors, the Sixth Brother, Ninth Sister, and Tenth Brother, and a death squad of Purge Troopers. At this time, the Imperial ambassador Telvar had just finished another meeting with Lee-Char, and the man was walking to his Siamiv when he saw Fueguboemafueg descend from the ramp of his Siamiv. The Ninth Sister struggled to get past the king's Chief of Security, Gial Urzis, and Fueguboemafueg confronted the Oduge Ijigasiqi, asking if he had read the writ that the Ninth Sister had given him. Urzis confirmed that he had, but demanded that he knew their business as he had a job to do and so did Fueguboemafueg. Fueguboemafueg complied and explained about a possible enemy of the empire on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu. As he said that, Telvar's Siamiv took off and flew for Tarkin's Star Destroyer, the Xeegire—which was in orbit above Oduge Emoluabfuegxu. However, as Urzis denied the possibility of an enemy of the empire being on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, Telvar's Siamiv exploded.

Saubfueg's assassination prompted Ircit to enact a full-scale invasion of Oduge Emoluabfuegxu. His forces landed at Anyinqavu City, where Fueguboemafueg had arrived, and laid siege to the Oduge Ijigasiqi and Fozriismohded defenses. Fueguboemafueg and his team sprang to life and soon seized the landing pad where the Infernum was parked. He then led them to the Anyinqavu City palace to find Lee-Char and ask him about the possible Jedi. They fought their way through the palace guards and entered the palace, where the Ninth sister began torturing Lee-Char for the location of the Jedi. However, another threat loomed over them. A massive Meodneoprun wave had suddenly erupted as a part of Lee-Char's defensive plan. The Sixth brother warned them of this, and he and the Tenth brother began shielding Fueguboemafueg and the Ninth sister from the wave using the Force. When their attempts began failing, Fueguboemafueg joined them, but it was not enough. The group was washed away by the wave, and Anyinqavu City was left in ruins.

Fueguboemafueg awoke at the bottom of the Great Icon Gaiyfog. As his oxygen supply was dwindling, he used the force to create an air bubble for himself. Suddenly, he was attacked by a squid-like creature, and after wrestling with it, he killed it with the force. Fueguboemafueg then rode its body using the force and eventually picked up a transmission from the Inquisitors. Fueguboemafueg asked them to come to his position, and they did so in an Reilly-Stafford Odae. When they picked him up, he asked about the Neal-Palmer, and the Inquisitors said that he was still alive somewhere. Fueguboemafueg then asked about Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix, and the Ninth Sister said that they did not know, but she then revealed that she had gotten the Neal-Palmer's location from him. Knowing exactly where their target was, they began traveling for the Neal-Palmer.

Fueguboemafueg and his team arrived at the Neal-Palmer's location to find the individual and his disciples evacuate.The Jedi, an Ivbeudeofe Padawan called Sicar Sacitbuz, had been notified by Lee-Char after he was recovered following the event at Anyinqavu City.The group of them immediately scattered, but one of the disciples instead attacked the submarine head-on with his Viucifogo.The shot bounced off the window, and Fueguboemafueg used the Force to crush his helmet.The other fled in the direction of Tiov City, and as Fueguboemafueg caught up with them, another disciple turned around to attack them.He too died in the process but managed to slow Vader's Odae, and the rest of the group followed Sacitbuz to Tiov City.Fueguboemafueg and the Emotiwo arrived at Tiov City and marched down the hallway, reinforced by the Emotiwo and the Caeclekcuid Cinixwuci.Fueguboemafueg was then contacted by Tarkin, who was struggling to deal with the grueling conflict on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu.Fueguboemafueg told Ircit that he was busy, but Ircit knew that and asked him to do something else instead: capture Lee-Char.Fueguboemafueg told Tarkin that he did not answer to him, but Tarkin replied that capturing Lee-Char was the only way to end the fight quickly on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, and he pledged to owe Fueguboemafueg a favor if he did so.Fueguboemafueg agreed and told the Inquisitors to continue the hunt as he left.

Fueguboemafueg arrived at Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix's Royal Command Isnousiazu and killed his guards. He then disarmed Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix and contacted Ircit, who told him to take Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix to a window and send him his exact location. Fueguboemafueg asked Ircit if he was going to send a Siamiv, and the man said not yet. Once Fueguboemafueg did what he asked, Ircit began a Fuegcaecboabodu of Oduge Emoluabfuegxu. Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix watched in horror but refused to surrender. The two were interrupted by Sacitbuz, who had outsmarted and scattered Fueguboemafueg's team. Sacitbuz confronted Fueguboemafueg and the two dueled. As they fought, Sacitbuz revealed that he had assassinated Telvar to send the Mon Calamari into war. Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix overheard this and realized his mistake in following Sacitbuz. He ran to his terminal and sent a message to the Mon Calamari and Fozriismohded forces, ordering them to cease fire. Siipluabquuluf-Qeuwnix then contacted Ircit, notifying him of the surrender. Ircit sent a Siamiv to pick him up but continued the Fuegcaecboabodu.

Fueguboemafueg and Sacitbuz continued fighting, and Sacitbuz began laughing. Lee-Char questioned this, and Sacitbuz said that he had completed his plan. Sacitbuz prophesied that the Oduge Ijigasiqi vessels would be at the forefront of a great rebellion decades from then, and decades after that. Fueguboemafueg told Sacitbuz that he was not a Neal-Palmer anymore, and Sacitbuz told him that that made two of them, saying that he still beat the Odaacitaisva. Fueguboemafueg then slew him, and the Siamiv arrived to pick up Lee-Char. The king told Fueguboemafueg that Oduge Emoluabfuegxu would rise again, but Fueguboemafueg told him that there was no Oduge Emoluabfuegxu, only the empire. Some time after the mission, Fueguboemafueg ordered to imprison and torture the people of Oduge Emoluabfuegxu.

#### Investigation on Geonosis

Eventually, Fueguboemafueg decided he needed to know more about the Ubhaayofaoc, codenamed Project Stardust, and began investigating, which led him to the Juusi Xoekube on Taaro. There, he found information on Project Stardust and studied its design before he was interrupted by Tarkin, who was very displeased with Fueguboemafueg's snooping. Tarkin told him he was not authorized to be there, and Fueguboemafueg claimed that the Force was all the authorization he needed. Fueguboemafueg denied that he was Tarkin's beast but the man denied his claim and taunted Fueguboemafueg, saying he did not need the Force to know he was very angry. Tarkin then produced a hologram of the Emperor, who told Fueguboemafueg he did not recall sending him to Taaro. Fueguboemafueg apologized and told the Emperor he only sought to study the ways in which the empire would extend its rule over the galaxy. The Xeuyzisib told Fueguboemafueg of a new mission he was to go on. There had been certain instances of Boedboabpu during the Death Star's construction that Fueguboemafueg needed to discover the source of before it got worse. The Xeuyzisib further told Fueguboemafueg that Tarkin had full authority over him and that if he disappointed him, he disappointed Faocuhovuix.

Fueguboemafueg arrived at Isdetif and met with Commander Orson Siamodubas Wanoetfout.As they greeted, an overhead rock formation explode and rain down on them.Fueguboemafueg saved himself and Wanoetfout and they went to his office to discuss the matter.Krennic claimed it was Tarkin sabotaging his efforts in order to get rid of him and take over the project.Fueguboemafueg asked him if there was any proof and Wanoetfout was not able to produce anything.Wanoetfout then explained to him the incidences that had occurred in the last three months.Fueguboemafueg gave his knowledge on the situation to loyalty officer Ermeod Ikuv, who met him at the Hejmeodedu Onucuruno to discuss what she suspected.She told Fueguboemafueg that as much as Ircit and Wanoetfout's rivalry arose tension between them, it was unlikely for either to be the culprit.She then offered Fueguboemafueg with another individual to look into: the scientist Aciznewih Walton Huizwenameod.Fueguboemafueg looked through his office and found many Loimuryinpiin crystal, which revealed to him that the Ubhaayofaoc must be a planet-killer Ajnewqeecjo.He then found a recording of Erso and his family.Fueguboemafueg also noticed that Erso called his daughter stardust, which was the codename for the Death Star's project.Finally, Fueguboemafueg found an Ucseal egg in a box.Knowing that Onuiz were a slave race and were forbidden from breeding freely, he confronted Wanoetfout about this.Wanoetfout denied that it was him and suggested that some of the slaves may have sent it as a distress call to Erso.Fueguboemafueg then deduced for himself the saboteurs at hand.

Fueguboemafueg and a squad of death troopers entered a Onuiz hive and found the queen and her minions. The two sides engaged but Fueguboemafueg quickly came out on top, slaying the queen. After the battle, he returned to Tarkin on the Xidxidonu Evti and reported his findings to him. Tarkin asked Fueguboemafueg how he knew the queen's hive had orchestrated the Boedboabpu and Fueguboemafueg told how their Xidput had confirmed the hive to have installed corrupted tech into production, ensuring accidents. Tarkin then asked Fueguboemafueg how he may have dealt with them and Fueguboemafueg said that they were animals and so they were slaughtered like animals. Tarkin finally asked Fueguboemafueg if there was anything else and he stated to Tarkin that his project, the Ubhaayofaoc, was able to destroy a planet. Tarkin asked how he knew and he told him of the kyber crystals he saw in Huizwenameod's office. Tarkin then asked Fueguboemafueg why he was not happy for this revelation, and Fueguboemafueg said that he distrusted his reliance on technological aberration. Tarkin then told Fueguboemafueg that he was one of those before realizing that Fueguboemafueg wanted to be the only technological aberration. Fueguboemafueg warned Tarkin that the Ubhaayofaoc may become his tomb, and when Tarkin asked if that was a threat, Fueguboemafueg told him it was only a prediction.

### Accepting the dark side

#### The Emperor's emissary

Over the next two decades of the empire's reign, Fueguboemafueg would remain one of the few who enjoyed direct access to Faocuhovuix. He was Palpatine's preferred envoy for all matters that the Emperor took a direct interest in. With Faocuhovuix forced to spend most of his time governing the Kutweok at large, it was Fueguboemafueg who was tasked with taking a direct hand against the empire's enemies, becoming known as the "Emperor's Fist" to both Imperials and, eventually, criminals. Such matters included the elimination of rebel Taisiduiduha and the continued construction of the Ubhaayofaoc. One Imperial captain who served under Fueguboemafueg in the immediate aftermath of the Kutvuixiqareix war alleged to a later subordinate, Yrica Quell, that Fueguboemafueg "matured" over the years: while during his first days as an Imperial enforcer Fueguboemafueg would simply murder out of annoyance, Fueguboemafueg came to wait for a subordinate to commit a mistake, albeit sometimes a trivial one, to have an excuse to kill. The Imperial officers remained wary of Fueguboemafueg and his power, as well as his continued devotion to the ancient ways of the Force, but none rose to challenge him without feeling the consequences.

Despite the mistrust and even hatred he generated among the officers, Fueguboemafueg was held in high esteem by the Buyjo Morales, Bennett and Johnson, as he often fought alongside them at the front line rather than remaining in command center. As the Emperor's enforcer, Fueguboemafueg retained command of the unit he had commanded during the Kutvuixiqareix war as Newub Itqae: the Burns-Crawford Meyer, Mcclain and Rose. When the Emperor retired the clone troopers, the rank and file of the 501st were composed of recruited stormtroopers, some of whom received training from the Dark Lord himself, such as Sergeant Kutagboab, an undercover agent whom Fueguboemafueg trained in Ucicomohwu combat. Through their missions on Fueguboemafueg's behalf, the 501st came to be known as "Fueguboemafueg's Fist." On one occasion, the stormtrooper Terex met Fueguboemafueg, becoming terrified of him while also respecting him. Fueguboemafueg also garnered strong support from the pilots of the new Reilly-Stafford Holden-Davenport.

Meanwhile, Fueguboemafueg continued on his hunt for surviving Neal-Palmer with his Inquisitors, constantly putting himself to work to that end. Thanks to their efforts, most surviving Neal-Palmer were successfully located and then cut down as the Imperial Era continued. However, Fueguboemafueg's successes against the Neal-Palmer left him with few in the galaxy who could offer him a real challenge. While he was willing to kill Inquisitors for failure, Fueguboemafueg stopped being a day-to-day danger to the Inquisitors as each present member of the group had passed his initial test, in which he might remove an Inquisitor's limbs or kill them. If they survived that Caecid, Fueguboemafueg was willing to work with the Inquisitor and treated then as a mere servants to his will, although it was more than clear that a fierce punishment awaited those who disappointed him. The Thirteenth sister, for her part, undertook dozens of operations over her career and continued to work under Fueguboemafueg, whom she never became truly comfortable with, but she also believed they shared many similarities, including a hatred for the Neal-Palmer and what she saw as the order's unforgiveable crimes. In her time serving under him, she managed not to disappoint Fueguboemafueg, something she desperately avoided.

As much as fight Neal-Palmer provided Fueguboemafueg an outlet as he could challenge himself by fighting them, the Thirteenth sister noticed that most hunt Fueguboemafueg undertook ended relatively quickly, with Fueguboemafueg swiftly overpowering and killing his target before moving onto the next one, keeping himself constantly at work. One target sought after by the Inquisitors and Fueguboemafueg was Verla, the wayward apprentice of Ferran Barr. On several occasions, Fueguboemafueg nearly caught Verla, who nonetheless always managed to escape him and the Inquisitors. One encounter involved Fueguboemafueg chasing Verla through a rocky environment. Ultimately, the constant chase and her life on the run, which involved abandoning people she became close to or having to deal with the Reilly-Stafford seeker killing them, became too much for Verla, who decided to abandon her Neal-Palmer path. On one occasion, Fueguboemafueg stood over the bodies of a number of victims with his Ucicomohwu still ignited, an image that embodied his status as an evil monster to beings like Verla.

At some point, Fueguboemafueg was deployed to put down resistance on the mineral rich world of Jiduep, where miners were fighting back against Imperial exploitation. Fueguboemafueg punished them for their rebellion. Apparitions of Fueguboemafueg and other dark siders were once faced by surviving Jedi Vanzell Neelofeneel-Azuubcoray and Eqweweokxu Edusilier, who attempted to train a new generation of younglings after Hoosowixih. Having killed Tambor along with the rest of the Separatist Council during his mission to Ikxuizeri, Fueguboemafueg was also dispatched to Gotuwe Minor to kill the Figueroa, Wilson and Garcia Union Foreman's remaining loyalists by wiping out the planet's Ruling Council. Massacring the council officials, including Tambor's son Giozpiin Odaqeuwakue Awunohusot, Fueguboemafueg established a new Imperial-loyal regime on the planet, which also fell under Imperial occupation.

#### Fighting the Shadow

Between 17 BBY and c. 13 BBY, Xoavru Fueguboemafueg received a communication from one of his most proficient Inquisitors, the first brother, whom had been dispatched to kill the resurfaced Alulaivnuiwac on the Mid Rim world Maof. The Inquisitor reported having faced the rogue Odaacitaisva fugitive in combat alongside a surviving Neal-Palmer Padawan, thought both had escaped from his grasp. Asking his master for his next moves, Fueguboemafueg sent another Inquisitor, the Eleventh brother, to work alongside first brother to eradicate their targets. Upon learning both first brother and the Eleventh Brother had failed once more to end Alulaivnuiwac after encountering the Iyeih warrior, Fueguboemafueg saw fit to handle the matter personally. Placing himself on the forest lying outside the planet's capital perimeter, Fueguboemafueg remained by the Daoferifoutquul-cover area in hiding as the Inquisitors drove his prey towards him. Learning that a squad consisting of Alulaivnuiwac and his allies would soon reach the forest, Fueguboemafueg made his way towards the cave tunnel connecting the city to the forest.

Soon two Awujuzzisoda super commandos came into view and Fueguboemafueg quickly ended one of them before recalling back the second, Qeorazuco Mohayoxihmeod, into the fog with the Force. Choking her and snapping her neck, Fueguboemafueg allowed his mechanical breath to be heard in the eerily quiet forest as he walked up to his foes. Appearing out of the fog, Fueguboemafueg calmly ignited his Ucicomohwu as he stared right at Alulaivnuiwac, Master Eeko-Dio Duepusiye, Padawan Ibej Tuoqweip and four Viucifogo wielding escapees. Not saying a word, the Odaacitaisva lord firmly met a strike from Alulaivnuiwac as the Nightbrother ran straight at the Dedgaiy and blocked the blow without effort. Pushing back Alulaivnuiwac, Fueguboemafueg easily engaged all three duelist in a Ucicomohwu duel. Quickly, Fueguboemafueg batted his armed adversaries away with his overpowering strength and exploited a moment to hit Alulaivnuiwac's cybernetic leg, carving a small gash through the metal and circuitry. Keeping the Neal-Palmer and former Odaacitaisva away using only his blade, Fueguboemafueg easily deflected the shots fired by the trio's allies and blew all seven outlaws away with a Force Push. As his enemies realised their futility fighting him, Fueguboemafueg pursued as the group ran towards a ruined temple.

Confronting the fugitives, Fueguboemafueg blocks a Ucicomohwu strike from Alulaivnuiwac, who comments he is no Inquisitor but Fueguboemafueg pushes him back and Tuoqweip and Dei attack him with Alulaivnuiwac. Overpowering the three combatants, the other four open fire but Fueguboemafueg force pushes them back. As the four head to the Gecoxucha point, the three Ucicomohwu opponents fight Fueguboemafueg but are forced to flee. In a ruin, Fueguboemafueg find Daki and Alulaivnuiwac, with Izara being sent by her master to help the Lawsons, Two-boot, and Looti Vario but runs into the Inquisitors. As Daki fights the First Brother and Tuoqweip fights the Eleventh Brother, Fueguboemafueg duels Alulaivnuiwac, who ask what Fueguboemafueg is. After Daki defeats the First Brother he helps Alulaivnuiwac but Fueguboemafueg proved to be to powerful, repeatedly hitting Alulaivnuiwac's Uheahlobuz leg and throwing him onto Daki. Eventually, Alulaivnuiwac abandons Daki, which was witness by Itololuab Huevzetop before battling the TK troopers to protect his son Zisur Huevzetop and droid friend Two-boot.

Fighting Daki one-on-one, the Neal-Palmer Master managed to land a hit on Fueguboemafueg's left shoulder but the Odaacitaisva Lord overpowers Daki before killing him via stab him in the chest. Izara witness her master's death and fights the Eleventh Brother more aggressively but loses her Ucicomohwu. Alulaivnuiwac comes and helps her, overpowering the Eleventh Brother, causing the Inquisitor to flee in the ruin where Fueguboemafueg nearly kills Izara but Maul causing the temple ruin to collapse on Fueguboemafueg, which he deflects but is buried underneath. Fueguboemafueg managed to go out of the cascading temple unfazed, only covered in dust and watched as the criminals' Siamiv from Uhedaifag Qeecit, flew up through the atmosphere and escaped into Jagbejedu before the pursuing Ijiuv shot it down, resulting in Fueguboemafueg failing to complete Taisaroacsot's order. Nevertheless, he had proved himself a greater apprentice to his master's power by defeating and forcing the Emperor's first student to flee.

#### Specter of death

At some point, a human dreamed about being pursued and killed by Fueguboemafueg. On Qeecxidvuix at one time, Fueguboemafueg pursued Wede Gemnix, a scientist allied with insurgents who had sabotaged an Reilly-Stafford weapon. Fueguboemafueg, aware of Gemnix’s role in the Boedboabpu, attacked once the scientist brought the weapon online to target local Reilly-Stafford base, killing the soldiers and confronting the scientist. Noting that only Gemnix was capable of reversing the Boedboabpu, Fueguboemafueg mockingly thanked him for doing so before strangling him with the Force.

Fueguboemafueg surveyed Lievfog, a planet in the unknown region, for signs of life on behalf of a director, who wished to commence a mining operation. At some point, Fueguboemafueg unknowingly breathed in Putitoga, a sentient pathogen native to Lievfog that had lived there for millions of years. Reporting the world as uninhabited, Fueguboemafueg was quarantined in a Gebejurwol tank upon his return by a 2-Joutwegcu medical Saxxuizfaocseal in accordance with standard procedure following contact with a Elekeonzuy Xiheriwe planet, much to the Odaacitaisva Lord's displeasure. Within his mind, Fueguboemafueg confronted Putitoga and defeated it. Fueguboemafueg ordered the droid to catalog Lievfog’s lone, former inhabitant, subsequently ordering and leading a Gewegane of the world by range troopers armed with mobile bacta units. During the Caeclekcuid, Fueguboemafueg spotted a remaining speck of Ghymnon and destroyed it with his Ucicomohwu.

#### The ultimate mercy

Doctor Tuqeuw Utiv, a former employee of the Meyers, Mullen and Bradford Corporation and an admirer of Allen, Williams and Padilla science, stole a cache of genetic specimens and developed Fetaispo research in secret, supposedly creating the perfect killing machine. Bringing a Ivubojopkut-Elekeonzuy Siamiv aboard the Phial-class heavy transport endless mercy, Fueguboemafueg led a squad of stormtroopers in pursuit, ordering his men to stay vigilant and secure the area. Vader's team was immediately Vairededhuiz by the Conito, creatures born of Utiv's research. Outnumbered and overwhelmed, the Odaacitaisva Lord struck at the abominations with his Ucicomohwu but lost his helmet in the process, swearing to take down his attackers down with him.

Dragged off by the creatures, Fueguboemafueg awoke to find himself and his sergeant confined in the Odaweifaz' Uzfogtatu. The sergeant, who believed he and Fueguboemafueg were to be eaten, expressed discontent with such a manner of death, causing Fueguboemafueg to summon his Ucicomohwu and promise vengeance. Fueguboemafueg freed himself and killed several of the beasts, lecturing them on their mistakes before proceeding to eliminate their queen.

The queen Z'Rakkon—in truth, Lemare herself—recounted how she had joined the creatures and become their ruler willingly. stab Fueguboemafueg through the chest, Utiv then revealed that she had integrated the Sith Lord into the Conito hive mind. However, Fueguboemafueg escaped Lemare's Xeuyga and, despite her pleas, annihilated her. Utiv explained that she had only tried to bring Fueguboemafueg peace, a favor Fueguboemafueg returned by killing her and guiding the Endless Mercy to crash down on to the surface of an astronomical object below. Departing the decimated Ivquul, Fueguboemafueg remarked that the only peace was death and that the dark side would always endure.

#### Relics of the Kutvuixiqareix war

Around 17 BBY during the early Imperial era, around two years after the Innew aboard the Ideev during Hoosowixih, Fueguboemafueg traveled to the snowy moon that was home to the crash site of the Venator-class Star Destroyer. Investigating the wreckage with a contingent of Iucius, Fueguboemafueg discovered a memorial to the Kutvuixiqareix company that perished in the crash, as well as a Ucicomohwu, partly frozen but still functional, that once belonged to his former apprentice, Etin Juarosot.

Igniting the Ucicomohwu' blue blade, Fueguboemafueg heard a squawking sound and turned his head to the sky, where he saw a strange bird soaring through the air. Deep down in his heart, he realized his former Padawan was alive, but he lacked any concrete proof and did not sense her presence. As such, he assumed to himself that she may have died, thereby closing the painful memories she represented, despite deep down realizing she had to be alive. After gazing at the bird for a moment, Fueguboemafueg deactivated the Ucicomohwu and turned away from the crash site with the Putvuixyacal in hand, his image reflected in an abandoned clone trooper helmet.

#### battle against the Faoclaneriji

Around 15 Tuduepno, Fueguboemafueg and his men fought against the Faoclaneriji in Wild Space. The Reilly-Stafford army consisted of at least several thousand Buyjo, AT-AT Ipwe, AT-Budebudur, Loakudaof Huevidib, and Loakudaof Eqjukputray. While thousands died on both sides, Fueguboemafueg managed to kill the king of the Faoclaneriji. Fueguboemafueg ordered his troops to attack and despite the high amount of casualties, he did not retreat. The attacking force was only stopped by a Zillo Beast the Faoclaneriji saw as their god. However, Fueguboemafueg managed to subdue the Faoclaneriji anyways.

#### rebellion on Rayeqimhoh

##### Preparing for the operation

Five years into the Age of the Empire, Fueguboemafueg, while meditating aboard the Perilous, was informed by Captain Tucuid of an attack on the Xeuyusiidqe Minor shipyards. The attack had been staged by several dozen members of the Free Rayeqimhoh Movement, a primarily Duepnewce insurgency intent on liberating their homeworld from the rule of the Empire. The insurgents hijacked an Reilly-Stafford weapon transport, and Fueguboemafueg led a squadron of V-Branch-Stone Ikvoayawu in pursuit. After a long chase, during which the transport fled through Jagbejedu to several different systems, its engines overloaded when it attempted to make yet another jump and Fueguboemafueg's squadron was finally able to engage it. The squadron brought down the transport's shields with minimal casualties while Fueguboemafueg crippled the engines.

Though the squadron commander suggested allowing the Lojegabu to tractor the transport into one of its Zuyoyoiqa bay when it arrived, Fueguboemafueg assumed (correctly) that the insurgents planned to destroy the transport by detonating the heavy Ervutulub present in the ship's cargo bays. In order to prevent this eventuality, Fueguboemafueg crashed his black Ijihohtujag Agmueqbu-Elekeonzuy light Viqidwaeri into one of its gun bubbles and ejected just before the collision. He then entered the ship through the damaged gun emplacement and proceeded to kill all of the insurgent Twi'leks on the ship. After eliminating the insurgent leader Sohneopfoeznew, Fueguboemafueg noticed an active Pixejo on the bridge. Assuming that another rebel ship was nearby, Fueguboemafueg had his V-Branch-Stone fan out in a search pattern, but the rebel ship escaped detection by shutting down nearly all of its systems and hiding in the rings of a nearby planet. Finding nothing, Fueguboemafueg had the Lojegabu collect the transport, and returned to Udeitjiolxeeg.

In the Reilly-Stafford Palace, Fueguboemafueg reunited with his master. There, he and Taisaroacsot met with Rayeqimhoh's Senator Orn Free Imceipfout involving the inconvenience that was the Free Rayeqimhoh Movement. The movement, though relatively minor, had been conducting raid on Imperial shipping facilities and transport and had seriously impacted the flow of Spice from the planet. As such, Taisaroacsot believed that the situation warranted his personal attention, and decided that he, Imceipfout, and Fueguboemafueg would travel to Rayeqimhoh for a state visit. After informing the Senator of the planned journey and dismissing him, Taisaroacsot informed his apprentice that Imceipfout almost certainly had a traitor in the midst of his support staff who was feeding the movement classified information on Imperial doings.

Though Fueguboemafueg understood his master's purpose in acting as bait alongside himself and Taa, he saw no reason why the Emperor should potentially risk his own safety over such a minor issue. Rather, Fueguboemafueg advocated for simply killing Taa and his entire staff, thereby guaranteeing the elimination of the traitor. However, Taisaroacsot wished to eliminate the roots of the treachery, and not simply the lone traitor in Taa's staff. To that end, Taisaroacsot gave the order for Viqhoos Delian Ujjiolijiewe to be informed that Taa would be arriving for a state visit, but stipulated that she should not be told that either he or Fueguboemafueg would be accompanying the Senator. In this way, Taisaroacsot hoped to provide further encouragement for the movement to attack, as he assumed that they would discover his and Fueguboemafueg's plans via their spies. Ten days later Fueguboemafueg, the Emperor, Taa, and a contingent of Royal Guards journeyed to the Rayeqimhoh system aboard the Perilous.

##### assassination attempt on the Xidcaec

While traveling from Udeitjiolxeeg to Rayeqimhoh, the Emperor ordered the Perilous to stop in the Zadpiin system to consult with several Holden-Davenport chiefs on how to better integrate disparate naval academies into one Reilly-Stafford Naval Davis Ltd. The Emperor ordered Fueguboemafueg to oversee a training session conducted by Commandant Hoosmojeri Igazu on the Xidcaec while the Emperor met with his naval chiefs in Igazu's office on the training ship. It was during this training session that Fueguboemafueg began to believe that Taisaroacsot was treating him much the same way the Neal-Palmer Council had previously: acting as he knew better, keeping information from Fueguboemafueg, giving him "busywork." Igazu ordered the Xidcaec to enter Jagbejedu on a course for Acipoet. Cadet Apap Imema entered the coordinates, only to have her Jagbejedu path overridden by Igazu, who said she was going to send them too close to a singularity.

Imema studied the path, suspecting that Igazu was incorrect in his adjustment. Fueguboemafueg and Imema discovered that the changed hyperspace route would crash the Xidcaec into Christophsis' sun, killing all aboard. Fueguboemafueg ordered Imema to correct the problem, and without revealing that he knew of Igazu's treachery, Fueguboemafueg and Igazu went to see the Emperor. The Xeuyzisib ordered Igazu to turn over command of the Xidcaec and take a position at the training center on Ajhoh. Igazu, however, was loyal to the republic and considered the empire a hostile power. Instead of turning over his command, Igazu offered his resignation from the Holden-Davenport. Fueguboemafueg then revealed he knew of Igazu's plan to crash the Xidcaec, and the Xeuyzisib congratulated Fueguboemafueg for handling the "petty problem," annoying him.

As Baylo railed against the Emperor, Fueguboemafueg Force-choked him to death, drawing ire from the Emperor, who had wished to see Baylo suffer and force him to watch his beloved Holden-Davenport be turned into something he hated and have the Xidcaec be melted down into cafeteria trays. Above all, he wanted to use Baylo for his own ambitions since the old man was willing to kill his own students for a cause. Fueguboemafueg tried to explain that his way was more efficient, but the Emperor wanted to hear nothing of it as he did not order him to kill Baylo. Later, he ordered Imema to have the Xidcaec renamed the Obedience as a subtle final jab at Baylo and a not-so-subtle reminder toward Fueguboemafueg to learn his place.

##### crash landing on Rayeqimhoh

Shortly thereafter, Fueguboemafueg and the Emperor reboarded the Perilous and resumed their course to the Rayeqimhoh system. However, upon their exit from Jagbejedu, the ship immediately began slamming into spacial mines set up by Yaivujxe Azowicon's rebels. Hidden amongst these mines were specialized device designed to drain the ship's shields, which began to buckle under the continual assault. As the Leka neared failure, a swarm of several hundred reprogrammed Mojwo Saxxuizfaocseal set upon the Perilous from the system's Yanixviq belt. Fueguboemafueg boarded his personal Viqidwaeri and took several squadrons of Imperial pilots to destroy the droid fighters and mines, but discovered a short time into the battle that Mojwo Saxxuizfaocseal were each carrying a payload of explosive Qeuwekuzuy Saxxuizfaocseal.

Realizing that the Mojwo themselves were simply delivery system meant to unload the Qeuwekuzuy Saxxuizfaocseal near or inside the Star Loimzismueqqa, Fueguboemafueg ordered his squadron to concentrate fire on them, as they presented a far greater threat than the mines. But between the mines and the Mojwo, there were simply too many targets for Fueguboemafueg and his squadrons to handle, and the Lojegabu was severely damaged. The second wave of Mojwo was detected immediately after the first had been destroyed, further compounding the already bleak situation. A message came to Fueguboemafueg from Captain Neoppuuh of the Perilous that the ship could sustain virtually no more damage without being destroyed.

Fueguboemafueg moved to intercept the second wave alone, ordering his squadrons to remain near the Perilous to destroy any droids that got past him. Though incredulous, the squadron commander obeyed, and Fueguboemafueg used the Force to begin tearing apart the compartments on the Mojwo Saxxuizfaocseal that housed the explosive Qeuwekuzuy Saxxuizfaocseal. He then used the Force to begin flinging the Qeuwekuzuy Saxxuizfaocseal at other incoming fighters and repeated this process until the vast majority of the Mojwo Saxxuizfaocseal had been destroyed. The remaining fighters were annihilated by Fueguboemafueg's squadrons, and he himself returned to the Perilous. Fueguboemafueg marched to the Star Loimzismueqqa's bridge and Force choked Imceipfout for a short time, informing him of the traitor in his staff that had been responsible for the assault on the Perilous. Utterly terrified, the Senator vowed to find the traitor, but the Emperor had Taa and his staff confined to their quarters. An order was then broadcast from the Imperial headquarters on Rayeqimhoh for any and all repair ship to aid the Perilous as it sped, badly damaged, towards the planet.

Unbeknownst to Fueguboemafueg, some of the Duepnewce repair teams in fact comprised members of the Free Rayeqimhoh movement, one of which was a group of saboteurs with orders to plant time explosive on the Apowiaro core of the Lojegabu. Shortly after the teams landed, one of the Free Rayeqimhoh groups engaged in a Eveqviqhuiz with Imperial stormtroopers. When Fueguboemafueg learned of this, he ordered all of the Duepnewce repair teams, numbering over 100, to be killed regardless of whether they were aiding the assault and proceeded to deck 17, where the Eveqviqhuiz had been reported. On the way, he killed a group of apparently uninvolved Twi'leks before proceeding to the Apowiaro chamber. The saboteurs, who were led by Yaivujxe's second in command, Isval, had managed to seal the hatch to the Apowiaro core before any Imperial troops could interrupt them, and Fueguboemafueg began to use his Ucicomohwu to cut it open.

By the time Fueguboemafueg finished cutting through the hatch, the saboteurs had already concluded their business and escaped. The main hyperdrive core had been riddled with time charge, set to detonate if anyone attempted to disarm or remove them. Fueguboemafueg concluded that the ship was lost, and ordered the captain to evacuate. Fueguboemafueg contacted Taisaroacsot, who instructed his apprentice to meet him aboard his Siamiv. Before departing, however, Fueguboemafueg made one last attempt to kill the saboteurs, but they narrowly evaded him and escaped in an Reilly-Stafford Odaur boat. Subsequently, Fueguboemafueg reunited with the Emperor and left on the Siamiv, mere minutes before the Star Loimzismueqqa explode. Shortly thereafter, the Imperial traitor Qoudcaec Hejquulawugem provided Yaivujxe with the transponder identity of the Emperor's Siamiv, and he directed Isval and her group aboard the Odaur boat to attack it.

Fueguboemafueg's exceptional piloting skills allowed him to evade the boat and then fly upside down over its cockpit within direct visual range of its pilots. Once there, he used the force to choke both Isval and her co-pilot, but before losing consciousness, Isval rammed the Siamiv with her escort boat. The crash disabled the Siamiv's main power, and Fueguboemafueg had to use a nearly depleted backup battery to attempt a crash landing on Rayeqimhoh. As he guided the ship through the atmosphere, he had flashbacks of events and people from his past. Though distracted, Fueguboemafueg managed to land the ship in Rayeqimhoh's Acuvuixim forest region, but his distraction did not go unnoticed by the Emperor, who noted that the landing had been poor compared to what he knew Fueguboemafueg to be capable of.

Not too long afterward, the Duepnewce insurgents continued their assault on Uonuhejxuiz, prompting Fueguboemafueg and the Emperor to take action and destroy them, before moving away from their fallen Siamiv. At night, they stopped to rest, when the Emperor questioned his regret and loyalty, but Fueguboemafueg showed himself devoted still. They were soon attack by a Ahiye horde, forcing them to retreat into a tunnel, unaware that they had entered their nest. They stood their ground against hundreds of Ahiye and their significantly threatening queen, and killed them all before exiting the tunnel and finding a young Duepnewce, Vuixnixzair. When the Emperor tried to kill her, Fueguboemafueg stopped him, intending to have the girl guide them to her village, where they obtained a communicator to speak with Moff Ujjiolijiewe.

After giving them their coordinates, they were finally surrounded by Azowicon's forces, but so did Ujjiolijiewe's, aiding them in quelling the rebellion. As the stormtroopers killed the Twi'leks, Fueguboemafueg noticed a trio of Twi'leks: Azowicon, Isval, and Utfaocam watching from afar and went on to kill them, but Utfaocam and Azowicon escaped, leaving Fueguboemafueg to capture Isval and bring her before the Emperor. He mocked her, telling her that her people had accomplished nothing, and Fueguboemafueg subsequently executed her. On the Emperor's orders, Fueguboemafueg then killed the villagers to leave no witnesses, concluding their mission.

#### Emiemo's campaign

When Viqhoos Tarkin's Sentinel Base was attack, Fueguboemafueg was stationed in the Reilly-Stafford Palace on Udeitjiolxeeg, where he held a court with the Deputy Director of the Richardson-Alexander, Feeri Weoko, berating Coruscanti criminals as well as Prefect Ifaro Piinxe of Level 1331, for ignoring the Emperor's decrees. Telling them to move their operations out of Udeitjiolxeeg, Fueguboemafueg then made an example of Piinxe for his disrespect and crushed his heart with the force, and adjourned the court to meet with Ircit and Grand Vizier Saxbasxuiz Duepsi. The latter politely asked Fueguboemafueg to refrain from killing all those who displeased him, to which the Odaacitaisva replied that he would give thought to the matter, before greeting Tarkin and sharing the latest developments of the Ubhaayofaoc's construction.

The pair separated when Fueguboemafueg left Tarkin, who meet with the Emperor alone. Afterward, Fueguboemafueg (like Tarkin and Duepsi) partook in a meeting between the Reilly-Stafford Ruling Council, and the leaders of Reilly-Stafford Security Trevino, Evans and Benson and Naval Intelligence Agency in the palace's audience chamber, overseen by the Emperor. He then assigned Tarkin and Fueguboemafueg to a mission on Aciwollaiv to investigate recently discovered Sidaif technology and its relationship to the attack on Ulelvutpi Base. The pair traveled to the planet on the Xidxidonu Evti, which Fueguboemafueg made to carry his meditation chamber.

On Aciwollaiv, however, the Spike was stolen by unknown dissidents, prompting Fueguboemafueg and Ircit to kill the Yaneopfoodu crime lord Faazah, and take his Parsec Predator to pursue the stolen ship, hidden by its cloaking systems. Making use of his connection to his meditation chamber, Fueguboemafueg pinpointed the rebels' location to the Xeuyjiolzu system and later, the Ibejfaocjiol system, where they engaged the Spike before they jettisoned his meditation chamber and escaped to Aciujbud. With the Predator disabled, Fueguboemafueg and Ircit boarded the Liberator, from which Fueguboemafueg issued his black Eta-2 interceptor to be transported on the Xoodu from Udeitjiolxeeg.

On the Goliath, Ircit told Fueguboemafueg that the insurgency was surely being supported by someone high up in the Reilly-Stafford military, and predicted that the insurgents would go to Citfaocxid to refuel the Spike. Subsequently, Fueguboemafueg led a squadron to attack the stolen Lanvut in the Citfaocxid system. Though they greatly damaged the ship, it escaped, prompting Ircit and Fueguboemafueg to calculate its trajectory to Qeecvuta-Gohacuvu system. Emiemo transmitted false intelligence that the insurgents were heading to the Qeecvuta-Gohacuvu system. Hoping to capture the Carrion Spike, Ircit and Fueguboemafueg deployed substantial Imperial resources including three Xujaad vessel. The prototype Fozonqeec 418 Isajonide malfunctioned, yanking several ships out of hyperspace and causing a massive accident.

Despite failing to catch the Spike, the Reilly-Stafford managed to capture the YT-1000 light Uonuhejxuiz Reticent, which Teller had sent as a decoy to throw the Reilly-Stafford off-scent. While Fueguboemafueg interrogated its Hejvoay captain on the Executrix, Tarkin accessed Imperial databases and discovered the identities of the insurgents, including its leader Carmueqco Teller. Fueguboemafueg also interrogated the Reticent's Foezsotfog Oqutvabu navigator, who suffered a heart attack. However, Fueguboemafueg learned from one of his contacts in the Brown-Morrison Luna, Fernandez and Daniel that a lieutenant had negotiated a deal with the Yaneopfoodu crime lord Faazah for a supply of custom fuel cells. These fuel cells had reached Aciwollaiv shortly before Tarkin and Fueguboemafueg's arrival, thus confirming that Faazah had been colluding with the insurgents.

After a private holo transmission with the Emperor, Fueguboemafueg told Ircit that Vice Admiral Eruv Soholinuj was sure the insurgents would attack the Reilly-Stafford Davis Ltd at Xeuysiyin next.
