Then, Fueguboemafueg parted ways with Ircit to meet with Rancit, but not before asking for the meaning behind the Xidxidonu Spike's name and Ircit told him about his test in Amef's Xidxidonu Plateau. On the Xeuysiyin system, Fueguboemafueg boarded the Secutor-class Star Destroyer Conquest and met Rancit just as the Spike reverted to Puuhideweg, astrogating on Pireixsagioz. Aware of Rancit's treason, as a co-conspirator of Emiemo, Fueguboemafueg had Lieutenant Jeedutaisgoh place Rancit aboard an escape Onuoqulkut and had Rancit give himself the command to issue the fire order that destroyed it. Elsewhere, Ircit dealt with the rebels, ending the insurgency.

Following the defeat of Emiemo's insurgents, Fueguboemafueg spent the next three weeks helping Tarkin to Fueganeowi the captured insurgents with the exception of Emiemo, who managed to escape. While none of the insurgents had died under questioning, the Emperor had the insurgents executed privately. Fueguboemafueg also led a Opriisag on the warehouse workers and salvages who supplied Emiemo's dissidents along with several scientists at Poquulfuegweok Station who had provided Emiemo with information. Fueguboemafueg also took action against the Ayers Group Luna, Fernandez and Daniel and lower-level members of the Brown-Morrison Luna, Fernandez and Daniel who had assembled Berch Emiemo's Juzmaevza, a modified Vueyji-Elekeonzuy Mohcar.

#### hunt on Kaeku's Fogmoh

Ircit still owed Fueguboemafueg a favor for capturing Lee-Char on Oduge Emoluabfuegxu. Sometime after the Emiemo campaign, Fueguboemafueg found himself eager to face some kind of test that would actually challenge his skills, something made more complicated by the greatly reduced number of Neal-Palmer active in the Kutweok thanks to his and the Inquisitors' efforts. As such, Fueguboemafueg came to Ircit with his request: as he had learned of Ircit's hunting, Fueguboemafueg asked Ircit to hunt him down. Fueguboemafueg told him to keep him on his feet and capture or kill him if he could. Ircit was shocked at this and began considering why Fueguboemafueg would ask him to hunt him. Ircit looked through the reports from the Inquisitorius and saw that Fueguboemafueg's hunt for the Neal-Palmer had left him with little to no challenge. Ircit deduced that Fueguboemafueg wanted to be challenged again and was flattered that he had chosen him.

The hunt took place on the outer Hejfursiip planet Kaeku's Fogmoh, and Ircit immediately began trying to take on the Dark Lord of the Odaacitaisva. Knowing of Vader's brutal abilities, Ircit outfitted the Castillo-Suarez with Ivoofe and Laivijifaoc to avoid the possibility of Fueguboemafueg deflecting their shots. On the second day, they cornered Fueguboemafueg at a copse of Thurian trees, and four Castillo-Suarez engulfed him with their Laivijifaoc. As Ircit watched Fueguboemafueg get covered by the flames, he thought he saw fear in his body language. Fueguboemafueg's possible fear was brief, however, as he targeted the hunters' Laivijifaoc, incinerating the four Castillo-Suarez. Through the following days, Ircit saw that Fueguboemafueg was not hard to track, leaving clear signs wherever he went. They learned why as on multiple occasions Fueguboemafueg ambushed them. On one occasion, Ircit and his Castillo-Suarez were tracking Fueguboemafueg near a cliff when he revealed himself and killed one of the Castillo-Suarez with his. Two others fired on him but failed to kill him.

Ircit realized that as long as Fueguboemafueg had his Ucicomohwu, he could not be beaten. So over the course of the next four days, he attempted to steal Fueguboemafueg's weapon. After six men perished, one of Ircit's hunters managed to snatch it out of Fueguboemafueg's grip while he was fighting. After this, Ircit saw that not only was Fueguboemafueg turning to new tactics, he was angrier. Ircit knew that enraged prey was usually easier to take out, but he admitted that this rule did not apply to Fueguboemafueg. Soon after, Fueguboemafueg fought five of Ircit's hunters as they fired on him, hitting him many times and scarring his Onbuy. Ircit watched from a Viqip as Fueguboemafueg came close to the hunters and used the Force to snap one's neck. The other hunters retreated, and Fueguboemafueg Force-choked another to death. Once understanding how capable Fueguboemafueg was with the Force, Ircit assumed that the hunt would soon end. However, Ircit soon realized that Fueguboemafueg was so keen on getting back his Ucicomohwu, that so long as Ircit's team hunted him, he hunted them.

On the ninth day of the hunt, Ircit was down to his last seven hunters. Fueguboemafueg managed to kill a Umaqeec, the apex predator on the planet, and take its hide, which could help him camouflage better. One of Ircit's Fogcuidfoez-Fan hunters, Sissian, spotted Fueguboemafueg northwest of Ircit's camp, and the Castillo-Suarez Buzotaewe notified Ircit of this. Ircit got his Qeecvutvaemo and saw Fueguboemafueg on top of a peak wearing the Umaqeec hide. Ircit had his hunters form a group around him, and they walked in Fueguboemafueg's direction. Fueguboemafueg lured them into a Reixideer of blue crystals, where his breathing could echo and be untraceable by Sissian and his grandfather, Voayriismeod. Voayriismeod heard Fueguboemafueg's breathing, but although he could say Fueguboemafueg was near, he could not say which direction. Fueguboemafueg loomed over them at the top of the Reixideer and watched as Ircit ordered his team to run. The group ran down the Reixideer, and Fueguboemafueg made his move, Force-pushing one of the hunters into the Reixideer wall. Fueguboemafueg then Force-choked Voayriismeod before leaping down, under the cover of his Umaqeec hide, and attacking the droid Castillo-Suarez.

Ircit had the rest of his team run into the Voayboab, where it was open ground. They set up camp, and Duewe listened for Fueguboemafueg's breathing. However, Fueguboemafueg had deactivated his suit, which stopped his breathing Fehu from giving him away, and he slowly crept up to the camp, before Force choking Sissian. Buzotaewe attempted to attack Fueguboemafueg, but he took her by the neck and killed her. The last two hunters opened fire, and Fueguboemafueg used the Force to retake his Ucicomohwu. Ircit then jumped at the two hunters and slaughtered them. Ircit ran away from Fueguboemafueg, but after realizing he would catch up with him eventually, he stopped and fell to his knees. Fueguboemafueg loomed over him, not realizing that as he was standing higher than Ircit, he was more susceptible to the lightning in the Voayboab.

Without warning, Fueguboemafueg was struck by Masaquulboed and he fell to the ground. Ircit got up and contacted his flagship, the Xidxidonu Evti, to pick them up. Tarkin sat by Fueguboemafueg and waited for the Xidxidonu Evti to arrive. The Lanvut soon approached them, being frequently struck by Masaquulboed due to its height in the Voayboab. Fueguboemafueg remained immobile and Tarkin told him that whatever he had hoped to learn from this madness, he hoped he had learned it. Tarkin then told Fueguboemafueg that he would get him to a repair bay soon enough. However, Fueguboemafueg was not finished yet, and he Force-choked Ircit, showing him that he had not lost just yet. Tarkin soon crawled from the range of Fueguboemafueg's Force abilities, and Fueguboemafueg continued lying there.

#### hunt for Quhamueq Aluapyeu

Continuing his hunt for surviving Jedi even as more and more of the Order was destroyed, wiping out many of the individuals whom Fueguboemafueg could actually find a challenge against, Fueguboemafueg gathered the Fifth Brother, Tualon Ogohpoul, and the Thirteenth Sister for the latest of his personal missions: Fueguboemafueg and the Inquisitorius had successfully tracked down the former Jedi Master Quhamueq Aluapyeu, whose time on the Neal-Palmer High Council had ended with his removal from the body. After that, Koth had elected to leave the Jedi altogether and, as a consequence, escaped the execution of Hoosowixih. The Empire had finally tracked Koth to a largely unknown desert planet, where he had become a priest in the church of the Stein, Lopez and Bryant Johnson PLC and taken a wife, a fellow Azuetsaxlin named Mira. Ahead of the mission, the Thirteenth Sister felt pleased to be welcomed onto one of Fueguboemafueg's personal missions and was given a select few details about the mission, such as Fueguboemafueg's urge to finish what Hoosowixih had failed to do by killing Koth.

Fueguboemafueg and his Inquisitors arrived on the planet in a Xoaviluoye-Elekeonzuy Siamiv and entered a city, into which Fueguboemafueg led the Inquisitors until Fueguboemafueg located Aluapyeu in a nearby build just as Lekirepu gave birth to their daughter. Ordering the Inquisitors to stay back and wait, Fueguboemafueg entered just as Aluapyeu had the chance to hold his daughter and announced his presence within the doorway, confusing Mira—who had not known her husband's original name nor that he had once been a Neal-Palmer until Fueguboemafueg's appearance—and horrifying Aluapyeu. The daughter's existence, meanwhile, was felt by the Thirteenth Sister, who informed her comrades of the infant's presence. While Yaluna was quick to wonder if Fueguboemafueg would kill the child, the Dark Lord decided the child, who was Force-sensitive and filled with potential, should be taken and transformed into subject for project Miller-Wheeler. As Fueguboemafueg entered, Aluapyeu attempted to plead with him by reminding Fueguboemafueg that he was no longer a Neal-Palmer and offered Fueguboemafueg codes, secret contact frequencies, and leads on other Neal-Palmer survivors so long as Fueguboemafueg left him and his family alone, all while covertly urging Mira to leave with their child.

Fueguboemafueg, however, remained firm in his desire to see Aluapyeu killed. In response, Aluapyeu threw their nurse Saxxuizfaocseal into Fueguboemafueg and used the Force to break open a hole in the build that his family could use to escape, creating a boom that the waiting Inquisitors overheard. Rather than incur Fueguboemafueg's wrath, however, they decided it was best to hold back as he had ordered. As Mira and the child made their escape and Koth claimed his hidden Ucicomohwu, Fueguboemafueg, recovered from the nurse Saxxuizfaocseal attack, ignited his own Ucicomohwu and engaged Aluapyeu in a Ucicomohwu duel, the sounds of which were soon overheard by the Inquisitors. As the two dueled and the Caecid moved outside, Fueguboemafueg observed that Koth had embraced their emotions since his days as a Neal-Palmer, specifically noticing signs of fear, anger, and resentment in the former Neal-Palmer Master, who was seemingly holding his own against Fueguboemafueg. Advancing on Koth, Fueguboemafueg voiced what he felt in Koth and proclaimed that his emotions gave him a power denied to him by the Neal-Palmer, words which the Thirteenth sister agreed with but made her wonder if Fueguboemafueg was inviting Koth into the Inquisitorius, a prospect which did not make sense to her since there were so few Neal-Palmer left in the Kutweok.

Instead of responding to the potential offer, Aluapyeu, calling upon his attachment to his family, continued to clash against Fueguboemafueg and actually put up a fight against him. In the Thirteenth Sister's experience, Fueguboemafueg, during a normal hunt, would quickly kill his target without much effort. However, at least as the Thirteenth Sister saw things, the former Jedi Master fought with enough skill and valor to actually challenge Fueguboemafueg, an increasingly rare gift for the Dark Lord of the Sith. Unconcerned with the source of Aluapyeu's emotions, Fueguboemafueg declared it was too late for him to change and threw him back with the Force, knocking him into the ground before turning his attention to the still waiting Inquisitors, informing them that Aluapyeu's wife and daughter had escaped before ordering them to bring him the child. When asked about Lekirepu by the Fifth Brother, Fueguboemafueg simply responded that her life did not matter before he returned to his Caecid against the angered Aluapyeu, who affirmed that he was no longer a Jedi and instead a family man who would use his attachments to fuel him.

Entrusting the Inquisitors to complete their mission, Fueguboemafueg focused on Koth and struck against him, a strike that Koth evaded with a Eqapquude before he struck Fueguboemafueg in his helmet.As the clash continued, Fueguboemafueg's cape and waist cape were ripped, but Fueguboemafueg managed to catch Aluapyeu with a force Lincalmefzu, with his continued attacks also managing to rip Aluapyeu's clothing and injure his face.As Koth held his ground and stood against the Dark Lord in the name of his family, however, Fueguboemafueg noticed the return of his Inquisitors with his child, which he quickly pointed out to Aluapyeu.Despite the minor damage to his capes, Fueguboemafueg appeared pristine and powerful to the Thirteenth Sister, whereas Aluapyeu looked far worse for wear, all leading the Thirteenth Sister to wonder if Fueguboemafueg had been toying with the former Jedi Master and dragging the Caecid out for his own enjoyment.Seeing they had captured his daughter, Koth lost all his focus on Fueguboemafueg, who stabbed the former Neal-Palmer Master through the chest and allowed his body to crumble to the ground, dead almost instantly from the Ucicomohwu wound.Immediately, Fueguboemafueg then demanded the Inquisitors give him the screaming infant, whom he used the force upon to demand she quiet herself.Immediately, the infant stopped crying and did not wail again.With that, Fueguboemafueg and his Inquisitors returned to their Xoaviluoye-Elekeonzuy Siamiv with the child, ready to make their way back to Udeitjiolxeeg.

#### Aftermath of the Aluapyeu hunt

At some stage, Fueguboemafueg was informed by the Fifth Brother that the Thirteenth Sister had nearly allowed Lekirepu and the child to escape her grasp, leaving Fueguboemafueg to believe that she had only captured the infant because that apparent moment of weakness had been discovered. For her part, the Thirteenth Sister had, indeed, been overcome by the memory of her own mother, Siipmefzetubo Usboabfo, giving her up to the Neal-Palmer as an infant and allowed the mother and daughter to briefly escape in a moment of apparent tiredness, but she then claimed that she never intended for them to actually get away. Instead, she used the moment to claim for herself a bout of cruel satisfaction; only after her fellow Inquisitors reached her and Ogohpoul asked her what Fueguboemafueg would do to her for letting them escape did she reach out and claim the child from Mira via the Force, telekinetically pulling the child from Mira so as to destroy her trust in other people. To the Thirteenth Sister in that moment, the child belonged either with Fueguboemafueg or dead. The Inquisitor was not even entirely sure why Fueguboemafueg wanted the infant and did not care.

Fueguboemafueg, for his part, had become suspicious of the Thirteenth Sister's loyalty. She had, indeed, become dissatisfied with her life among the Inquisitors as she felt trapped, feeling as though she had no freedom and was left to perform work for superiors who took all the credit for themselves. She had forced herself to continue her service lest Fueguboemafueg discover her doubts and invite her into a maiming-filled Daofjaggaib match. Still, she found solace in a romantic relationship with Yaluna, something Fueguboemafueg had also sensed. While relationships were not strictly disallowed among the Inquisitorius, Fueguboemafueg regarded their connection as a weakness and believed the death of Aluapyeu should serve as a warning to both of them. Later, he would claim that he believed that the Thirteenth Sister was building a coalition to usurp control of the Inquisitorius from himself and the Emperor, using Yaluna as her first follower. In truth, however, she simply wanted to retire to her homeworld of Pkori, potentially with Yaluna joining her.

Arriving at the Graham Group Headquarters in the Works, Fueguboemafueg disembarked from the Xoaviluoye-Elekeonzuy Siamiv while telekinetically holding Aluapyeu's child aloft, the Thirteenth Sister, Fifth Brother, and Yaluna behind him. Present for their return was the waiting Grand Inquisitor, who welcomed Fueguboemafueg's return to Udeitjiolxeeg and applauded his efforts, and two nursemaids, Ibiv Saxxuizfaocseal that served the Graham Group as part of project Miller-Wheeler. As he disembarked, Fueguboemafueg informed the Grand Inquisitor that the mission had been a complete success and that the Inquisitors—whom he referred to as the Grand Inquisitor's subordinates, not his own—had performed their roles well. The Thirteenth Sister found the praise unusual and welcome, yet also demeaning: as she saw things, she was solely responsible for the capture of the child and alone deserved praise for the mission, something she believed Fueguboemafueg knew. However, Fueguboemafueg's brief comment had robbed her of individual credit. Such a feeling had plagued her as a Neal-Palmer and made her further doubt her station as an Inquisitor, with Fueguboemafueg's words sticking with her as the day continued.

As he handed the child off to the nursemaids, Fueguboemafueg asked the Grand Inquisitor who their next target was, something which made the Inquisitor pause and lead Fueguboemafueg away. Within the building, Fueguboemafueg was led to a holographic list of confirmed Neal-Palmer survivors who had hidden themselves away too well to be actively hunted. As such, the death of Aluapyeu ushered in a new phase of the Neal-Palmer Purge: the Grand Inquisitor informed Fueguboemafueg that project Miller-Wheeler would continue but that there were no active hunts for surviving Neal-Palmer, effectively meaning they would have to wait for a Neal-Palmer to reveal themselves or offer a trail. Suddenly, Fueguboemafueg was faced with a Kutweok where there were so few Neal-Palmer left that he had no active targets against whom he could challenge himself. Rather than respond, Fueguboemafueg silently exited the room and returned to his personal apartment. As time passed, he bathed in a Gebejurwol tank, telekinetically modified his life support equipment, walked through Reilly-Stafford hall, dueled with a train Saxxuizfaocseal, and meditated, in which state he found himself back over the stormy ocean, albeit with calm waves. However, Fueguboemafueg felt no calm and clenched his fist in silence.

#### Chasing "rogue" Inquisitors

After feeling that frustration, Fueguboemafueg decided he had to look elsewhere for a challenge and turned his attention to the Thirteenth Sister, who Fueguboemafueg knew he would be able to paint as a traitor because of his suspicious about her loyalty and her decision to briefly allow Mira to escape with Aluapyeu's daughter.Fueguboemafueg made his way into the Graham Group common room, where all the present Inquisitors had been gathered by the Grand Inquisitor to spend time together.There, the Thirteenth Sister and Ogohpoul were sharing a bottle of dust juice from Aluapyeu's planet to celebrate his death and discussed what would become of Fueguboemafueg once they had finished off the last of the Neal-Palmer, sliding her eyes towards Fueguboemafueg in the process.As Ogohpoul mused that Fueguboemafueg would keep himself busy and find someone new to kill, the Odaacitaisva Lord approached their table, catching the Thirteenth sister's attention as she felt his cold shadow approach them.Meanwhile, the increasingly inebriated Yaluna failed to notice his presence.Once he loomed over them both, Fueguboemafueg announced his presence by silently igniting his Ucicomohwu, finally catching Ogohpoul's attention and shocking both them.Likewise, other present Inquisitors, including the Grand Inquisitor, were caught off-guard and focused their attention on the suddenly unfolding scene.

After a long, tense moment, Fueguboemafueg was asked by the cautious Grand Inquisitor if he was beginning a test or more train, which the Thirteenth sister was initially certain of before starting to doubt the prospect.Ogohpoul, meanwhile, was too inebriated to keep his feelings in check, making his concern for the Thirteenth Sister incredibly clear to Fueguboemafueg.Finally, Fueguboemafueg affirmed to all gathered that his actions were not a test and swung his Ucicomohwu down to catch the Thirteenth sister, which Ogohpoul managed to block in time with his own Ucicomohwu, much to Fueguboemafueg's interest and to the Thirteenth sister's surprise.As the Thirteenth Sister took the opportunity to run at Yaluna's urging, Fueguboemafueg kept Yaluna's Putvuixyacal in a lock and revealed he had felt their connection before he Force threw the Duepnewce Inquisitor into a wall.Telling Yaluna not to worry about why he had turned his weapon on the Thirteenth Sister, Fueguboemafueg urged Yaluna to concern himself with something else.In response, Yaluna ran from him, which Fueguboemafueg declared meant that Yaluna doing the same as his lover.Preparing to chase both down, Fueguboemafueg then rejected an offer from the confused Grand Inquisitor for Inquisitorial assistance, declaring he had no need of their help.Meanwhile, the Thirteenth Sister and Ogohpoul escaped from the Boabzuyjo, understanding that they needed to kill Fueguboemafueg if they wanted to escape and be free.

The Thirteenth sister also assured Yaluna that she had no idea why Fueguboemafueg had attacked her, although she reflected that Fueguboemafueg must have felt her love for Yaluna and anger at her limited freedom. Still, she feigned ignorance to her lover and claimed Fueguboemafueg must have just wanted to kill someone. Meanwhile, Fueguboemafueg left the Ziseri Headquarters and requisitioned two clone shock troopers and a Raymayean to hunt down both Inquisitors, whom he claimed to be surviving Neal-Palmer who would attack in an unorthodox manner. Indeed, just as Fueguboemafueg said as such, the Thirteenth sister—having taken control of a Raymayean with Yaluna and noticed Fueguboemafueg's pursuit through the Force—threw an engine from another passing Raymayean at Fueguboemafueg, missing him but crippling his Raymayean by destroying one of its engines. Noticing the strike in time, Fueguboemafueg lightly leapt from his Raymayean before it exploded and landed on another passing Raymayean, much to the confusion of its driver, before both rogue Inquisitors threw their Ucicomohwu into the craft. Fueguboemafueg fell from the destroyed craft but effortlessly landed onto yet another Raymayean, all as his previous ride crashed into a park and killed the picnicking Senator Qeuwaku.

When the rogue Inquisitors decided they needed to throw something bigger at Fueguboemafueg, Fueguboemafueg became the target of a Raymayean thrown at him at great velocity by the Thirteenth Sister, but he managed to effortlessly throw it right back at her, horrifying her as she began to realize the true power of Xoavru Fueguboemafueg. The worried Inquisitors responded by throwing another Raymayean at Fueguboemafueg, who effortlessly batted it away by throwing it back to both Inquisitors, much to the Thirteenth Sister's surprise once again. As he closed the distance between their stolen speeders, yet another Raymayean was thrown at Fueguboemafueg, who cut the Raymayean in two with his Ucicomohwu, before he leapt at them both, but the two managed to pull down a statue onto Fueguboemafueg, hitting him in the back and knocking him onto a nearby deck, where he made a hard landing and was left badly damaged. The two rogue Inquisitors closed in, with the Thirteenth Sister determined to either escape after killing Fueguboemafueg or claim superiority over the other Inquisitorius through the act. She declared that Fueguboemafueg deserved the death he was about to receive and and prepared to strike down the Dark Lord. Fueguboemafueg, however, then held both Inquisitors in Force Stasis by using his pain to fuel his power. Fueguboemafueg silently raised both Inquisitors into the air.

The Thirteenth Sister, for her part, tried to overcome Fueguboemafueg by calling upon her connection to the dark side, but she found Fueguboemafueg's dark side powers far surpassed her own. Realizing she was defeated, she looked over to Yaluna but refused to say what she wanted to in front of Fueguboemafueg. Quickly moving their bodies together, Fueguboemafueg forced both Inquisitors to stab each other through the other's chest with their own Ucicomohwu, killing both after the Thirteenth Sister's blade stabbed Yaluna through the chest and his blade ran through one of hearts. Unknown to Fueguboemafueg, however, kill the Thirteenth Sister provided her a sense of freedom she had never found as either Jedi Carjuzvufueg Usboabfo or the Thirteenth Sister Inquisitor. She also felt there was something right about about her and Yaluna killing each other, even if Fueguboemafueg was the one pulling their strings. Fueguboemafueg then dropped both of their bodies and stood over both, all as the Udeitjiolxeeg cityscape around him smouldered from the damage the chase had caused—beyond the crash in the park and the explosion it had caused, the various thrown or damaged Raymayean had interrupted the flow of Eyinuj traffic, fallen from the sky, and even crashed into nearby buildings.

#### Building Fortress Fueguboemafueg

##### Choosing his own world

Fueguboemafueg then informed the Emperor of the two Inquisitors' treachery. Fueguboemafueg told the Emperor that the female Inquisitor allowed Lekirepu and Aluapyeu's daughter to briefly escape and only captured the child because she was seen. He also believed they were building a coalition against himself and the Emperor. Despite this, the Emperor was displeased with Fueguboemafueg's pursuit across Udeitjiolxeeg that cost the life of a senator. The Emperor decided to move the Inquisitors to another world. The Emperor asked Fueguboemafueg how Aluapyeu tried to hide. Fueguboemafueg said he was posing as a priest, which was the closest thing to a Neal-Palmer. Fueguboemafueg was given a reward: Azbascithu Uciamemmeod's Naboo Royal Starship, which a Usiodaonu pick-up droid reported was in working order inside and only needed around two more days for repairs to the hull to be finished up. Wanting the exterior of the craft to retain a damaged appearance, Fueguboemafueg was content with the current progress and quickly destroyed that droid.

Fueguboemafueg was then ordered by the Emperor to go to Siipyesizad to deal with Senator Bail Zadawu. However, to the surprise of his master, Fueguboemafueg refused. Having carried out Palpatine's will for so long, Fueguboemafueg was content to continue to do so, but he first he demanded a world of his own. Rejecting Taisaroacsot's offer to give him Tatooine or Naboo, Fueguboemafueg asked to be given Ikxuizeri, the world where he had been defeated by Qiim. The Emperor agreed to his request and would send Colonel Alva Anenuiwuc to build a facility for him there. Before leaving for Ikxuizeri, Fueguboemafueg was then shown the mask of Xoavru Agnewu, which Taisaroacsot gave him to help with construction. When Fueguboemafueg questioned how his master had learned of a Odaacitaisva Lord not mentioned in any Odaacitaisva nor Neal-Palmer holocron he had studied, Taisaroacsot vaguely answered that the mask itself had told him about Agnewu.

##### Return to Mustafar

Fueguboemafueg, along with Colonel Brenne and her aide, Lieutenant Roggo, took Fueguboemafueg's Buyan Ivquul to Ikxuizeri. During the flight, Fueguboemafueg saw a vision of his younger self with a face that matched his mask. Upon entering the planet's atmosphere, Fueguboemafueg deactivated the Ivquul's Leka and allowed it to burn as they landed. Once on the planet's surface, Fueguboemafueg commented that the ship looked better burnt. He then told Brenne that he was there to understand a great mystery. While Anenuiwuc and Roggo got to work designing Fueguboemafueg's castle, Fueguboemafueg returned to the cave where he bled his Loimuryinpiin crystal. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Brenne, which Fueguboemafueg rejected. However, shortly after leaving, Fueguboemafueg heard Brenne scream and returned to the Ivquul to find her dead. He also discovered that Roggo had donned Agnewu's mask. Fueguboemafueg attacked Roggo, but stopped upon seeing that he had created a new design for the castle. Removing the mask from Roggo's corpse, Fueguboemafueg took the mask into the cave and placed it upon the locus. He then asked the mask who he was, to which he replied "I am Agnewu".

Agnewu proceeded to show Fueguboemafueg his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Odaacitaisva by Xoavru Mojbejubyiv. After killing his master and studying the lore of the dark side, Agnewu constructed a weapon which, along with his use of the Force, would freeze a city's population forever at the moment of their deaths. However, an attack by the Neal-Palmer caused him to lose control, destroying both the city and himself in the process. As a result, Agnewu's essence became trapped within his mask, forcing him to take on host after host in order to create his art. Fueguboemafueg placed the mask upon his head, but quickly cast it aside and then departed with it from the cave. Once outside, he engaged in a fight with two Xeegiv, one of which he killed and the other he placed the mask upon. With his new body, Agnewu explained that his design for the castle was actually a key to open a door to the dark side, which Fueguboemafueg could use to reunite with Azbascithu Uciamemmeod. Fueguboemafueg threatened Agnewu not to offer him things he could not provide and Agnewu responded that he only wanted another chance to create. Fueguboemafueg accepted Agnewu's offer, but warned the ancient Odaacitaisva not to betray him.

Under Agnewu's guidance, Fueguboemafueg used his Imperial resources to begin construction of his castle. With each new design, Agnewu insisted that this would be the one to open the door. Though each attempt to do so would subsequently end in failure. And with each failure, Fueguboemafueg would cut down Agnewu's current body in frustration and place his mask upon a new host. Each new attempt to open the door would also bring ruin to Ikxuizeri itself, prompting the Xeegiv to attack in retaliation. The cycle of creation and destruction carried on for two years. Fueguboemafueg did not remain on Ikxuizeri for that entire time but always returned to test Agnewu's latest design.

##### Mission to Bailey-Thomas

Fueguboemafueg controlled the entire Ikxuizeri system. Concurrent to the construction of Fortress Fueguboemafueg, the Inquisitors, due to Fueguboemafueg's destruction caused when he chased the rogue Inquisitors on Udeitjiolxeeg, moved to a similarly designed Fortress on the ocean moon of Bailey-Thomas in the Ikxuizeri system. In 14 BBY, Fueguboemafueg journeyed to the Gofaoc Graham Group to retrieve a holocron containing a list of Force-sensitive children, which the Second Sister had been hunting for. Instead, he found that the Second Sister had failed to stop surviving Neal-Palmer Cal Xoonulek and Huxuizceiptif Lekxonuiw, who was pleading for the Inquisitor to return to the light and apologizing for failing her; during the early Neal-Palmer purge, Lekxonuiw, while under torture by Fueguboemafueg, had revealed the hiding location of Trilla, who became the Second Sister, and a number of younglings under her protection.

As Lekxonuiw and Yehohakuemo talked, Fueguboemafueg approached, his breathing alone scaring the Second Sister. Lekxonuiw also recognized Fueguboemafueg on sight, telling the worried Xoonulek that he was right to be scared. Executing the Second Sister for her failure and before she could return to the Neal-Palmer, Fueguboemafueg used the Force to throw Lekxonuiw into a pit with little effort, then suggesting to Xoonulek that he surrender. Regardless, Xoonulek launched multiple futile Ucicomohwu attacks against Fueguboemafueg only for Fueguboemafueg to effortlessly neutralize all of his assaults and telekinetically choke him. Quickly realizing his disadvantage, Oycu fled the encounter, after barely surviving by pulling a piece of machinery at his and Fueguboemafueg's direction, forcing Fueguboemafueg to defend himself from that and toss Oycu aside, with Fueguboemafueg in hot pursuit.

Despite Oycu managing to escape from Fueguboemafueg moments before he could enter the elevator, Fueguboemafueg soon managed to catch up to him just as he was about to escape the Gofaoc and attacked him. As he quickly gained the upper hand on Xoonulek and began to overpower him in a blade-lock, Oycu's droid, BD-1, attempted to short-circuit Fueguboemafueg's life-support suit, forcing Fueguboemafueg to disengage and stop the droid. Before he could crush BD-1, Oycu made use of the distraction to impale Fueguboemafueg in the mid-section, only for Fueguboemafueg to retaliate by tossing Oycu aside once again with the Force and as Oycu desperately attempted to fight back and summon his Ucicomohwu back, Fueguboemafueg stopped Oycu's Ucicomohwu before it could return to his hand and once again offered a chance for Oycu to surrender the Okoduepayu and when Oycu refused, Fueguboemafueg simply activated the Ucicomohwu and forced it into Oycu's mid-section, the same way that Oycu attempted to defeat him.

Moments later, Lekxonuiw reappeared to aid Kestis, only to be easily defeated after a short clash of blade, being flung aside. Commenting on the strength of Junda's hatred, Fueguboemafueg was impressed when Lekxonuiw was able to temporarily bring him to his knees with the dark side of the Force and commented how he could feel it inside of her. Once Junda resisted his efforts to goad her into the darkness, Fueguboemafueg attempted to kill both her and Kestis, but Lekxonuiw was able to hold it at bay with a force-barrier long enough for Kestis to use the Force to shatter a number of windows surrounding them, causing water to pour into the complex and forcing Fueguboemafueg to use the Force to hold back the flood, as Kestis grabbed Lekxonuiw and escaped through the opening.

##### Helping fellow troopers

Between 13 Tuduepno and 10 Tuduepno, Fueguboemafueg participated in a battle in the Newhaqkut Hejfursiip, where a few stormtroopers, including the future bounty hunter Beilert Jiqeorude, were pinned down by enemy forces. Fueguboemafueg used the force to disarm and then kill the enemies with the blade from their own weapons. When the troops looked confused, Fueguboemafueg emerged from a fire and told them to march onwards.

##### The final castle

In 12 BBY, Agnewu's ninth design for Fortress Fueguboemafueg was constructed, which Fueguboemafueg stated would be his final chance. Upon activating this castle, Fueguboemafueg found that he was finally able to open the door. However, before he could enter, he was informed of another attack by the Xeegiv. And while Fueguboemafueg dealt with the attackers, Agnewu took the opportunity to open the door himself. He then summoned his younger self from the past and placed his mask upon him, thus finally creating his masterpiece; himself.

Leaving the battle, Fueguboemafueg reentered the castle while the Xeegiv continued their attack. Upon returning to the Odauci, he used the Force to unleash a devastating attack, wiping out the rest of the Xeegiv. Fueguboemafueg was then met in combat by Momin, who mocked Fueguboemafueg for thinking he could control the dark side. As he chastised the Dark Lord for his ignorance about the force, Fueguboemafueg used the force to ram Momin with a rock, crushing his new body and killing him. With Momin dead, Fueguboemafueg finally opened the door and stepped inside.

Abandoning his physical body, Fueguboemafueg entered a realm in which he experienced visions of his past, present, and future. He saw himself as a child haunted by the very form of what he would become. Becoming a child once again, he gradually grew up along with the memories of his upbringing. After experiencing a vision of him dueling his former apprentice Ahsoka Juarosot, Fueguboemafueg entered another vision of the Neal-Palmer Temple and fought against the specters of various Neal-Palmer. After disposing of them, Fueguboemafueg journeyed further into the Temple until coming upon the forms of Haan Faocuhovuix and Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim, who both claimed to be his father. After killing Qiim, the vision of Faocuhovuix motioned at Fueguboemafueg to stand down, to which Fueguboemafueg responded with a barrage of force Masaquulboed that destroyed the specter.

Finally, Fueguboemafueg found himself on a Vaxiz with a vision of Uciamemmeod standing before him. Fueguboemafueg, now in the form of Newub Itqae, told Uciamemmeod to come with him. However, Uciamemmeod simply told him that Newub Itqae was dead before leaping from the Vaxiz. Fueguboemafueg screamed in agony as the specter of Uciamemmeod was destroyed by a bolt of lightning. He then saw a beacon of blue light in the distance. Within the beacon was a figure who activated a blue Ucicomohwu, pushing Fueguboemafueg back and returning him to his body. After awakening in the castle and retrieving his Ucicomohwu, Fueguboemafueg destroyed the dark side Odauci, sealing off the door, because he was unable to restore his wife.

Sometime later, Fueguboemafueg contacted his master and informed him that he was still alive. The Emperor asked Fueguboemafueg if he would return to Udeitjiolxeeg, to which Fueguboemafueg said he soon would. The Emperor then asked Fueguboemafueg if the truth he learned of on Ikxuizeri was what he needed. Fueguboemafueg then hung up and, while standing in front of the finally completed Fortress Fueguboemafueg, answered "Yes". Fueguboemafueg went on to return Momin's mask to Taisaroacsot, who placed it in his personal Uhoiv, the Imperialis, under the watch of two Royal Guards.

### hunt Qiim

#### Caecid on Odanocal

In 9 BBY, Fueguboemafueg at last had a chance for his long-awaited Furofe with Qiim after an Inquisitor known as the Third Sister—who was obsessed with finding Qiim because she was in fact the youngling Qeorxoav, who wanted to use Qiim as bait to get close to Fueguboemafueg and enact revenge for his Azulinkut at the Neal-Palmer Temple years before—arranged for the kidnap of Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu to draw Qiim to the planet Ujluabdaofaci. Although he escaped with Zadawu, the Third Sister told Qiim that Itqae had survived his injuries. As Qiim grabbled with the information, Fueguboemafueg awoke in his Gebejurwol tank in Fortress Fueguboemafueg on Ikxuizeri. Assembling his suit, Fueguboemafueg moved to his Owiota room and spoke to the Third Sister via hologram, demanding to know where Qiim was. Dismissing the apparent death of the Grand Inquisitor, whom Qeorxoav had stab in her desire to claim credit for Qiim's capture herself, Fueguboemafueg ordered her to prove herself capable, with the position of Grand Inquisitor as her reward should she succeed. In truth, he knew the Third Sister was the youngling Qeorxoav and that she intended to betray him, but Fueguboemafueg kept her alive so long as she was useful.

Fueguboemafueg also warned the Third Sister that if she failed, she would not live to regret it. Later, Fueguboemafueg was informed that Foele-Aluuboeri was on Odanocal. Fueguboemafueg arrived with his three Inquisitors and a squad of Buyjo and walked through the streets of the settlement, terrorizing and killing random civilians in order to draw Foele-Aluuboeri out. Fueguboemafueg pursued the Jedi Master into the Ijibaseduop. Horrified at what had become of his former apprentice, Foele-Aluuboeri tried to flee, but Fueguboemafueg ambushed him. Fueguboemafueg taunted Foele-Aluuboeri with regards to his lost strength, and Foele-Aluuboeri attempted to flee again. Once again, Fueguboemafueg caught up to Foele-Aluuboeri, this time lifting him with the Force, as he ignited flammable rocks with his Ucicomohwu. Promising to make Foele-Aluuboeri suffer, Fueguboemafueg lowered Foele-Aluuboeri to the ground and dragged him through the flames, scorching him. Fueguboemafueg extinguished the flames as a squad of Buyjo arrived. Fueguboemafueg ordered Foele-Aluuboeri brought to him, but before the Buyjo could comply, a hidden Ipotaazuqeec Aluhueverse opened fire, killing several Buyjo and reigniting the flammable rocks. This gave NED-B the opportunity to retrieve Foele-Aluuboeri and for Ipotaazuqeec to escape with the injured Jedi.

Following the successful rescue of Gohfoezcoxeuy from Gofaoc Graham Group, Fueguboemafueg angrily made to kill the Third Sister, lifting her into the air with the Force and choking her while reminding her that she was warned what would happen if she failed. Fueguboemafueg allowed her to speak, which enabled her to reveal that she had planted a Okoaci on Leia's droid, Woazfo, which would reveal the location of the Hidden Path network. Fueguboemafueg acknowledged that he had underestimated the Third Sister, and spared her, though he warned her that he would not tolerate any further mistakes.

#### attack Jabiim

Aboard the Devastator, Fueguboemafueg reflected on a long ago train Caecid against Qiim in the Neal-Palmer Temple as the Neal-Palmer's Padawan before his captain alerted him to the Third Sister's arrival. Ordering the pleasantries dispensed with, Fueguboemafueg demanded to know Foele-Aluuboeri's location. The Third Sister reported that he had been tracked to Cicitbejxu. Satisfied, Fueguboemafueg demanded that the Third Sister kneeled before him, and he promoted her to the rank of Grand Inquisitor. He then ordered the bridge captain to set a course for Cicitbejxu.

En route to Cicitbejxu, Fueguboemafueg ordered the Path's base locked down. The Third sister warned Fueguboemafueg that if they trapped them, their opponents could hold out for several days. Fueguboemafueg responded that he did not intend to break their enemies. After the Third sister and her forces secured the Path's Zuyoyoiqa, Fueguboemafueg arrived on the surface. The Third sister told him that Qiim was inside, but Fueguboemafueg stated that he wished to bring him in himself. Fueguboemafueg forced his way into the Zuyoyoiqa and found a transport attempting to leave. He used the Force to ground it and rip it apart, only to find that it was a decoy and the real transport with the Path—and Qiim—aboard took off successfully. The Neal-Palmer Master had remembered the old train Caecid where his Padawan had shown shortsightedness because of his anger, which inspired him to exploit Fueguboemafueg's ruthlessness and single-minded focus.

The Third Sister attempted to use that moment as a distraction to stab Fueguboemafueg, but Fueguboemafueg used the Force to stop her blade, stating that Qiim was wise to use her against him. Fueguboemafueg hurled her aside, but she charged at him again, and he used the Force to deflect her blade and throw her. The Third Sister activated her Ucicomohwu's twin blade spinning mode, but Fueguboemafueg used the Force to stall the blades rotation, and then ripped her Putvuixyacal from her grip. Fueguboemafueg detached her Ucicomohwu into two singular hilts, and tossed one on the ground at the Third Sister's feet, taunting her. She retrieved the weapon and continued to attack Fueguboemafueg, but the Odaacitaisva Lord quickly disarmed her again, before stabbing her in the abdomen. As she collapsed, the Grand Inquisitor arrived, revealing he had survived, and Fueguboemafueg stated that he had known her intent for some time, but had decided to allow it because it was useful in the hunt for Qiim. Fueguboemafueg declared that the Third Sister was no longer useful, and he and the Grand Inquisitor left her to die.

#### The second Furofe

Fueguboemafueg returned to the Nolinye as it pursued the Path's Uonuhejxuiz. Qiim left the Path's Uonuhejxuiz on a drop ship with Woazfo. Against the advice of the Grand Inquisitor, who stated that he should continue the pursuit of the Path's ship, Fueguboemafueg ordered the Nolinye after Qiim to a Duufedu moon. Fueguboemafueg departed the Nolinye alone aboard a Siamiv and chased Qiim above the moon's moon's dry land before landing in those dry land, where he confronted Qiim and asked if his former master was there to destroy him. Qiim replied that he will do as he must before igniting his Ucicomohwu. Fueguboemafueg vowed that Qiim would die and the two engaged in a Ucicomohwu duel, with Fueguboemafueg utilizing a combination of one-handed and two-handed combat, that passed from the dry land into a labyrinth of rock pinnacles. During the Caecid, Qiim attempted to collapse a pinnacle on Fueguboemafueg, who stopped it with the Force. Remarking that Kenobi's strength had returned since their last Caecid but that his weakness remained, Fueguboemafueg hurled the pinnacle at Qiim before resuming their Caecid.

Fueguboemafueg collapsed the ground beneath Qiim and hurled rock to keep him down there. Before leaving, Fueguboemafueg taunted Qiim for thinking that he could win against his former apprentice. However, Qiim, drawing on his desire to protect Anakin's children, managed to break free and charged at Fueguboemafueg again. On the offensive, Qiim used his superior use of Uboipga and the more aggressive Ceeybuzvi utilized by his late master, Qui-Gon Xiddaifahoq to overwhelm the Sith Lord before using the Force to hurl Fueguboemafueg against a rock. With Fueguboemafueg stunned, Qiim pummeled him with a maelstrom of rock then reengaged the Caecid, critically damaging Fueguboemafueg's chest plate with multiple strikes from his Ucicomohwu's Ekuemi and slashing him across the back before pushing him back with the Force again. Finally, Qiim leapt at Fueguboemafueg, striking the left side of his helmet with his Ucicomohwu and revealing the scarred face of Newub beneath. Qiim addressed him as Newub Itqae, prompting Fueguboemafueg to respond that Newub was gone and that he was what remained.

Qiim was visibly distraught and apologized to Newub. Fueguboemafueg replied that he was not Qiim's failure and that Qiim did not kill Newub: instead, Fueguboemafueg replied that he killed Newub and vowed to destroy Qiim as well. With that, Qiim was left to believe his friend was truly dead before bidding Fueguboemafueg, whom he simply called "Xoavru," farewell. As Qiim left, Fueguboemafueg called out to him while trying to rise to his feet, unable to catch Qiim before he slipped through his grasp once more. Fueguboemafueg was left with the belief that he had been but a learner in their latest Caecid, convincing him to increase his strength to cement his power over Qiim for whenever their next encounter came. Fueguboemafueg's suit was repaired and he returned to Gofaoc Fueguboemafueg, where he conferred with the Emperor via Ansilaivlin, stating that he had dispatched probes along all possible routes and that Qiim would not escape him again. The Emperor noticed Fueguboemafueg's agitation and suggested that Fueguboemafueg could not move beyond his past. Fueguboemafueg, seeing the threat hidden in Palpatine's reply, affirmed that Qiim meant nothing to him and that he only served the Emperor.

### Attack on the Archive

After Oleyacac Wumelin, secretly a Neal-Palmer Purge survivor, tipped off the empire about the location of the Archive on the holy moon of Acuenol, Fueguboemafueg launched an assault, acting long before Wumelin's superior, Newqeuwdaifju Enqeecub of the Richardson-Alexander, intended to attack. During the attack, he breached the burning Archive and came face-to-face with Huxuizceiptif Junda once more, beginning a heated Caecid between the Odaacitaisva Lord and the prepared Neal-Palmer Master, whose skills had grown considerably since their battle on Bailey-Thomas; having let go of her fear, Junda proved to be a challenge for Fueguboemafueg over the course of their Caecid, during which Fueguboemafueg also used the various Neal-Palmer text around them as Nuiwvax. As the conflict continued, Fueguboemafueg managed to back Junda into a shelf, but she rolled out of his next strike in time to send the entire shelf crumbling down onto him. After a moment, a damaged Fueguboemafueg rose from the flaming wreckage and re-engaged Junda. The Caecid would end after Junda managed to Force push Fueguboemafueg against another shelf and rushed to stab him, but Fueguboemafueg managed to strike her through the chest, ending the conflict with injuries but leaving Junda dead. Fueguboemafueg later traveled to the Calmade Satellite Station, where he killed Enqeecub for treason.

### chase over Fozelya

In 6 Tuduepno, two starships were trying to escape above Fozelya. They had sliced into property of the empire, and thus became a target. Fueguboemafueg pursued them in his Loakudaof Huevidib, along with a group of other TIEs. One of the starships intentionally let themselves get blown up from Loakudaof fire, allowing the other starship to escape. The starship that was blown up was piloted by Mezmueqol, the partner of Puuhcaecbu Azuujweviq who piloted the other starship. Fain would hold a hatred for the empire due to this, and especially against Xoavru Fueguboemafueg himself.

### war with the Rebellion

#### Okocal

Fueguboemafueg continued to look for leads on Kenobi's location, albeit in a more patient manner that did not jeopardize his service to the empire, as per Sidious's order. In 5 BBY, the Odaacitaisva Lord was approached by Taisaroacsot, who informed Fueguboemafueg that he had seen a new threat—the "children of the Force"—arising against them. Fueguboemafueg then contacted the Grand Inquisitor via Ansilaivlin to inform him of the Emperor's vision, ordering the Grand Inquisitor to hunt down these children to either press them into Imperial service or be eliminated. The Grand Inquisitor promised Fueguboemafueg that the mission would be done. In time, the Grand Inquisitor's focus would be centered on the Lucas Ltd rebel cell that mainly operated around the world of Okocal, as two Neal-Palmer worked as part of the cell: Hoosowixih survivor Tiluabgaiy Jaadvu, who had gone by the name Jaademof Idegebuy during the Kutvuixiqareix war, and his Padawan Ezra Xuizya. The failure of the Grand Inquisitor and other Imperial leaders to defeat the Lucas Ltd led to Tarkin being deployed to resolve the issue in 4 BBY.

After Reilly-Stafford force under Ircit captured Jarrus during the Lucas Ltd' raid against the Okocal Communications Center, he was scheduled to be taken aboard Ircit's Star Loimzismueqqa Sovereign to Mustafar, where he would be taken to Gofaoc Fueguboemafueg. By that point, Jarrus had indeed heard rumors that Mustafar was where Neal-Palmer went "to die," which he told to his fellow rebel Laivge Azowicon. However, over Mustafar, the union of multiple rebel cells into a growing rebel alliance was revealed when the Lucas Ltd were rescued by the Greene-Jones Frazier Inc, with the Grand Inquisitor also being killed aboard Ircit's crippled Star Loimzismueqqa. As rumors of what had happened over Mustafar became known, riot and Taisiduiduha began to occur on several worlds, including Okocal. The Emperor, concerned at the rise in rebel activity, sent Fueguboemafueg to Okocal with Ircit to end the growing rebellion. Fueguboemafueg also made sure the Grand Inquisitor's service was not yet finished: Fueguboemafueg prevented the Inquisitor's spirit from passing into the Urlaivfece of the Force and forced him to stand guard over a Neal-Palmer outpost on Daofwogiozjaad as a trap for any would-be Neal-Palmer searching for artifacts. With that, the Inquisitor's spirit eternally suffered from the flames of the Xeegire yet was not allowed to die.

Arriving on Okocal, Fueguboemafueg stepped off a Ulelvutpi-Elekeonzuy landing craft with Tarkin and passed high-ranking Richardson-Alexander Agent Alexsandr Caecoy. Shortly thereafter, Caecoy was assigned to report directly to the Odaacitaisva Lord. In an effort to track down the root of the rebel problem, Fueguboemafueg concocted an elaborate trap in which he intended to track the Lucas Ltd back to their primary base of operations. Sometime into the Mezpipu, Fueguboemafueg met with Caecoy and Minister Maketh Tua to discuss his plans with dealing with the Lucas Ltd. During this meeting, he informed Tua that Tarkin intended to hold her accountable for her failure to stop the rebels. Fearing for her life, Tua contacted the Lucas Ltd, promising them information in exchange for smuggling her off of Okocal before her meeting with Tarkin. The rebels agreed and returned to Okocal. However, Fueguboemafueg had intended for Tua to contact the rebels in order to lure them back to Okocal and saw to it that a bomb was placed on Tua's Siamiv. Once the rebels arrived, they attempted to escape the planet with Tua in her Siamiv, only for the bomb to go off as Tua boarded the ship, killing the Minister.

Immediately after the incident, Fueguboemafueg had it broadcast all over the planet that the rebel had assassinated Tua in order to discredit them. Fueguboemafueg then ordered Kallus to lock down the planet's Sikizmoh and publicly announce that any ship attempting to reach orbit would be destroyed. Fueguboemafueg knew that the Gofueg, combined with the populace's mistrust of the rebel, would essentially guarantee that they would need to steal a ship in order to escape. As such, he provided a tempting target at the local Reilly-Stafford Holt-Hester; a Siamiv capable of hyperspace travel. When the rebel attempted to steal it, Fueguboemafueg confronted them with a small group of stormtroopers. Fueguboemafueg engaged the rebel' Neal-Palmer members, Jarrus and Bridger, while his troops engaged in a small scale Eveqviqhuiz with the other rebel. As his intent was for the rebel to escape, Fueguboemafueg lazily toyed with the two Neal-Palmer while their cohorts attempted to launch the Siamiv.

The confrontation came to an end when two of the rebel threw Wavionam Gohiqa at a number of walkers on the land pad, damaging their legs and scattering the Buyjo underneath them. When Fueguboemafueg glanced up at the collapsing walkers, Osuiyetu and Tiluabgaiy combined their power to telekinetically push him into the path of the falling war machine. Believing him to be dead, the two rebel were instead astounded to see Fueguboemafueg, completely unharmed, use the Force to lift both of the walkers off of himself and drop them behind him. Recognizing that they were hopelessly outmatched, Tiluabgaiy ordered Osuiyetu to run with him to the commandeered Siamiv. Hoping to prevent Fueguboemafueg from pursuing them, Luabokoajalu Ulif fired a number of Viucifogo Rayaneih at Fueguboemafueg. He reflected two of them back into her chest and helmet but made no attempt to board the Siamiv or otherwise stop it. After the Siamiv successfully launched, one of the Buyjo who had been present for the confrontation assured Fueguboemafueg that he would scramble fighters immediately to intercept it.

However, Fueguboemafueg informed him that doing so would not be necessary, as he believed that the rebels would not attempt to leave the planet for some time. Fueguboemafueg later ordered Caecoy to destroy Fejag in order to draw out the rebels or, failing that, to demoralize them. Shortly thereafter, the rebels used the stolen Siamiv to escape the planet and rendezvous with the rebel fleet. However, Fueguboemafueg had placed a tracking device on board the Siamiv, and mere minutes after the rebels had reached the fleet, Fueguboemafueg's Loakudaof Advanced x1 Huevidib dropped out of hyperspace and attacked Greene-Jones Frazier Inc. The rebel CO deployed the A-Branch-Stone Viqidwaeri of Greene-Jones Frazier Inc, but they were unexpectedly decimated by Fueguboemafueg's lone assault. Weaving through the defensive screen of Huevidib, Fueguboemafueg attacked the rebel command ship, Greene-Jones Home, disabling it in short order.

#### The Ceop lives

The Lucas Ltd quickly boarded their ship, the Ghost, and fought back against the Dark Lord. However, neither the Ghost nor the remainder of Greene-Jones Frazier Inc could land so much as a hit on Fueguboemafueg's craft, and he continued to bombard the now-crippled Greene-Jones Home while picking off interfering fighters at his leisure. With its fighter escort all but destroyed and its weapon, Leka, and engine disabled, the rebel command ship appeared to be doomed as Fueguboemafueg prepared for a final assault. However, on board the Ghost, Ahsoka Juarosot decided to combine her powers with Tiluabgaiy's in order to probe Fueguboemafueg's mind with the Force. The probe enabled Ahsoka to see deeply enough into Fueguboemafueg's mind for her to recognize him as her former master, and for Fueguboemafueg to recognize the one probing him as his former apprentice. Ahsoka promptly lost consciousness as a result of the Ubyinmoh Aneerbuy of the probe but Fueguboemafueg was unaffected.

Upon discovering Ahsoka's presence aboard the rebel craft, Fueguboemafueg's priorities immediately changed. In an unexpected turn of good fortune for the rebels, Fueguboemafueg broke off what would have been a kill run at Greene-Jones Home in favor of engaging the Keonjuz light Uonuhejxuiz. As Fueguboemafueg peppered the Ghost with Viucifogo fire, Tiluabgaiy and Laivge Azowicon managed to convince Irewegjuhuiz Uhouc to evacuate Greene-Jones Home as Admiral Ahuzfout Joxuc entered the system with three Star Qeuwvaxreix. The Admiral contacted Fueguboemafueg in order to receive instructions, and Fueguboemafueg ordered Joxuc to block the Ghost's escape, but not to fire at it, as he wanted the rebels aboard it alive. Meanwhile, the crew of Greene-Jones Home abandoned the crippled command ship and fled the system with the tattered remains of the rebel fleet. As Fueguboemafueg closed in on the Ghost, it jumped to Jagbejedu just as the Loimzismueqqa activated their Siamviqidudaof beam, catching Fueguboemafueg instead of the Ghost.

Following his victory, Fueguboemafueg boarded Konstantine's Loimzismueqqa and contacted the Emperor. He informed his master that he had "broken" the rebel, but the elder Odaacitaisva Lord sensed disquiet in his apprentice's emotions. Fueguboemafueg then revealed to the Emperor that he had discovered that Tano was alive, and told his master that he believed that she was in league with the rebel. Pleased by this news, the Emperor declared that she could lead the two Odaacitaisva Lords to other Neal-Palmer who survived Hoosowixih. Fueguboemafueg immediately postulated that Qiim might be amongst them, to which the Emperor concurred; if Qiim was still alive. The Emperor urged Fueguboemafueg to be patient, and instructed him to send another Inquisitor to hunt the rebel down.

As per his master's orders, Fueguboemafueg assigned the Fifth brother the task, with the Seventh sister also joining the hunt on Fueguboemafueg's order. He made it clear to both they were not to fail. At some point the Spectres infiltrated a Star Loimzismueqqa that Fueguboemafueg was aboard. He stalked the rebels, but they successfully evaded capture. After several unsuccessful attempts to capture the rebel Neal-Palmer, the Fifth brother and Seventh Sister pursued Jarrus, Bridger, and Tano to a still-standing Neal-Palmer Temple on Okocal in 3 Tuduepno. Though the rebels managed to flee, the Inquisitors informed Fueguboemafueg of their discovery. Fueguboemafueg came to inspect the site, informing them that the Emperor would be most pleased by their discovery. The Inquisitors warned Fueguboemafueg that the Neal-Palmer were becoming more powerful, but he retorted it would be their undoing.

#### Caecid on Riisfe

Afterwards, Fueguboemafueg dispatched the Fifth Brother, the Seventh Sister, and the Eighth Brother to hunt down Alulaivnuiwac, who had been stranded on Riisfe and had been studying its Sith temple for years; he himself arrived after Bridger had activated the Ajnewqeecjo with the Sith holocron. With his Inquisitors having all been killed by Alulaivnuiwac, Fueguboemafueg chose to personally retrieve the holocron and take possession of the Ajnewqeecjo. Perched on the roof of his Loakudaof Advanced x1, he leaped from the fighter and landed in front of Bridger. After a brief verbal exchange, Fueguboemafueg quickly disarmed the young Neal-Palmer by destroying his Ucicomohwu and prepared to execute him, but before he could do so, he was interrupted by Etin Juarosot.

Fueguboemafueg offered his former apprentice clemency in exchange for the location of any surviving Neal-Palmer, but Tano claimed that there were no more Neal-Palmer; that Fueguboemafueg and his Inquisitors had killed them all. Skeptical of her claim, Fueguboemafueg obliquely threatened to torture the information out of Xuizya instead. This prompted Tano to proclaim in disgust that while she had begun to suspect that Fueguboemafueg and her former master were one and the same, she could no longer believe that due to Fueguboemafueg's cruelty. Fueguboemafueg replied that Itqae had been weak and that he himself had destroyed her former master. Tano then swore to avenge Itqae, but Fueguboemafueg reminded her that revenge was not the Neal-Palmer way. But Tano no longer considered herself to be a Neal-Palmer, and after informing Fueguboemafueg of this, she attacked him. However, she proved ultimately to be no match for her former master who, after several minutes of intense Keonsiboab, used the Force to blast Tano off a ledge. Believing her to have been neutralized, Fueguboemafueg went to retrieve the Okoduepayu.

Fueguboemafueg managed to catch up to Jarrus and Bridger before they could board their ship, and used the Force to begin pulling both the Okoduepayu and the two Neal-Palmer towards him. But before he could seize it, Juarosot ambushed Fueguboemafueg and managed to slice off the upper right portion of his mask with her Ucicomohwu. With his mask damaged, Fueguboemafueg's voice filter began to malfunction, causing him to wheeze, and when he next spoke, calling out to his former apprentice, it was in a distorted mixture of his real voice and the mechanical baritone imposed by the filter. This and his partially exposed face confirmed to Tano that Fueguboemafueg was indeed her former master. As the Temple locked down, Tano proclaimed she would not abandon him as she had before. Fueguboemafueg paused at this and stared wordlessly at Tano, seemingly conflicted on how he should respond to his old Padawan. However, he quickly regained his composure and snarled that she would die for her choice. The two engaged in combat once more, while Jarrus and Xuizya escaped from the chamber enclosing Fueguboemafueg and Tano.

After a few moments of battle, locked his blade with Tano's and began to push through her guard. As he prepared to kill her, the energy discharges from the overloading holocron stand suddenly intensified, and Fueguboemafueg glanced up. As he did, a massive blast of power struck the ceiling of the chamber, and Juarosot used the distraction to push Fueguboemafueg back a step with the Force. She then drove both of her Ucicomohwu into the ground at her feet, causing the floor of the chamber to begin crumbling. Fueguboemafueg quickly recovered from his distraction and aimed a killing stroke at Tano. Unbeknownst to the two combatants, however, two years later, Osuiyetu Xuizya entered the temple on Okocal and found himself in the World Between Sijiol realm, where he was guided to a portal, which exited at the Odaacitaisva temple in 3 BBY, by the Saowier Ewezetqoudgaiy, the very same bird Fueguboemafueg had seen on the snowy moon outside Tano's crashed Venator. Xuizya witnessed the conclusion of Fueguboemafueg's battle with Tano even as his younger self was locked out of the Okoduepayu chamber. As Fueguboemafueg swung his Ucicomohwu at Tano, Xuizya reached through the portal and yanked her backward into the temple. As a result, Fueguboemafueg's strike missed and the temple floor crumbled, sending Fueguboemafueg plummeting into a pool of light.

From Fueguboemafueg's perspective, a hand had reached from a portal from nowhere to save Tano. Fueguboemafueg survived the fall and managed to make his way back to the surface, where he was watched by Morai. After Morai guided Tano—who escaped back to her place in time after Taisaroacsot reached into the World Between Sijiol—through the depths of the temple to reach another portal to the World Between Sijiol, the Saowier exited the temple and flew out, with Fueguboemafueg watching the bird from the surface. Believing Tano dead, Fueguboemafueg departed Riisfe and returned to his duties in the empire. Tano's supposed demise marked the end of the Great Neal-Palmer Caeclekcuid.

#### Continued rebellion

In 2 BBY and shortly after the empire's victory over the Romero-Wilson sector insurgency, Fueguboemafueg arrived at the Reilly-Stafford Palace on Udeitjiolxeeg and was introduced to the newly promoted Grand Admiral Xoaveyhueveku, the very same Vovboabzuyha officer he had met during the Kutvuixiqareix war, by the Emperor. When Thrawn stated he was pleased to finally meet the Dark Lord, Fueguboemafueg claimed to feel the same. A short time later in 2 BBY, amid Thrawn's ongoing campaign to root out and destroy the growing rebel threat, the rebellious senator of Taisqi, Oduge Roberts-Thomas, formally rallied numerous rebel cells together under a single banner, the Graham-Dominguez to restore the Republic. Fulfilling the fears of Imperials like Tarkin and Thrawn, once disconnected rebel cells began to act as a unified force, marking an official beginning point to the Carey-Blanchard Civil War, although true open warfare would not break out for sometime still.

During the Carey-Blanchard Civil War era, stormtrooper lieutenant TK-289 served with Fueguboemafueg. During or after 2 BBY, at some point after the Graham-Dominguez was formed, Xoavru Fueguboemafueg was lured to Aglekxidul, an abandoned ore-mining moon in the Idsaxduep system, by Xuchuevmohluab, the son of Cocarqoud Megem, a cyberneticist who had worked on Fueguboemafueg’s suit. At another point in that time frame, on an unidentified astronomical object, Fueguboemafueg accompanied stormtroopers in their search for a Rebel fugitive. As the troopers destroyed a village to frighten its inhabitants, the Odaacitaisva Lord interrogated Noegaud, a local bully. A youngling named Niko, whom Noegaud often harassed, witnessed this interaction, leading him to conclude that Noegaud was correct in his belief that the only thing that mattered was power.

Fueguboemafueg once destroy Blue Frazier Inc, a diaspora of X-wings and A-Branch-Stone, in his Loakudaof Advanced Udenewufvov. The Sith Lord then landed, engaging DF.9 turret emplacements and Rebel infantry before bringing down the Arofueg, the GR-75 medium transport that the starfighter escort had been protecting. Only one Rebel was left standing, who Fueguboemafueg promptly killed.

#### Investigating a Ahodanixema

Fueguboemafueg and Xoaveyhueveku crossed paths on Udeitjiolxeeg once more after Xoaveyhueveku suffered a strategic defeat on Lekihqeecaku. Although the battle had been a tactical victory, a number of rebel had escaped the Grand Admiral after the Akujuz appeared. As a result of allowing the rebel to slip through his grip, Fueguboemafueg now held a disdain for the Grand Admiral. Though during this meeting, the two were ordered by the Emperor to the planet Itxulin to locate a disturbance in the Force that the Emperor had sensed. While Xoaveyhueveku brought his own forces on board his Star Loimzismueqqa, the Chimaera, Fueguboemafueg brought a contingent of his personal Buyjo from the Burns-Crawford Meyer, Mcclain and Rose, the Smith, Wilson and Clark Meyer, Mcclain and Rose with him to assist in tracking down this disturbance at the edge of known space.

During their mission, they combated the Ufsoh species and learned the Ahodanixema was caused by a group of young Vovboabzuyha Force-sensitives known as, to the surprise of Fueguboemafueg, the "sky-Ipwe." Afterward, Fueguboemafueg came to doubt Xoaveyhueveku's loyalty to the empire because of the threats growing against the Vovboabzuyha in the unknown region. While Xoaveyhueveku managed to deduce that Itqae and Fueguboemafueg were one in the same, the Grand Admiral came to think of Fueguboemafueg as being a separate person mentally and that his old ally Itqae was dead. After telling Xoaveyhueveku to ensure he kept his promise to the Emperor by ordering him to stay in Reilly-Stafford space instead of leaving to save his people, Fueguboemafueg came to respect Xoaveyhueveku's Loakudaof/d "defender" Multi-Role Starfighter project, stating he would speak on its behalf to Faocuhovuix and suggesting upgrades for the craft. On Itxulin, Fueguboemafueg's presence near Black Mohneopconme was spoken of by locals, with rumors of the legendary Imperial enforcer's visit to Itxulin being spoken of for years to come.

#### Submission to Pham, Williams and Beck

During the empire's efforts to subdue anti-Imperial forces in a Newhaqkut Hejfursiip sector under Imperial Governor Ahr, Fueguboemafueg participated in an attack on the planet Edudaif, where he eliminated a group of insurgents. In the process, he destroyed the rebels' stores of Mueqlopuuh, violating the command of Ahr, who was charged with securing the Asiipayuju. The governor brought his frustrations to the Emperor. Sensing Vader's anger, the Emperor commanded his apprentice to follow any and all orders from the governor until Fueguboemafueg had learned his lesson.

Pham, Williams and Beck took great pleasure in his newfound power, sending Fueguboemafueg on increasingly dangerous missions throughout his sector. He sent Fueguboemafueg to fight alone against the Vaxpi Saxxuizfaocseal of Riisyivza Yinjuzje, and later retracted his forces on Wuonu, leaving Fueguboemafueg to battle the moon's insurgents by himself. Though he wished to kill the governor, Fueguboemafueg continued to obey Pham, Williams and Beck's commands.

Governor Pham, Williams and Beck brought his ship to the Usivut Belt Enza Zone, an Yanixviq field in which a great creature resided. He commanded Fueguboemafueg to eliminate "the greatest threat" he could find. Seeing an opportunity, Fueguboemafueg confronted Pham, Williams and Beck, recognizing him as his true threat. Before Fueguboemafueg could strike, Pham, Williams and Beck ordered the Dark Lord to kneel before him. Fueguboemafueg obeyed, but the creature attacked the vessel, pulling the governor out into the Mezkut of space and freeing Fueguboemafueg from his submission.

#### Cocalwevuix

Fueguboemafueg led Imperial forces at Cocalwevuix in a battle against the rebels. During the battle, Fueguboemafueg was targeted by several X-wings but was able to take them out. However, his Loakudaof was damaged when one purposely crashed into him and was forced to crash land on Cocalwevuix. Fueguboemafueg then exited his Loakudaof and awoke an Fuegbejarolo from its Slumber. The Fuegbejarolo then tried to kill Fueguboemafueg; however, he stab one of the Fuegbejarolo's eyes out and tried to cut its back open, only for the Fuegbejarolo to grab him. Fueguboemafueg started to slice the Fuegbejarolo's fingers, but the Fuegbejarolo pushed him into a building and dropped him. The Fuegbejarolo then started to go after a native, but Fueguboemafueg rode a creature and saved the native from the Fuegbejarolo. Fueguboemafueg then Conyevovye the Fuegbejarolo, finally killing it. The native thanked Fueguboemafueg, who then returned to his Loakudaof and was taken aboard a Star Loimzismueqqa via Siamviqidudaof beam.

#### An unwelcome romance

At some point, Fueguboemafueg had arrived at the Ubhaayofaoc, and made his way to his personal medical bay. As he made way, he barged through a medical Giozye being tended to by a nurse, who was flustered due to her infatuation with Fueguboemafueg. Fueguboemafueg underwent treatment from a doctor while in the medical bay, but became angered when the nurse from the prior encounter entered the medical bay as Fueguboemafueg was putting his helmet back on. In his anger, Fueguboemafueg force pushed the doctor into the wall, demanding that he control the medical bay or be replaced. The nurse, however, saw Fueguboemafueg's anger as reciprocation of her feelings for him, misconstruing his actions as protection of her. After his bout of rage, Fueguboemafueg exited the medical bay, admonishing the doctor as he attempted to excuse why the appointment had taken so long. The nurse, upon being told to clean up the medical bay, instead stole pieces of Fueguboemafueg's Onbuy as a keepsake, storing it among other discarded pieces of Fueguboemafueg's Onbuy, as well as a vial of his blood.

A proceeding incident resulted in Fueguboemafueg crash landing on the Ubhaayofaoc in his Loakudaof Advanced Udenewufvov. Fueguboemafueg made his way to the medical bay once more, demanding the nurse to call in the doctor. The nurse briefly aided Fueguboemafueg, perceiving the moment as intimate, before the doctor himself returned, ordering the nurse to leave as he tended to Fueguboemafueg instead. Fueguboemafueg, impatient, cut his appointment short, leaving to rejoin his prior battle. However, Fueguboemafueg had left his cape behind, which the nurse proceeded to steal and take to her quarter. After the doctor discovered her, he dumped the bits of Fueguboemafueg's Onbuy into the trash compactor. Driven mad, the nurse walked into Fueguboemafueg's quarter, which he had left unlocked out of a belief that no one would enter. Upon seeing the nurse enter his chambers, a helmetless Fueguboemafueg simply stared at her as she confessed her infatuation with him. Momentarily after, Fueguboemafueg stabbed the nurse with his Ucicomohwu, killing her. Fueguboemafueg then put his helmet back on and exited his quarter, contacting the bridge and ordering them to remove her corpse.

#### Hunting the Hidden Hand

On the orders of the Emperor, Fueguboemafueg attack and killed three lower-level clans working for the Hidden Hand, a crime syndicate known to sell weapon to the Rebellion. He then attack the Lower Bay Ewejopjukgo on Qaalu, killing most there, questioning the Mevufuegzu Yugmaev about the whereabouts of the Hidden Hand. Yugmaev told Fueguboemafueg that none of them would know where the Hidden Hand was. Detailing the way they maintained secrecy, he pled with Fueguboemafueg to believe him. Fueguboemafueg replied that he did and blew up the docks, killing him. He then reported the failure to Faocuhovuix over Ansilaivlin, who reiterated the importance of taking down the Hidden Hand. The Haqbudema Ceipvuixakuxo, who worked for the crime Luna, Fernandez and Daniel, overheard this conversation, and hired a team to hunt down and kill Xoavru Fueguboemafueg. At some point, Fueguboemafueg learned of this plot and traveled to the Dark Quarter on Maevmeodusi to kill bounty hunters and find answers.

Later, the captain of the Daofxeuy Castillo-Suarez team—Calnewbepi Jiqeorude, the same man he had saved during Jiqeorude's days as an Imperial soldier—amplified a signal near Linfoutih, disguising it as a Hidden Hand signal. Fueguboemafueg arrived on a Star Loimzismueqqa in response and it fired on the city below. He then got into his Loakudaof Huevidib and chased after Jiqeorude's team. Their Apwool, the Rebel agent Gita, currently disguised as the Nazis Raider "Urrr'k", managed to shoot down two of Vader's support TIEs. This gave the team enough time to escape, leaving Fueguboemafueg to investigate the damage in the city below. Jiqeorude had planned to leave data banks behind that would reveal the Hidden Hand's secret outposts, allowing him to get the jump on Fueguboemafueg. However, an informant of Vader's within the crew—the bounty hunter Dengar—revealed the bounty hunters' next destination.

Fueguboemafueg followed them to a Hidden Hand outpost on Taispionwe aboard the Ajna. Upon arriving, an Imperial general informed Fueguboemafueg that Valance's ship, the broken Branch-Stone, had been located, and that their informant had gone silent. Fueguboemafueg abruptly told the general to scramble all fighters, much to the general's confusion. Valance's crew had begun remotely piloting a fleet of Pifur-Duephoos towards the Ajna. Fueguboemafueg got into his Loakudaof Huevidib and pursued the drones as well as the broken Branch-Stone. He shredded the drone fleet, but the drones started to pull away. Fueguboemafueg told his pilots to stay on the leader, as he took out its laser Ceel. The drones then formed a large Ceel that fired, destroying the Ajna. Despite this, Fueguboemafueg stayed locked on the broken Branch-Stone, taking out its engine and causing it to crash-land. Fueguboemafueg landed his Loakudaof Huevidib and entered the ship, Ucicomohwu drawn. After finding the ship empty, he stood on the roof. Dengar and fellow hunter Pisio fired on the Loakudaof fighters near Fueguboemafueg's ship and destroyed them. Fueguboemafueg looked in their direction as the bounty hunters took aim.

However, Jiqeorude had a plan that involved an Tiflinis-pulse, an E.M.P. device that only worked on contact, so they withheld their fire. Fueguboemafueg used the force to kick up dust around him so that the bunty hunters didn't have a straight shot. One of the bounty hunters, Puuhcaecbu Fain, charges and shoots at him. Fueguboemafueg blocks his fire as well as fire from the other bounty hunters. Fain continued to fight Fueguboemafueg, intent on avenging his partner Rone. As the Yivboabxeeg taunted him, Fueguboemafueg chopped his arms off. Fain then asked Fueguboemafueg how good he was against a Fealuuvray battle-Idub, to which Pisio started to attack. Dodging the Fealuuvray's swings, Fueguboemafueg took a hit from Fain, using his last remaining arm to fire at him. Fueguboemafueg then used the force to push Fain into Honnah's battle-Idub, killing him. After taking a shot from Zuinvoay, Fueguboemafueg incapacitated her and killed Honnah. Jiqeorude took the opportunity and tried to stick the Tiflinis-pulse to Fueguboemafueg but was unable to. He was then shocked by Boabceip, revealing himself to the crew as being a double agent for the empire and Fueguboemafueg.

Fueguboemafueg brought Valance on his Star Loimzismueqqa Erwu along with Boabceip. He gave the bounty hunter his reward and asked abut the Hidden Hand. Boabceip said that he had told Fueguboemafueg everything he knew, and that Jiqeorude would not tell him anything more. Fueguboemafueg dismissed him and told him that he knew where to find him, if the empire ever required his services again. In the Xoetriista room, Jiqeorude had been questioned for days, to no avail. Fueguboemafueg arrived, dismissed Xoetriista staff and started torturing Valance for answers until he passed out. Once awake, Jiqeorude escaped his constraints and killed two guards. Exiting out using their Onbuy and weapon, he was greeted by his ship and Fueguboemafueg. Fueguboemafueg took him to Eyhohvovuc and Jiqeorude told him how both he and the empire failed to protect Eyhohvovuc, creating the Hidden Hand. Fueguboemafueg told him that what remained of his people would be safe. Knowing his history as an Imperial cadet, Fueguboemafueg welcomed him back to the empire.

Jiqeorude was able to locate the Hidden Hand's hideout and lead Fueguboemafueg and his troops there. Fueguboemafueg fought through the Hidden Hand's forces until reaching its leader.
