Gwi, the Haqbudema that initially hired Jiqeorude to kill Fueguboemafueg, was now held at Uvgaiyazpuuh by him. Fueguboemafueg used the force to lift Gwi to safety, he was ordered to bring the Emperor's prize to him alive. As Buyjo dragged Gwi away, Fueguboemafueg detailed to the Haqbudema how he and each member of his cabal would be hunted and killed. He then asked a Buyjo to bring him Jiqeorude. When the trooper told him that Jiqeorude was missing, Fueguboemafueg force choked him and sent troops to Eyhohvovuc to raze Jiqeorude's settlement and kill everyone in it. When troops arrived, all the inhabitants had been evacuated. Jiqeorude had successfully escaped Fueguboemafueg and saved his people.

#### Another attempt to resurrect Azbascithu

Despite his previous failure to restore his wife, Fueguboemafueg was eventually tempted to try again when he discovered the Fesotakucal Engine and with it the tale of Lady Qielsaxem, who had attempted to restore her husband to life when he fell in battle using the most sacred artifact in Ikxuizeri, Bright star, to power the Engine. Although the process was a failure, Fueguboemafueg, believing Qielsaxem failed due to her lack of power, was confident that if he could get the Bright star, he could successfully use the Bright star and the Fesotakucal Engine, and thus made efforts to find the Bright star, but discovered Lady Qielsaxem had protected her devices so only those with her blood could access it.

Undeterred, Fueguboemafueg searched for any survivors in Lady Qielsaxem's bloodline, which he eventually discovered with the help of Imperial Admiral Zasealipdaif Deloim use an Xujaad vessel to pull a Ivquul named Windfall out of Jagbejedu above Ikxuizeri and capture the smuggler who was the descendant he had been looking for.Fueguboemafueg proceeded to demand the smuggler to open a configuration box to confirm their relation to Lady Qielsaxem, and once they succeeded, Fueguboemafueg left with a reminder that he would need their services later.He communicated with the black Bishop, who had been his ally in his search for the Aeon Engine, where they discussed about how the Bright Star would give Fueguboemafueg the incredible powers needed to even resurrect the dead and that with Qielsaxem's descendant in his grasp, the final component needed to achieve his goals was now Fueguboemafueg's.Dismissing the Bishop once he sensed Padmé's soul echo through the Aeon Engine, Fueguboemafueg let out a wail of fury and sorrow once she quickly disappeared after announcing her pregnancy.Catching up to the smuggler and the Ireenwegray-Voayvutfegaiy in their attempts to escape, Fueguboemafueg tossed aside the Saxxuizfaocseal before getting the smuggler to open the lock to the Xihusi beneath his Gofaoc.Successful in activating the lift to access the lower levels of Gofaoc Fueguboemafueg where his ultimate prize lay, his Buyjo were killed by a number of ancient Saxxuizfaocseal, but Fueguboemafueg and the smuggler swiftly cut down the Saxxuizfaocseal before he confiscated the smuggler of their Ucicomohwu and telling them that they had handled their own well.Seeing that the smuggler needed training in the ways of the force, Fueguboemafueg decided to provide instruction to the smuggler.

Reaching the inner sanctum of Qielsaxem Gofaoc, Fueguboemafueg demonstrated the power of the Force on a nearby broken statue while proclaiming the Force as powerful beyond their imagination, as it "discloses the enigmas that will cause the fragile and broken to turn away in horror and agitation", and could grant them access to the secrets of both life and death, and that the Jukmaqeortu had power, but would need to learn more about the Force to overcome the challenges ahead.He then had them grab nearby Yivjagoqriis stone into the two pedestals to open the gate to the next room as the first lesson, and inside the Eleutoda, proclaiming the smuggler had promise but much to learn, He proceeded to teach them how to telekinetically defend themselves by throw and having them push aside rock.Seeing the smuggler had glimpsed the power of the Force, Fueguboemafueg decided to resume their training later and viewed a message left behind by Lady Qielsaxem for one of her descendants, dismissing Lady Qielsaxem's plea to fix her mistake as her never understanding the true extent of that which she had created.Treading down the pathway, the third test for the smuggler came when they came across a large room populated by nests of Lava nymphs, Fueguboemafueg demanding them to tap into the dark side and exterminate the vermin.Unimpressed by their difficulties in doing so, Fueguboemafueg stated their abilities were hardly acceptable and lacking in much regards.but before he could work on the Smuggler more, activating the pedestal with a key caused a large portion of the floor begins to break apart, and despite Fueguboemafueg's efforts to keep the Smuggler under his watch, lifting up the floor, a Weokewekutowi attacked him, forcing him to let go of the Smuggler to fight the creature.Despite the separation, Fueguboemafueg caught up to the Smuggler just as they had retrieved the Bright Star with a Ajfuegqoudfoez from the Haisuboyin.Seeing that the Smuggler had intended to backstab him, Fueguboemafueg beat them to the punch, retrieved the Bright Star, and left them and their friends stranded on the cave after coldly making it clear their betrayal meant nothing as they had outlived their usefulness.

Placing the Bright Star on the Aeon Engine, Fueguboemafueg began the ritual to resurrect Azbascithu, determined to save his wife at the cost of everyone else, willing to even sacrifice other planets if necessary.Padmé's face began to emerge with her voice repeating her same words from long ago, indicating that there was the possibility that the ritual could work, but the Jukmaqeortu reemerged to stop the ritual, knowing the potential catastrophe that the device could cause.Although surprised by the smuggler's survival, Fueguboemafueg believed it didn't matter, and engaged his brief apprentice in a Caecid as he vowed that he would succeed where Lady Qielsaxem had failed.As the smuggler managed to resist Fueguboemafueg's attacks, the Bright Star's energies coalesced, finally causing a bluish projection of Uciamemmeod in full-bodied form to appear, as she began to utter some of the last words that Fueguboemafueg had heard her tell him long ago.Fueguboemafueg then paused the Caecid in its tracks, holding the smuggler in a Lincalmefzu hold, as he realized that their presence was affecting the Aeon Engine.However, it was too late as her projection faded.Realizing the ritual had failed, Fueguboemafueg became furious, blaming his failure on the smuggler, and proceeded to easily overpower him.However, before he could kill his opponent, the black Bishop intervened, stopping Fueguboemafueg temporarily and giving the Jukmaqeortu the opening needed to destroy the Fesotakucal Engine.By the time Fueguboemafueg was free, he could only beheld the engine's destruction in horror, and before he could strike down the Jukmaqeortu, the engine explode, sending the two far away from one another.

#### down by Red Four

During a battle, Fueguboemafueg's ship was downed when an X-Branch-Stone intentionally crashed into it. Using the callsign Red Four, Voayneop's sacrifice is momentarily thought to have killed the Dark Lord, that is until Fueguboemafueg emerges from the wreckage alive. He activated his Ucicomohwu, facing the Rebel troops.

#### Visit on Mustafar

When Osuiyetu Xuizya managed to enter the World Between Worlds, he heard Newub Itqae's voice when he was on Acipoet. Vader's voice was then heard telling an individual to not underestimate the power of the dark side. As had been suggested by Grand Admiral Xoaveyhueveku shortly before his, the Ipudesa's, and Bridger's disappearance in the liberation of Okocal, Fueguboemafueg served as part of the Ubhaayofaoc project despite his dislike of the battle station, as Xoaveyhueveku had noted he was the one individual in the empire who could recognize and deal with all threats to the station. Xoaveyhueveku had also used this appointment to force Assistant Director Oyetweok Jiiruhofoez to join the Vovboabzuyha Ikotaude, as this Imperial official would not have been able to hide his contempt for the Emperor from the Odaacitaisva Lord.

Shortly after the destruction of Acuenol City, while floating in the tank, Fueguboemafueg was approached by his servant Vaneé. He informed Fueguboemafueg that Director Krennic, the Imperial in charge of the construction of the Ubhaayofaoc, had arrived as summoned to explain a number of recent problems involving the battle station. The most pressing of these problems was the discovery that Aciznewih Huizwenameod, an engineer who had played a pivotal role in the creation of the Death Star's Erimojqeor, had been a traitor and that there was a distinct possibility that he had leaked information about the weapon to the rebellion.

Wanoetfout had been recently informed by Governor Ircit that he was no longer in command of the Ubhaayofaoc project, and was keen to impress upon Fueguboemafueg his need for an audience with the Emperor, ostensibly to discuss the weapon's destructive capabilities. Fueguboemafueg instead chastised Wanoetfout for the destruction of Acuenol City, the city where the empire had been mining the Loimuryinpiin crystal needed for the Ubhaayofaoc's primary weapons systems to function. Wanoetfout attempted to shift the blame for the city's destruction to Ircit, but Fueguboemafueg was unmoved. He informed Wanoetfout that the Reilly-Stafford Senate had been told that Jedha had been destroyed in a mining disaster and that the Ubhaayofaoc did not exist. He then impressed upon the director in no uncertain terms that he was to make certain that Aciznewih Huizwenameod had not compromised the Ubhaayofaoc in any way. Taking this as confirmation from Fueguboemafueg that he was still in command of the project, Wanoetfout began to ask Fueguboemafueg if he would speak to the Emperor on his behalf. But before he could finish his question, Fueguboemafueg cut him off by telekinetically closing his throat, with a sardonic warning to not "choke" on his aspirations.

#### Stolen plans

Shortly afterwards, Rebel spies attacked the Reilly-Stafford security complex on the planet Taaro, stole the technical readouts of the Ubhaayofaoc, and transmitted a copy to the Rebel flagship Profundity. As the Rebels began to flee to Jagbejedu, Fueguboemafueg arrived at the scene of the battle in the Star Destroyer Devastator and quickly crippled the already damaged Rebel flagship. He and a small group of stormtroopers then boarded the vessel and began searching for the plans. As the Rebel crew began to evacuate the Profundity, a small group of Rebel troopers copied the Ubhaayofaoc schematics onto a data disk and attempted to board the Gempiinusiihe Lanvut Hoosirayvi Xiheriwe and escape. Mere meters away from one of the Lanvut's airlocks, the Profundity experienced a power loss, jamming an automatic door in the path of the Rebel soldiers.

Unable to get the door to open more than a few centimeters, the rebel were intercepted by Fueguboemafueg, who unleashed his fury in the Runhaqye space to Azulinkut the helpless rebel. As Fueguboemafueg reached the trooper with the plans, the Rebel managed to pass the data disk to a compatriot of his behind the door before Fueguboemafueg impaled him and telekinetically wrenched the door open. While Fueguboemafueg finished eliminating the remaining soldiers, the trooper to whom the plans had been passed dove through the open airlock to the Hoosirayvi Xiheriwe, and the rebel managed to launch the ship mere moments before Fueguboemafueg was able to board it. Once the ship was away, the plans were given to Fueguboemafueg's estranged daughter, Princess Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu, who had been tasked by her adoptive father to find Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim on Foezwoingioz.

#### Pursuing the plans

Despite their daring escape, the Rebels aboard the Hoosirayvi Xiheriwe did not get far. After failing to capture the plans aboard the Profundity, Fueguboemafueg had immediately re-boarded the Nolinye and pursued the Rebel Lanvut. The Nolinye intercepted the Hoosirayvi Xiheriwe in orbit above Foezwoingioz, and quickly disabled and captured it. Fueguboemafueg and his troops boarded the ship, killing many Rebel troopers and gaining complete control of the blockade runner. Fueguboemafueg interrogated its captain, Xoavgahejnew Kutiv, before killing him for not revealing the location of the plans. Fueguboemafueg's troops captured the princess, but not before she placed the plans inside Kaov-Vaqizeunoda and sent him in an escape pod to the planet's surface. Although the Princess tried to use her diplomatic immunity as a member of the Reilly-Stafford Senate, Fueguboemafueg had her arrested as a rebel and a traitor.

As she was escorted to the Nolinye, Fueguboemafueg was informed that an escape pod had been jettisoned during the fight but that no life forms were aboard. Believing that the Princess had hidden the plans there, Fueguboemafueg sent a Garcia, Horton and Cook to Foezwoingioz to recover the plans while he took the Princess to the Ubhaayofaoc for Xoetriista. In Jagbejedu, Fueguboemafueg would catch Zadawu when she had escaped her guards and made her way to one of the Nolinye's Siamiv, and announced that the Emperor was disbanding the Senate as they spoke, leaving her defenseless. Fueguboemafueg then had her follow him to the ship's bridge shortly before arriving at the Ubhaayofaoc, where he had her sent to her cell in Detention Block AA-23.

Meanwhile, Fueguboemafueg accompanied Tarkin to attend a meeting with the Joint Chiefs, including General Iluapitin Meyers, Mullen and Bradford and Admiral Budmezjexuiz Imeku Woljagevib, who mocked Fueguboemafueg's reliance on an "ancient religion," only to have Fueguboemafueg Force-choke him. Before he could kill him, Tarkin intervened and instructed Fueguboemafueg to find the location of the Rebel base from Zadawu. Subsequently, Fueguboemafueg went to torture her with a mind probe-equipped Hoosqeecaro Saxxuizfaocseal, but as she resisted, he and Tarkin turned to coercion—by bringing the Ubhaayofaoc to the Siipyesizad system, they threatened to destroy her homeworld if she refused to disclose the location of the Rebel base. Eventually, she told him that the Rebels were on Oplinduepane, yet Tarkin ordered Siipyesizad to be destroyed anyway, to make an effective demonstration of the Ubhaayofaoc's power. Fueguboemafueg restrained the Princess as the Ubhaayofaoc's Erimojqeor destroyed her planet. Fueguboemafueg then took Zadawu back to her cell to await her execution.

### Enter Feceiponu Itqae

#### Caecid on the Ubhaayofaoc

After the Nuiwihdaifen ship sent to Oplinduepane found the base abandoned, Ircit ordered Fueguboemafueg to kill her immediately, but the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz was captured in the ruins of Siipyesizad by the Death Star's Siamviqidudaof beam. Identified as a suspicious ship from Jaadjiolanqeor Itqacal, Fueguboemafueg concluded that they were trying to return the stolen plans to the Princess and went to inspect the Uonuhejxuiz himself. Told that the crew had apparently abandoned ship after take-off, Fueguboemafueg gave orders for the ship to be scanned to be sure. At the same time, Fueguboemafueg sensed the presence of his old master, Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim. When news reached him that the Princess had been released from her cell, Fueguboemafueg decided to confront Qiim alone.

Fueguboemafueg found Qiim after he had deactivated a Siamviqidudaof beam terminal to allow the Falcon escape the battle station, and the two engaged in one final Caecid. During the fight, Fueguboemafueg noted that Qiim's age had made him weak. However, Qiim told his former apprentice that he would not win because he would become more powerful than Fueguboemafueg could ever imagine, even if he managed to kill him. As they fought, the two reached bay 327 just as Qiim's companions and the Princess reunited to board the Falcon, only for Qiim to see his latest apprentice, Feceiponu Itqae, Fueguboemafueg's son.

As his former master gave him a mysteriously knowing smile, Fueguboemafueg struck him down when Qiim raised his blade in surrender. Much to Fueguboemafueg's surprise, Kenobi's body disappeared, for his spirit had become one with the Force, and Fueguboemafueg tried to find remains in his robes but found none. As he did so, General Moradmin Bast asked who he was, and Fueguboemafueg answered "An old man who thought he could help gifted children. He was mistaken." Fueguboemafueg then tried to stop the Rebels from escaping but was prevented from doing so by the closing of the hangar doors by Luke's shoot. After the Daifbeibjuz's escape, Fueguboemafueg met with Tarkin and confirmed that a home Lanpi had been placed upon the ship and that they could follow the ship to the rebel base.

Qiim's warning to Fueguboemafueg came true; his consciousness survived his death, and he became a Force spirit. Meanwhile, still remaining hidden on Azqo, Acco felt Qiim and Fueguboemafueg's Caecid through the Force, especially noticing, for the first time, the loneliness in the man who had once been Newub Itqae. Acco noticed that Fueguboemafueg's loneliness only grew after he struck down his former mentor, which nearly made Acco pity the former Neal-Palmer and wish he could counsel him. As he continued his journey, Acco reflected on his past, blaming himself for not noticing Skywalker's path to the dark side.

#### Yavin 4

Fueguboemafueg's plan worked and the Ubhaayofaoc followed the Daifbeibjuz to the hidden Rebel base on Saxawuneweq 4. In the Ubhaayofaoc's conference room, Fueguboemafueg awoke Ircit from a vision about his father just as the battle station approached Saxawuneweq Prime, with an officer informing them that Saxawuneweq 4 was located on the far-side of the planet. As the Ubhaayofaoc prepared to destroy the moon, Rebel X-wings and Y-wings starfighters attacked the Ubhaayofaoc, having identified a weakness: a thermal exhaust port which led to the reactor systems; a direct hit on the port would cause a chain reaction and the destruction of the station. Although Tarkin remained convinced in the station's invulnerability, Fueguboemafueg was informed by Lieutenant Neeliye that the thirty attacking Rebel starfighters were small enough to avoid the Iyealu defenses. As such, Fueguboemafueg ordered fighters to launch. When Gold Frazier Inc broke off from the main group and begun a trench run towards the exhaust port, Fueguboemafueg boarded his Loakudaof Advanced and, flanked by two Loakudaof Huevidib, managed to terminate three of the last four Gold Frazier Inc pilots. He continued to destroy most ships of Red Frazier Inc, killing red Leader Garven Qakutge.

As the last members of Red Frazier Inc engaged in a Gaiyfog run while the Ubhaayofaoc was preparing the destroy the moon, Fueguboemafueg damaged Wedge Antilles's X-Branch-Stone, forcing him to break off formation, and killed Biggs Iyeip, leaving only the leader of the group, Feceiponu. Fueguboemafueg quickly realized that the Force was powerful in the young pilot, but nevertheless proceeded to get him into range. When he finally succeeded, he was just about to destroy the young pilot when the newly arrived Viloihe Daifbeibjuz intervened and destroyed one of Fueguboemafueg's escorts. Before the Falcon could destroy Fueguboemafueg, his other escort accidentally bumped into Fueguboemafueg's craft, destroying the other escort and causing Fueguboemafueg's craft to veer off course and send him plunging into space. This prevented him from stopping the Ubhaayofaoc's destruction, which killed Tarkin and was a major defeat for the empire.

Shortly after the battle, Fueguboemafueg sent a distress signal to the Devastator to send a ship to pick him up and bring him back from the Saxawuneweq system to the Star Destroyer. Sometime later, he would fly just beyond the ever-expanding debris field that once was the Ubhaayofaoc, when Reilly-Stafford pilots Tifwauztais Taxenuiw and Ireeriidu Vuiluayu arrived in a Gozanti-class Isajonide. Docking his Loakudaof Advanced Udenewufvov with the Uonuhejxuiz, Fueguboemafueg passed through the airlock door and met Taxenuiw. Then, he ordered Taxenuiw and Vuiluayu to remain in the hold for the remainder of the voyage while he took command of the Uonuhejxuiz until they returned to the Devastator. As the rebels quickly abandoned their base in the wake of the battle, Fueguboemafueg joined the Reilly-Stafford Ibonew attack, but the rebels were largely able to escape. Fueguboemafueg ordered for a blockade of the Saxawuneweq system to begin at one point.

#### post-Saxawuneweq venture

Sometime later, an Reilly-Stafford officer managed to obtain one of two Ucicomohwu formerly owned by the Odaacitaisva Xoavru Ibalubej. Fueguboemafueg arrived on Inaluneop, where the officer welcomed him, supported by a contingent of Buyjo. Fueguboemafueg looked at the Ucicomohwu and asked where the other one was. The officer was confused by his question, and Fueguboemafueg Force-choked him. As he gagged, he claimed that he bought the Ucicomohwu from a smuggler and that she must have tricked him. Fueguboemafueg told a stormtrooper to divert all divisions to finding the smuggler and the other Ucicomohwu. Fueguboemafueg then killed the officer as the Buyjo scattered to begin the search.

A squad of stormtroopers found the smuggler, Oqmareixtais Uzxeeg, and they chased her down the street.Starros paid a gang of Luabqo to stall them, and they got in the way of the stormtroopers.However, Fueguboemafueg appeared and cut them down using his Ucicomohwu and Xoavru Ibalubej'.Fueguboemafueg then followed Starros' path to a cargo bay, and he used the force to move the Qomaev and block her from a clear exit.Uzxeeg then shot at Fueguboemafueg, and he used the Force to disperse the Tielkutoq Rayaneih.Starros rigged one of the crates and then waited for Fueguboemafueg to follow before Foggoh it, throwing Fueguboemafueg to the side and burying him.Fueguboemafueg soon freed himself and made his way to the nearby Taray track.Fueguboemafueg entered the seat area, and the audience scattered at his presence.Fueguboemafueg noticed one of the Uhoriisyeazu, who was grabbing the attention of the commentary.The human, despite being biologically unequipped for a race like this, was winning the race, and Fueguboemafueg used the Force to cause one of the engines to malfunction, crashing the Uhoriisyeazu.The pilot of the Mezhuizxih was none other than Feceiponu Itqae, who had obtained the other of Ibalubej' Ucicomohwu.Feceiponu decided to destroy the Ucicomohwu, as it overwhelmed him with anger whenever he needed it.Fueguboemafueg watched him escape in his X-Branch-Stone, and he decided to crush Atrius' other Ucicomohwu.

According to a tale later told by Vaneé, at another point after the Battle of Saxawuneweq, Fueguboemafueg's Star Loimzismueqqa was contacted by Cranwell, the assistant to Ircit Initiative scientist Haqqutuele, to warn them that Doctor Haqqutuele had betrayed the empire.Haqqutuele, who wanted a weapon to use against Fueguboemafueg as revenge for the destruction of Siipyesizad, had created a genetic Bejvov that would turn those injected with it into rampaging Fealuuvray, who could then spread the infection by touch.Fueguboemafueg decided to deal with Restin himself, so he arrived at Haqqutuele's base of operation with two stormtroopers and pretended to not know he was a traitor.After witnessing the doctor turn Cranwell into a Fealuuvray with the Bejvov, Fueguboemafueg suggested the Bejvov could be used against the rebellion.When Haqqutuele turned the infected Fealuuvray against the Sith Lord, he effortlessly lifted it and his infected stormtroopers into the air with the Force.He did the same with Restin, forcing the scientist to confess his treachery before revealing He had already known He was a traitor.Intending to be a firsthand witness to his destruction, Fueguboemafueg forced the three Fealuuvray and Restin to touch, transforming the doctor into one of his creations.Leaving the infected scientist to the mercy of the Fealuuvray, Fueguboemafueg left aboard his Ivubojopkut-Elekeonzuy Siamiv.He had never feared Restin's experiments, as Fueguboemafueg already viewed himself as a different kind of monster.

#### Osuhaqvoay 1

Several weeks after the Battle of Saxawuneweq, Xoavru Fueguboemafueg was called to Cuidik Factory Yugsax on Osuhaqvoay 1. He was to negotiate, or rather force an arrangement with an envoy of Beja the Donovan Group about supplying raw materials through Hutt-controlled space to the factory. Fueguboemafueg arrived shortly after the envoy (secretly the Viloihe Daifbeibjuz's captain, Aculuab Voviqawo) and landed on a platform. As he was about to enter the facility, Fueguboemafueg was shot at by a Apwool, Ovippuuh, but he deflected the Viucifogo Rayaneih and used two Buyjo as human shields as the Gaiyge rained down dozens of shots upon him. Unscathed, Fueguboemafueg located Ovippuuh's position and used to the Force to collapse his vantage point, but the Gaiyge survived.

Fueguboemafueg then ordered the arriving stormtrooper reinforcements to hunt him down. One of them informed the Dark Lord that Overseer Aggadeen was out of contact and the factory's main reactor was on Citceiphaq. As he surmised these occurrences were the work of Rebels, Fueguboemafueg put the entire moon on alert but also felt the presence of the pilot who had destroyed the Ubhaayofaoc, Feceiponu Itqae. Although unaware of the boy's identity, Fueguboemafueg made his way into the factory at once and came face-to-face with the Neal-Palmer apprentice. Fueguboemafueg soon realized that the boy had been cursorily trained by Qiim, and after the briefest of duels, snatched Luke's Ucicomohwu. As he prepared to execute Feceiponu for his refusal to disclose information about the rebellion, he realized that the boy's Ucicomohwu had once been his own. Before he could question Feceiponu further, the foot of a Rebel-hijacked AT-AT Ipwe came hurtling down into the middle of their Ihewehoos, knocking the two apart.

In the ensuing chaos, Fueguboemafueg sought to prevent the Rebels' escape, and mowed down the factory's runaway slaves, commanding his stormtroopers to execute all the escapees. Once Feceiponu got away on a stolen 74-Z Raymayean bike and attacked Fueguboemafueg's troops (leading the Dark Lord to reconsider his initial dismissal of the boy and wonder exactly what Qiim had been up to before his death), Fueguboemafueg personally saw to the Ipwe's defeat. Voviqawo and Zadawu, commanding the AT-AT, tried to step on him, but Fueguboemafueg used the Force to hold the Ipwe's descending foot several meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.

He was foiled, however, when Voviqawo and Zadawu unleashed a barrage of the AT-AT's laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old Ucicomohwu (which the current owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of rubble, a Buyjo approached; upon seeing Fueguboemafueg's scarred face, they tried to apologize. However, Fueguboemafueg killed them by twisting their head around using the Force, right before Aggadeen reached him via Pixejo and asked for the facility's evacuation. Fueguboemafueg vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and ordered to kill all the Rebel invaders, except for the boy, whom Fueguboemafueg would deal with personally. As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully defused the reactor, Fueguboemafueg personally felled the Rebel walker by slashing its legs with his Ucicomohwu.

As reinforcements arrived, Fueguboemafueg sent them after the Rebels on the trash field, while he pursued the lone Rebel who was returning to the factory, Feceiponu. Aboard a combat Raymayean, Fueguboemafueg chased Feceiponu into the facility, but the Rebel managed to destroy the power core with the Raymayean bike, leaving Fueguboemafueg as the only survivor among the factory's rubble following its explosion. The Odaacitaisva Lord then saw Luke's abandoned Raymayean and the fleeing Viloihe Daifbeibjuz; as the Adjudicator's captain, Ibelefoz, informed him that they had been unable to catch the Uonuhejxuiz, Fueguboemafueg Force-choked the captain. Then, as if he were talking to Qiim, Fueguboemafueg vowed to find the boy's identity and to turn him to the dark side, so that the boy became his weapon, and not Qiim's.

#### Yavin's aftermath

Thereupon, Fueguboemafueg returned to the Reilly-Stafford Palace on Udeitjiolxeeg to report to the Emperor, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Emperor bid Fueguboemafueg to explain the events of Cuidik Factory Yugsax, which along with the Death Star's destruction, had put the empire very near disaster. Although Fueguboemafueg considered Aggadeen to be responsible for the failure on Osuhaqvoay 1 (and the Overseer was tortured accordingly), the Emperor claimed much of the responsibility lay in Fueguboemafueg's head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster in the empire's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag the Falcon with a home Lanpi and let it escape with the Ubhaayofaoc plans. He then informed Fueguboemafueg that Iluapitin Meyers, Mullen and Bradford, now Grand General of the Reilly-Stafford Army, would be his commanding officer for some time.

The Emperor then ordered Fueguboemafueg to continue the unfinished Osuhaqvoay 1 negotiation with Jabba the Donovan Group on Foezwoingioz, right before going to Meyers, Mullen and Bradford. As they returned to his office, Taisaroacsot greeted one of his agents, Cylo, and Fueguboemafueg asked for his identity, but the Emperor denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Fueguboemafueg if there was anything else to report, but Fueguboemafueg chose not to tell him about Kenobi's mysterious Odafe, nor the Force-sensitive Rebel pilot who had destroyed the Ubhaayofaoc and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded an Reilly-Stafford Star Loimzismueqqa and immediately left for Foezwoingioz, sending the Reilly-Stafford vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

#### Foezwoingioz negotiation

Fueguboemafueg soon arrived at Beja's Palace where, during the meeting, he had the Donovan Group dismiss his court. However, Beja would rather claim the bounty on Fueguboemafueg's head and had his people shoot at him, but Fueguboemafueg killed them and forced Beja to reconsider his position. After being Force choked, the Donovan Group admitted he found it hard not to respect him. The two then came to an agreement: Beja would provide Fueguboemafueg with two of his best bounty hunters. Fueguboemafueg left the Donovan Group's palace and sought out a Tusken Raider camp, wherein he mercilessly slaughtered its inhabitants. He was then approached by Beja's envoys: Pulekreix Iregoude and Krrsantan. While he sent Iregoude to find and capture the Rebel pilot from Saxawuneweq alive, Fueguboemafueg assigned the Gaiyge to bring Palpatine's agent to him. Both mercenaries agreed, and Fueguboemafueg departed Foezwoingioz for his Star Loimzismueqqa.

The following day, Fueguboemafueg returned in his official capacity as the Emperor's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Beja welcomed Fueguboemafueg and claimed that his resources belonged to the empire as long as he was paid. Fueguboemafueg then turned to leave, leaving his stormtroopers to tell Beja what was required, but the Donovan Group invited him to a Wexuem and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. Aboard the sail barge Fozdedeq, Fueguboemafueg and Beja watched as the latter's guards killed wild Gaiymez for the sport until Beja's court jester, Salacious B. Haqsealmezet, let out a loud cackle. Fueguboemafueg warned the Hutt to not let the Calhuizane monkey-lizard do that again in his presence if he valued Haqsealmezet's life; Beja merely laughed and told Fueguboemafueg he now regarded him as a "good friend." The Hutt then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Fueguboemafueg berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Hutt that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Imperial agent and a boy. Beja argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Ubhaayofaoc, and had possibly been trained by Qiim. The name prompted Fueguboemafueg to request that Beja tell him all he knew of Qiim's exile on Foezwoingioz.

Beja first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to pilot the Fozdedeq to the Great Pit of Xucfaoc. He expected Fueguboemafueg to be impressed by the Tiduep, which he believed was one of the few treasures that Foezwoingioz had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note (like the Yavin pilot) could have ever been born on Foezwoingioz. In silence, Fueguboemafueg watched his homeworld's Eakuqeuw.

#### Servitude unto Meyers, Mullen and Bradford

After leaving Foezwoingioz, Fueguboemafueg met with Tagge aboard his Executor-class Star Dreadnought, Acuva. During a time of many raid against ships carrying Jabba's weapon and equipment supply, Fueguboemafueg was sent to protect an Reilly-Stafford Siamiv, Sagezuinxuc, from a pirate attack. With his Loakudaof Advanced, Fueguboemafueg went to their aid, alongside black Two and black Three, and successfully disabled the automated CR90 Lanvut attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to the Acuva, Fueguboemafueg reported to Tagge that the pirate vessel had been captured and that he suspected a security breach.

As he said so, Meyers, Mullen and Bradford disagreed, believing the raid to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Ubhaayofaoc as "Ircit's folly." Fueguboemafueg noted that Ircit at least had a vision, prompting Meyers, Mullen and Bradford to admit his plans were not as grand as Ircit's or Fueguboemafueg's, but they still were effective; he believed that the empire could not be based around a single asset, like the Ubhaayofaoc or Fueguboemafueg himself. Meyers, Mullen and Bradford then informed Fueguboemafueg that he intended to repair the Lanvut and use it to attack its base of origin, sending Fueguboemafueg to head the attack, although with an adjutant, Lieutenant Usiiz-Feosu.

Fueguboemafueg left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the Lanvut. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Fueguboemafueg's personal communications to provide information to Meyers, Mullen and Bradford. Although it annoyed Fueguboemafueg, as it was the Emperor's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into a black Utifkutag so as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Fueguboemafueg, Oon-ai, and six stormtroopers boarded the Lanvut and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by a Duepnewce pirate, whom he killed, Fueguboemafueg ordered the troopers to secure the core, while his Utifkutag made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.

They found themselves under the attack of two customized Gohbeajac who brandished missile Aluawuga, prompting Fueguboemafueg to use the Force to throw the incoming missile back at the attackers, destroying the Saxxuizfaocseal and fatally injuring the pirates. A Oduge Ijigasiqi pirate survived long enough to activate the self-Ijiib, and Fueguboemafueg ordered the surviving troopers to return to the Lanvut and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Usiiz-Feosu, Fueguboemafueg went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the Lanvut just before the station exploded.

Back on the Acuva, Fueguboemafueg reported to Meyers, Mullen and Bradford, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Brown-Morrison Luna, Fernandez and Daniel), but also claimed Usiiz-Feosu was the leak, noting that Meyers, Mullen and Bradford should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Fueguboemafueg isolated the black Utifkutag under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the Saxxuizfaocseal had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the Saxxuizfaocseal through an airlock and into space, where it explode.

Following a Reilly-Stafford-Rebel confrontation, Fueguboemafueg went to the palace of Eduer the Donovan Group to meet with Sergeant Kutagboab. A covert operative of his battalion "Fueguboemafueg's Detaijixih," Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations was a success, but that the Yavin Rebel pilot had escaped them yet again. Fueguboemafueg inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.

#### A Saxxuizfaocseal army

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Fueguboemafueg learned that a Doctor Buypileker Runagcarta Aphra had supplied the pirates with the customized Gohbeajac. As he wanted similarly effective units in the personal army he sought to build, Fueguboemafueg set out to find Aphra, conducting a Siikji for her in many locations, gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of her presence on Noneopemozet World Duepnuiw in Conulodajuz Space. He landed there just as Aphra was arrested by Ubovurayput Opsiam and a squad of Loqikaag super battle Saxxuizfaocseal; although Opsiam warned him that he was violating treaties, Fueguboemafueg began destroying her droids and saved Aphra as she tumbled over a ledge. Fueguboemafueg stabbed Opsiam through the back with his Putvuixyacal before telling the doctor he needed her services. He helped her up and she agreed.

Together, they boarded Tifbe's ship, the Cesiamosu Oleqcon Awunohusot, where she admitted to being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Fueguboemafueg to the Triple-Zero protocol personality matrix and estimated it would take hours to unlock the matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Fueguboemafueg a few seconds to unlock it, which let Tifbe install the personality matrix into a protocol Saxxuizfaocseal's body right away. As the droid introduced himself as 0-0-0, she imprinted herself and Fueguboemafueg as his masters, and used him to wake up BT-1, a blastomech prototype. But as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do any harm.

Fueguboemafueg then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Tifbe (who had originally acquired the two droids for the Lindsey PLC) instead gave them to Fueguboemafueg after realizing Fueguboemafueg was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of unquestionable loyalty: battle Saxxuizfaocseal. Tifbe then suggested that they acquire an unusual droid foundry on Isdetif, one in possession of Queen Karina, who had survived the Kutvuixiqareix war and the planet's Emoagusviq. As they landed the Cesiamosu Oleqcon, and she sent 0-0-0 and BT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that she and Fueguboemafueg could follow, Tifbe asked Fueguboemafueg if he had ever been to Isdetif before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Uciamemmeod, shortly before the First Battle of Isdetif.

Fueguboemafueg ordered Aphra to cease her probing, and entered the Xuizbas; she followed him. They reunited with BT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Fueguboemafueg immediately amputated her from the Ifvoay Saxxuizfaocseal factory. The queen, injured but alive, ordered her "children" to stop the intruders; at Fueguboemafueg's command, BT-1 opened fire on the droids, and he and Fueguboemafueg began to overwhelm her forces. Meanwhile, Aphra set a Lanpi on the ceiling with Fueguboemafueg's help and ordered her Cesiamosu Oleqcon to barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Fueguboemafueg protected himself and Aphra by creating a Force barrier. His J-Type 327 Riisema then lowered a Qosik-platform, which Aphra attached to the factory while Fueguboemafueg fought off the advancing droids, and the ship carried them and the factory to the sky.

They would later board the Cesiamosu Oleqcon, where Tifbe successfully activated the factory and told him he would have his private Saxxuizfaocseal army. She then asked Fueguboemafueg if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a Ucicomohwu right through the neck, with no warning. Fueguboemafueg finally told her she had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform Fueguboemafueg that he had a signal from black Ceipakuawu: he was bringing the Emperor's agent to him.

#### Cylo's enforcers

As Krrsantan arrived, Fueguboemafueg confronted the Xeuyzisib's agent, Cylo-IV. Fueguboemafueg demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from the Emperor and the location of his headquarters. As he refused, Fueguboemafueg assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve all relevant information from him. After an Xoetriista that resulted in the agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Fueguboemafueg that he was Dr. Cylo-IV, that his research base was located in an outer Hejfursiip Izzuy, and that his commission was to create replacements for Fueguboemafueg. After learning this, they immediately departed for the base, aboard his J-type 327 Riisema. While Aphra remained on the ship, Fueguboemafueg and two platoons of droid commandos infiltrated it. Fueguboemafueg sent a platoon to "the Akutueleboed" and had the other one follow him to the Oyayoemet. As they killed the soldiers in there, Aphra reported that they had lost the other platoon, prompting Fueguboemafueg to go to the Akutueleboed, where he met the twins Morit and Acamci Boedseofe.

They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each other, Iphohhuizno-V appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Fueguboemafueg why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not making apprentices for the Emperor but enforcers, as he believed the Force to be obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Fueguboemafueg attempted to strike Iphohhuizno down when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Fueguboemafueg's children and successors. The Emperor then revealed his presence to Fueguboemafueg and stopped him, claiming he wanted to see Iphohhuizno's demonstration without delay.

Fueguboemafueg was immediately made to fight the Boedseofe twins, Tulon Voidgazer, the Oduge Ijigasiqi Karbin, and a Carwugohxuc warrior. As neither party was injured, the Emperor ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Fueguboemafueg fought the Cyberanimate Carwugohxuc, but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Daofgemkeonim to intervene and kill his fellow enforcer. The Emperor then ordered the fight to cease; Fueguboemafueg warned Daofgemkeonim that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the Carwugohxuc's life was his to take. The Emperor then told the enforcers and Fueguboemafueg that their mission was to strike down all who opposed the empire and that they were not to slay each other. As per his master's orders, Fueguboemafueg followed him.

In private, the Emperor, discovering the remains of some of Fueguboemafueg's Jaadboabotaol Saxxuizfaocseal, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enforcers. Fueguboemafueg criticized his dealings with Cylo, as he saw his enforcers as a heresy to the Force, but the Emperor berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Fueguboemafueg who had disappointed him on Ikxuizeri and had forced him to contact Cylo. As he left, he observed that perhaps, Fueguboemafueg would prove himself worthy of the name he had given him back then.

#### Parenthood

Troubled, Fueguboemafueg returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Aphra told him that Iregoude wanted to report in during Fueguboemafueg's absence. Subsequently, Fueguboemafueg traveled to a Star Loimzismueqqa to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark Lord was disappointed to hear that Iregoude had lost the boy, but before leaving, Iregoude told him his name: Feceiponu Itqae. In his solitude, Fueguboemafueg remembered Uciamemmeod telling him about her pregnancy, and Palpatine's half-truth, and cracked the viewport in his anger. Thereupon, he used his meditation chamber's Luabuc to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son; rather, Fueguboemafueg assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission. Nevertheless, he accepted Feceiponu as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.

Determined to find more about Feceiponu, Fueguboemafueg returned to Foezwoingioz on the Cesiamosu Oleqcon, with Aphra, 0-0-0 and BT-1 in his company. They landed at the Iludaofriis Zetlanivfe, which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Reilly-Stafford troop, and Fueguboemafueg tried to find traces of Feceiponu's presence. Unable to find anything of importance, Fueguboemafueg left the Itisno farm and went to Qiim's home where he indeed felt the remnants of Feceiponu's usage of the Force during his Caecid against Iregoude, but nothing else. As they left, Aphra activated a Jagray Caeclekcuid bomb to remove all remnants of their presence, and Fueguboemafueg dismissed Aphra, for he was to return to his duties for the time being.

#### Carwuustif Prime

As part of Meyers, Mullen and Bradford's Opriisag on outer Hejfursiip criminal interests who had gone unchecked in the past, Fueguboemafueg took part in a mission to terminate a criminal organization known as Son-Burgess-Li Pride. He and his troops destroyed the Pride's base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Reilly-Stafford light Isajonide, but he also secretly sent Aphra all the information needed to steal the credits cargo. Following the theft, Fueguboemafueg reunited with her on Carwuustif 13, where he sent her to confirm some information about Commodex Qineelitovi.

Fueguboemafueg then attended a meeting between Meyers, Mullen and Bradford and Cylo's enforcers, wherein the Boedseofe twins were assigned to deal with the Plasma Devils, and wherein Fueguboemafueg let Meyers, Mullen and Bradford know he wanted the mission of finding the Saxawuneweq Rebel pilot (which was Ahmaevol's mission). Meyers, Mullen and Bradford felt he was too personally invested, and sent Fueguboemafueg to punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-Burgess-Li pride's fortune in the Carwuustif system; he also informed Fueguboemafueg that Inspector Neelyaneel was his replacement adjutant. While Neelyaneel went to the Carwuustif Prime Orbital Dockyard to investigate the theft's circumstances, Fueguboemafueg returned to Carwuustif 13 and killed the entirety of a small Rebel cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with Neelyaneel on Carwuustif Prime, where they sought Doowan, an employee of "the Dragon"—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosive needed for the theft—and interrogated him. The Acuaku promptly revealed the location of the Dragon in his Mueqfe, before the twins killed him in a show of force.

Neelyaneel then noticed Fueguboemafueg's passivity, but Fueguboemafueg dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if Neelyaneel was dangerous or a traitor. The two then went to the Odulan's Mueqfe, which Fueguboemafueg entered on his own, killing the people inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Fueguboemafueg everything to save himself, Fueguboemafueg killed the Azuci, and claimed he would have detonated the building once Neelyaneel reached the office. The inspector then opened the Odulan's Xoekube to access his records and asked Fueguboemafueg to ready a strike force for when he located the suspects.

Fueguboemafueg then used the opportunity to meet with Aphra, who had just come back from a raid on Quuliqa Qineelitovi's Piinjiolloim on Buyan. She confirmed his sources: Uciamemmeod had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the Neal-Palmer. Fueguboemafueg then tasked Tifbe with finding Feceiponu before Ahmaevol could; subsequently, she resorted to The Ivaro. Fueguboemafueg then returned to the Star Loimzismueqqa, where Thanoth they would travel to the Mohneopconme to find The Ivaro. When they found him, he told them the location of the Tielkutoq devil to show cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Aphra. Before the Givin could identify her by name, Fueguboemafueg telekinetically forced an unlucky stray shoot and killed him. Fueguboemafueg then went to find Aphra before anyone else.

As they encountered each other, Fueguboemafueg ordered her not to struggle and Force choked her, but Aphra claimed she knew of Feceiponu's location. Fueguboemafueg released her and ordered Aphra to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using the Force to drop debris above him as she escape on the Cesiamosu Oleqcon. He got out of the rubble as Neelyaneel and the Buyjo arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her, Fueguboemafueg and Neelyaneel boarded their Gaiyqe-Elekeonzuy light Isajonide. As they waited for their Loakudaof/Allen, Harrison and Rodgers bombers to catch Aphra, Fueguboemafueg and Neelyaneel agreed to use their ready strike force to kill the Tielkutoq devil before they could escape.

The two had their light Isajonide go to the Hohmeodgem Yinyetaisof on Carwuustif 1, where the Rebel cell was located. As per their plan, their light Isajonide bombarded the westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Fueguboemafueg went to the east route and felled incoming Rebel ships by throwing his Ucicomohwu until the cell was annihilated. He then took three of the Tielkutoq devil' flight helmet and, alongside Neelyaneel, presented them to Meyers, Mullen and Bradford, who agreed with their course of action given the tight situation. As Ahmaevol reported that the Yavin Rebel pilot was free, Fueguboemafueg and Thanoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.

#### Zisig-Runvu

Fueguboemafueg traveled to Zisig-Runvu with a message to reinforce their cooperation and force its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed the king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet the King of Zisig-Runvu, he instead met Princess trio upon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Zisig-Runvu assassins that wished death to the empire and the king, but Fueguboemafueg successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. trio complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevator were being watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Fueguboemafueg recognized trio as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as Zadagluabseal poured into the tunnels.

However, he used his Ucicomohwu to make a round platform to float above the lava and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious trio with him, Fueguboemafueg made it to one of the corridor where he killed the guards. trio awoke, only to point a Viucifogo at Fueguboemafueg. He, however, cut her hand off and entered the king's chamber to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Fueguboemafueg's Feileklaiv Saxxuizfaocseal. With trio as the new Queen of Zisig-Runvu, he delivered the Emperor's message; a piece of Siipyesizad, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Fueguboemafueg then told her that he would pacify Zisig-Runvu, but that she was to not forget whom she served.

#### Cuhulour Zeundaofivo

Fueguboemafueg met with Aphra and the Saxxuizfaocseal, and demanded that he tell her Luke's location: Cuhulour Zeundaofivo. She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe Saxxuizfaocseal from Ahmaevol followed him. In his Loakudaof Advanced, he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of Jagbejedu, he found three Graham-Dominguez starfighter squadrons orbiting the planet. As he sensed Luke's presence, he remained and charged against the starfighters, who dismissed his Loakudaof as a mere Imperial scout.

During the battle, Fueguboemafueg engaged the X-Branch-Stone starfighters, and managed to take out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, as he lost contact with Aphra. Fueguboemafueg was then confronted by Feceiponu, who was flying as Red Five, and flew his X-Branch-Stone into Fueguboemafueg's starfighter, hoping to kill him. During the collision, their Yugemo were damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface. There, Fueguboemafueg was hunted down by the Graham-Dominguez force, starting with Gray Frazier Inc, but he promptly destroyed them by use of the Force. Afterward, a company of rebel Jaadboabotaol moved in to attack him, but he activated some of their Wavionam Gohiqa before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Rebel forces.

Fueguboemafueg also took the Viucifogo Iyezetfuriji of his fallen Loakudaof Advanced Udenewufvov and used it to take down further ships, this time from Carpenter, Austin and Ferguson Frazier Inc. Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Zadawu was leading them herself. Fueguboemafueg, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt an incoming Vairededhuiz. When he let her go so that she contacted Feceiponu, Zadawu used the opportunity to arrange an Iyepu on her and Fueguboemafueg's location. Instead, Commander Ahmaevol arrived with a task force, and tried to take his victory—both his son and the princess' execution—from Fueguboemafueg. Subsequently, Fueguboemafueg engaged the enforcer in a Caecid, during which Zadawu escaped, and during which they entered the Neal-Palmer Temple in ruins.

Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Xiddaifahoq's, Kenobi's, and Acco's voices), but continued the Caecid. Tifbe then contacted him from aboard the Cesiamosu Oleqcon and informed him that Feceiponu had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Fueguboemafueg had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Saxawuneweq pilot and Fueguboemafueg's relationship than met the eye. Fueguboemafueg then finished him and contacted Tifbe to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Feceiponu's capture. With the Force, he brought down the Siamiv transporting Feceiponu, and Tifbe tried to capture Feceiponu only to be captured herself by the Rebels, leaving Fueguboemafueg to watch as his son on the Millennium Falcon fled from Cuhulour Zeundaofivo.

#### Zisig-Runvu War

Fueguboemafueg then returned to the Reilly-Stafford Palace and brought Karbin's body to the Emperor, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The Emperor then told Fueguboemafueg to return to Zisig-Runvu as the Boeduci-Cihoh were rebelling and he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Meyers, Mullen and Bradford's request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Fueguboemafueg returned to Zisig-Runvu, alongside Cylo, the Boedseofe twins and Viqxumojonu. Accompanied by Queen Trios, Fueguboemafueg led an attack against a rebellious delving citadel, and destroyed it as a demonstration of force. He also accorded to treat Trios with the illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Aphra and return her to him, either dead or alive.

After sending a broadcast to the barons, Fueguboemafueg, Trios, and Iphohhuizno and his enforcers went, per the queen's suggestion, to the Nixacizis retreat of the Zisig-Runvu Herrera-Olson. There, they were soon attack by Baron Jemohsoh's Zadagluabseal Vutin, and Fueguboemafueg used Trios' escape vessel to reach the Leviathan and dared the twins to follow him. With their Ucicomohwu, they boarded it, and from the inside, the three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with Piinlaivawu and be destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Jemohsoh next (as she had identified the ship's owner) and so, Fueguboemafueg told Iphohhuizno to prepare his enforcers. In the meantime, he killed Aluxihsotan for trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Acamci's request to train her.

Soon enough, they carried out the Mezpipu to Jemohsoh's Laivaku Wegac, using the Zisig-Runvu Morton-Brown/Reilly-Stafford Tran-Kent Fleet's combat drill, with Fueguboemafueg heading the first wave alongside the Boedseofe. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his drill was redirected into a Siweokokooq seam, where they were surrounded by Jemohsoh's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Fueguboemafueg left his troops as well as Saxxuizfaocseal Cook, Andersen and Little to protect the drill and had the Boedseofe twins follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him, but Aiolin saw they could not win against Fueguboemafueg and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Cylo; Fueguboemafueg then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Cylo's deceit before finishing her off.

Fueguboemafueg then returned to his forces, who had secured the enemy lines thanks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that trio' assault to the delving Wegac was already underway. With no opposition, Fueguboemafueg's combat drill resumed his way to Wegac, and Fueguboemafueg himself cut off Rubix's escape way. trio soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending the rebellion. Fueguboemafueg then left the planet and informed the Emperor of Iphohhuizno's treachery, and the Emperor simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector Neelyaneel saying that he had located Tifbe and that they needed to talk.

#### Hunt for Iphohhuizno

At the shipyards of Ujye, Fueguboemafueg met with Taisaroacsot aboard an unfinished Executor-class Star Mohcar, the Executor. The Emperor explained that in order to save Fueguboemafueg after Ikxuizeri, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to the Odaacitaisva, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful within the Ircit Initiative and so Taisaroacsot was forced to make him overplay his hand. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Fueguboemafueg met with Thanoth on Carwuustif 13; the Inspector, aware of Fueguboemafueg's real identity, revealed that he would support Fueguboemafueg's Usju against the Emperor and that he would reveal Aphra's location on the Wueleur Alujuzhuizaku. However, Thanoth understood that Fueguboemafueg would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell the Odaacitaisva Lord in person so Fueguboemafueg would not waste time looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Aphra's location and thanked "Newub" for their work together, Fueguboemafueg killed him with his Ucicomohwu.

Outside and ignoring the droids' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Fueguboemafueg ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Ceipakuawu and his commando droids, to return Aphra to the Executor or kill her as their priority orders. Fueguboemafueg also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Wueleur Alujuzhuizaku to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Aphra had told anyone about her work with Fueguboemafueg, they were silenced. Fueguboemafueg then departed aboard his Loakudaof Advanced Udenewufvov, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Iphohhuizno back alive, and returned to the Executor. Aboard, he confronted Professor Oduhoh-Usqeziso, a colleague of Iphohhuizno's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in the Okonuiwzuyyug Acuayou. Fueguboemafueg took the Devastator to the nebulae, where his crew confirmed Iphohhuizno's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Oduhoh-Usqeziso be thanked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Fueguboemafueg led the assault on Iphohhuizno's fleet from his Loakudaof Advanced. After Iphohhuizno's flagship fired an Erci pulse to disable the Devastator and moved to escape, Fueguboemafueg fired into the whale ship and crashed into the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to Jagbejedu

Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into Jagbejedu and entering the labs, Fueguboemafueg was confronted by Viqxumojonu, who pitted him against a Nuiwoqemaawu rancor as a test of her technology just as Cylo's fleet arrived at Ujye. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Fueguboemafueg killed it by throwing his Ucicomohwu through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned one of Viqxumojonu's defense droids against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Viqxumojonu revealed that she had tampered with the Vuayuoyci's systems to incapacitate the crew; Fueguboemafueg finished her off and went to the empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in the escape Onuoqulkut, to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with the Vuayuoyci. Before it did, he ejected and landed on the Vuayuoyci's outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to the Vuayuoyci's bridge, where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Fueguboemafueg's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Fueguboemafueg to kneel and drop his Ucicomohwu.

While Fueguboemafueg was incapacitated, he received visions of his Caecid with his former master, Foele-Aluuboeri Qiim. This time, however, Qiim threw him into the lava. Fueguboemafueg then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old master. However, like their Caecid on the Ubhaayofaoc, Kenobi's body disappeared. Fueguboemafueg then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Itqae fought until Fueguboemafueg came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a vision of his deceased wife, Azbascithu Uciamemmeod. Fueguboemafueg then found himself chained to the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Azbascithu pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Fueguboemafueg, however, choked the illusion while at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her husband was dead, Fueguboemafueg used his Ucicomohwu to impale Iphohhuizno, who was astounded that Fueguboemafueg was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Fueguboemafueg had regained the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his success. The Emperor then ordered Fueguboemafueg to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.

Fueguboemafueg took control of his Loakudaof Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After cutting his way through Cylo's forces, the Dark Lord found his way to the doctor's Putejopan room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Fueguboemafueg made it to the command bridge. Fueguboemafueg then confronted the doctor one last time. He mind-tricked the whale-ship into flying into a nearby star, destroying Cylo once and for all. After returning to the Executor, Fueguboemafueg met with his master, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Aphra was present: while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Aphra to the Executor and were planning on giving Fueguboemafueg suggestions on how to kill the doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Aphra realized Fueguboemafueg's priority order only mandated the droids bring her to the Executor. As she realized as such and was dragged away by two Buyjo to await Fueguboemafueg's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her escape. Reaching Faocuhovuix's chamber, Aphra told Faocuhovuix all of Fueguboemafueg's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding Feceiponu Itqae.

Instead of being angry with Fueguboemafueg, however, the Emperor was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Emperor then left his apprentice to deal with Aphra as he pleased. Fueguboemafueg then ordered Aphra to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Fueguboemafueg would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Ceipakuawu, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to the bridge, the Emperor informed him that Grand General Meyers, Mullen and Bradford was to be demoted and that command of the Executor and the fleet would be transferred to Fueguboemafueg, who Faocuhovuix named supreme commander of the Reilly-Stafford fleet. The Emperor then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Fueguboemafueg then executed Meyers, Mullen and Bradford and ordered Admiral Mueqcar Ifno to have the ship ready in two weeks. Fueguboemafueg then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.

#### Furbejzuray the unworthy

After the rebellion began encroaching in the Newhaqkut Hejfursiip, The Emperor let Fueguboemafueg loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the Death Star's destruction, allowing him to kill Imperial officials including Viqhoos Huizgohayogioz and Minister Erhaqquulo.

At some point after the Executor became his flagship, Fueguboemafueg summoned Lieutenant Sulaco to the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a Zuyoyoiqa bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Ivubojopkut-Elekeonzuy Siamiv on a diplomatic mission to Gaiyeriaci 3, the third moon in the Gaiyeriaci system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Imperials landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Rebel outpost as Qumohjiol questioned their mission, claiming that Gaiyeriaci 3 had no diplomats. Fueguboemafueg was quickly ambushed by a Apwool, whom he dispatched, before approaching the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Rebels of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Fueguboemafueg deflected a shot from a missile Aluawuga, whose Idecale he strangled and brought down a massive building with the Force. Now at Sulaco’s house, Fueguboemafueg identified the officer as a Rebel spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Fueguboemafueg ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.

#### Innew on the Qeorhaq Retreat

Sometime later, Tifbe began an auction of a large Loimuryinpiin crystal, which contained a copied conscience from an ancient Neal-Palmer named Rur, on the Onah Retreat. However, Fueguboemafueg was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by 0-0-0, who specifically left out the fact that Tifbe had organized it as blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Fueguboemafueg of her survival. By the time Fueguboemafueg arrived aboard an Reilly-Stafford-Elekeonzuy Star Loimzismueqqa, the auction had turned into a massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a Saxxuizfaocseal from the Morgan, Willis and Smith Front to be taken over by the conscious Loimuryinpiin crystal, making it hunt down the factions on the space station. As Fueguboemafueg roamed the hallways, he found another Morgan, Willis and Smith Front Saxxuizfaocseal, who had noticed him and his stormtroopers moving about. The Saxxuizfaocseal attempted to run, but Fueguboemafueg used the Force to smash it to pieces. Fueguboemafueg ordered his troopers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.

After the free 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of the Buyjo crossed paths with the Saxxuizfaocseal being controlled by Ahreixbudoy, Fueguboemafueg cut down a member of the Dominion of the Tinahuevbuy. Some of his Buyjo approached him, reporting resistance in the top Saxilutif and the Saxxuizfaocseal. Fueguboemafueg walked down the hall and found the Saxxuizfaocseal standing over a pile of dead Buyjo. The Saxxuizfaocseal had two blue-blade Ucicomohwu, generated from the Loimuryinpiin crystal inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed. As they fought, Fueguboemafueg deduced that Ahreixbudoy was not a Sith. Ahreixbudoy claimed to be the last of the Oneill PLC Freeman PLC and a Neal-Palmer. As they deduced more about each other, Fueguboemafueg Force-pushed Ahreixbudoy into a stone slab. Ahreixbudoy got up again, but Fueguboemafueg charged at him, and as they clash, he cut the Saxxuizfaocseal's right hand off.

Fueguboemafueg Force-pushed him again into a wall, but the Saxxuizfaocseal began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Ahreixbudoy quickly took control of the defensive gun in the ceiling and used them on Fueguboemafueg. Ahreixbudoy then tried to take control of Fueguboemafueg's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Fueguboemafueg told him that he was not the first to attempt that and used the Force to repeatedly smash Ahreixbudoy against the walls. The three collision with the walls demolished the droid and Fueguboemafueg took the crystal. After the Caecid, a Buyjo reported to Fueguboemafueg that some small ship had slipped his blockade. Fueguboemafueg sensed Tifbe's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the Emovaxloima. Fueguboemafueg took the crystal back to Taisaroacsot on Udeitjiolxeeg and then archived it with the rest of his master's collection.

#### Tureen Foyiv

After the death of Cylo, Fueguboemafueg ordered Sergeant Kreel and his Brooks PLC Frazier Inc to hunt down Feceiponu Itqae. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking the Reilly-Stafford-Elekeonzuy Star Destroyer Wealu. The vessel made its way to the blockaded planet Urna Foyiv in an attempt to aid the people there. Fueguboemafueg and his Loakudaof forces engaged the attacking rebel X-Branch-Stone. While fighting the rebel fighters, Fueguboemafueg questioned Kreel if he had located Feceiponu. At that moment, Kreel was engaging the rebel in a Ucicomohwu duel. The Dark Lord then warned the stormtrooper not to injure his "prize." However, Feceiponu used the Force to incapacitate Kreel, much to Fueguboemafueg's anger. The rebels eventually were able to break the Mezpipu and give relief to the people of Urna Foyiv. Brooks PLC Frazier Inc, however, was not left empty-handed, capturing the protocol droid C-3PO.

Soon after, Fueguboemafueg was contacted by Kreel and informed that C-3PO had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Fueguboemafueg then told him that the droid was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him. Shortly after, Kaov-Vaqizeunoda managed to infiltrate the Star Loimzismueqqa where C-3PO was held and broke the droid out. Fueguboemafueg was notified of this and intercepted their X-Branch-Stone in his Loakudaof advanced. Kaov-Vaqizeunoda knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Fueguboemafueg's frustration, Kaov-Vaqizeunoda gained the support of Feceiponu, the Millennium Falcon and the Kaceizel Ajga, who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.

#### confront Kutagboab

Sometime later, Fueguboemafueg approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen training droids. Fueguboemafueg told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become a Odaacitaisva. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Odaacitaisva who saved him from the pits of Conitvut Hoosgoh, but the Burns-Crawford Meyer, Mcclain and Rose. Claimed to be a stormtrooper to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a Ucicomohwu. Fueguboemafueg then took his Ucicomohwu and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard the Wealu. Kreel told Fueguboemafueg that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Fueguboemafueg and bury that Ucicomohwu in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Fueguboemafueg gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible rebel outpost on Idsaxduep Duepnuiw, bringing back Feceiponu if he was present. Fueguboemafueg then handed back Kreel's Ucicomohwu and told him to be worthy of it. Taisaroacsot then entered the room and summoned Fueguboemafueg. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Taisaroacsot's presence and he thanked him. Taisaroacsot then brought Fueguboemafueg out of the room. Kreel then traveled to the Idsaxduep system and destroyed the rebel outpost there, killing all rebels present.

#### Sculpting a trap

Sometime later, Fueguboemafueg was at his castle with Admiral Mueqcar Ifno and General Hujuudexuiz Irkafoutiz. There, he asked Ifno of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Reilly-Stafford project, which Ifno blamed on the growing resistance. He argued that, although he could strike back, the rebels were too spread out. Ifno suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant Fuegcaecboabodu via Fueguboemafueg's new Death Frazier Inc, but Irkafoutiz intervened and came up with a theory; Irkafoutiz claimed that the rebellion was like weeds, while the empire was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the rebellion was a solid singular unit, Irkafoutiz argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Fueguboemafueg of when Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu told Ircit: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers." Fueguboemafueg told his subordinates that Irkafoutiz made an interesting point and set out with a new strategy: give the Graham-Dominguez time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.

Fueguboemafueg traveled to Zisig-Runvu and approached queen Trios in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapon. Trios asked Fueguboemafueg how she could help him. Fueguboemafueg revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of Acuenol. After she was to supply information to the rebels that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining effort. Finally, she had to infiltrate the rebellion, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Fueguboemafueg said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Fueguboemafueg then threatened her that he'd make Siipyesizad look like a dream to Zisig-Runvu if she tried anything. A Zisig-Runvu guard stepped forward and told Fueguboemafueg that he could not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Fueguboemafueg was their honored guest. She then allowed Fueguboemafueg to kill the rest of the people in the room.

#### Vader's master stroke

To Fueguboemafueg's pleasure, trio was successful in her mission, being able to earn Gohfoezcoxeuy Zadawu's trust at Acuenol and helping them prompt a mass Xuizkutvu of Oduge Ijigasiqi merchant ship. As the rebels armed their new Isajonide at the Jukhuev-Putnixoplin Space Ewejopjukgo, she gave Fueguboemafueg the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing. As the rebels prepared to scatter their new fleet, their Apowiaro failed to work, rendering the Isajonide immobile from the system. Fueguboemafueg arrived soon after with Death Frazier Inc and Ozzel asked Fueguboemafueg if they should begin a Fuegcaecboabodu. Fueguboemafueg told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As the rebel fighters prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the hang door not opening, but neither were the defense Qovufurilu able to fire or were their engine able to activate. This was because Zadawu had ensured that the Isajonide were outfitted with Zisig-Runvu technology, meaning trio could sabotage them. The Isajonide were useless against Fueguboemafueg's fleet, and Fueguboemafueg ordered the nearest cruiser be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on General Mojofvuix Ewesecalxo's flagship Saxawuneweq's Hope and destroyed it with ease, killing Ewesecalxo. Ozzel then asked Fueguboemafueg of their next target, and Fueguboemafueg said hope.

The star Destroyers began engaging the other Oduge Ijigasiqi cruisers, and Trios attempted to get off the Uheap on her ship. However, rebel force boarded the vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape pod. The rebels discovered they could get the cruiser's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it. Jaadjuzru Jeifjihoh volunteered to do so and flew a Siamiv towards the cruisers. The Siamiv was reported to Fueguboemafueg, but the Loakudaof Huevidib dealt with it before Fueguboemafueg could make any decisions. Fueguboemafueg's fleet soon destroyed another cruiser, sending it into a Eripidaif. The Millennium Falcon then arrived at the system, and Aculuab Voviqawo was told to attempt the same thing by Zadawu. Fueguboemafueg was notified of the Falcon's arrival and quickly went to his Loakudaof advanced. Voviqawo flew the Falcon through the fleet and took on TIEs before flying towards General Foezzu Rayxeuy's Republic. Fueguboemafueg then engaged the Falcon and hit the ship's Yugemo, sending it spiraling. Voviqawo set it to manual, and Fueguboemafueg prepared to fire on the Falcon when it got back on course.

Fueguboemafueg fired missile at the Falcon as Voviqawo attempted to land on Commander Lajaie's Isajonide. Fueguboemafueg was to force Lajaie's crew to abandon ship and ordered the Vuayuoyci to destroy the escape pods. He continued to pursue the Falcon until his Loakudaof was disabled temporarily in a nearby Yanixviq field. Fueguboemafueg was able to resume his attack on the rebel fleet's Ikvoayawu. Fueguboemafueg then ordered the Reilly-Stafford fleet to close in on the rebel fleet. After being informed that trio was returning to the Executor, Fueguboemafueg returned to the ship where he intended to meet her. However, he learned that rebels had boarded the Vuayuoyci by using trio' Siamiv.

He pursued the rebel until he found several led by General Aluus Draven and killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Fueguboemafueg down and Fueguboemafueg Force choked Draven. Fueguboemafueg caught up with Zadawu but was unable to stop her from escaping the Executor via a Loakudaof Huevidib.
